# Krama-sandarbha 4 Canto

## Chapter One

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*manos tu śatarūpāyāṁ*  
*tisraḥ kanyāś ca jajñire*  
*ākūtir devahūtiś ca*  
*prasūtir iti viśrutāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Through Śatarūpā, Manu bore three daughters known as Ākūti, Devahūti and Prasūti.**

**Комментарий:**

*śrīkṛṣṇāya namaḥ*  
*caturthe kramasandarbhe/ sandarbhāṇāṁ samāhṛtiḥ*  
*kriyate yannideśena/ sa me ’nanyagatergatiḥ*

**He by whose order this collection of sandarbhas is made into the Fourth Krama Sandarbha is the goal of this person with no other goal.**

### Text 2

*ākūtiṁ rucaye prādād*  
*api bhrātṛmatīṁ nṛpaḥ*  
*putrikā-dharmam āśritya*  
*śatarūpānumoditaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With the consent of Śatarūpa, Manu gave Ākūti to Ruci, taking shelter of** ***putrikā-dharma,*** **even though Ākūti had brothers*****.***

**Комментарий:**

The commentary on the Fourth Canto begins. Having known of the birth of the Lord, Manu acted in this way. Though she had brothers, he gave her under the condition that the son born would be his son. It is also necessary to change she syllables of the mantras for giving the bride, in order to follow the rules properly.

### Text 8-9

*tuṣitā nāma te devā*  
*āsan svāyambhuvāntare*  
*marīci-miśrā ṛṣayo*  
*yajñaḥ sura-gaṇeśvaraḥ*  
*priyavratottānapādau*  
*manu-putrau mahaujasau*  
*tat-putra-pautra-naptṝṇām*  
*anuvṛttaṁ tad-antaram*

**Перевод:**

**During Svāyambhuva’s period, the Tuṣitas were the** ***devatās*****, Marīci and others were the sages, and Yajña was the head of the** ***devatās.*** **Svāyambhuva Manu's two sons, Priyavrata and Uttānapāda, became very powerful, and their sons and grandsons were active during that period.**

**Комментарий:**

*Naptṛṇām* means the great grandson.

### Text 15

*atreḥ patny anasūyā trīñ*  
*jajñe suyaśasaḥ sutān*  
*dattaṁ durvāsasaṁ somam*  
*ātmeśa-brahma-sambhavān*

**Перевод:**

**Anasūyā, another daughter of Kardama and Devahūti, became the wife of Atri Muni, and gave birth to three very famous sons— Dattātreya, Durvāsā and Soma,—who were partial representations of Lord Viṣṇu, Lord Śiva and Lord Brahmā.**

**Комментарий:**

The word atmā indicates that Viṣṇu is independent and Brahmā and Śiva are dependent on him.

*sṛjāmi tan-niyukto ’haṁ haro harati tad-vaśaḥ \|*  
*viśvaṁ puruṣa-rūpeṇa paripāti tri-śakti-dhṛk \|\|*

**I create under his order, and Śiva destroys under his order. Holding his three energies, he protects the universe as the Paramātmā. (SB** [**2.6.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/6.32)**)**

Elsewhere also the word *ātmā* means Viṣṇu. Atri made his wish without too much knowledge in verse 20.

### Text 18-19

*tasmin prasūna-stabaka-*  
*palāśāśoka-kānane*  
*vārbhiḥ sravadbhir udghuṣṭe*  
*nirvindhyāyāḥ samantataḥ*  
*prāṇāyāmena saṁyamya*  
*mano varṣa-śataṁ muniḥ*  
*atiṣṭhad eka-pādena*  
*nirdvandvo 'nila-bhojanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In a grove of flowering** ***palāśa*** **and** ***aśoka*** **trees filled with the sound of the Nirvindhyā River, Atri, controlling his mind and breath, remained standing on one foot for a hundred years, consuming only air.**

### Text 20

*śaraṇaṁ taṁ prapadye 'haṁ*  
*ya eva jagad-īśvaraḥ*  
*prajām ātma-samāṁ mahyaṁ*  
*prayacchatv iti cintayan*

**Перевод:**

**He thought, “I surrender to the Lord of the universe. May he give me offspring equal to him!”**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together. First sitting, he performed prāṇāyāma, then stood on one leg to do special worship, while thinking of a boon. When the Lord did not appear, in lamentation he sat again and used prāṇāyāma to produce fire to burn up his body. When he started to burn up, they suddenly appeared before him, giving him faith. They appeared externally. After they appeared in his mind, he stood up.

### Text 21-22

*tapyamānaṁ tri-bhuvanaṁ*  
*prāṇāyāmaidhasāgninā*  
*nirgatena muner mūrdhnaḥ*  
*samīkṣya prabhavas trayaḥ*  
*apsaro-muni-gandharva-*  
*siddha-vidyādharoragaiḥ*  
*vitāyamāna-yaśasas*  
*tad-āśrama-padaṁ yayuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva, spreading great fame, seeing that the three worlds, like fuel, were burning because of the fire emanating from the sage’s head by the fire of** ***prāṇāyāma,*** **went to his hermitage along with Gandharvas, Siddhas Vidyādharas and the Nāgas.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verse go together. Citsukha has *prāṇāyāmena.*

### Text 24-25

*praṇamya daṇḍavad bhūmāv*  
*upatasthe 'rhaṇāñjaliḥ*  
*vṛṣa-haṁsa-suparṇa-sthān*  
*svaiḥ svaiś cihnaiś ca cihnitān*  
*kṛpāvalokena hasad-*  
*vadanenopalambhitān*  
*tad-rociṣā pratihate*  
*nimīlya munir akṣiṇī*

**Перевод:**

**He offered respects by falling on the ground and then, with articles of worship in his hands, approached Śiva on his bull, Brahmā on his swan and Viṣṇu on Garuḍa, marked with their insignias, whom he understood to be his superiors by their glances of mercy and smiling faces. Because of their effulgence he closed his eyes.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 27

*atrir uvāca*  
*viśvodbhava-sthiti-layeṣu vibhajyamānair*  
*māyā-guṇair anuyugaṁ vigṛhīta-dehāḥ*  
*te brahma-viṣṇu-giriśāḥ praṇato 'smy ahaṁ vas*  
*tebhyaḥ ka eva bhavatāṁ ma ihopahūtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Atri said: In every** ***kalpa*** **Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva accept bodies with** ***guṇas*** **divided up for creating, maintaining and destroying the universe. I offer respects to you. Whom among you have I called?**

**Комментарий:**

He knew that Viṣṇu does not accept a body made of the guṇas. Who is it among you three who was called by me, because of having distinct forms of power? Who is distinct from the other two? Perhaps Viṣṇu is not among them, if his distinct powers are not shown.

### Text 28

*eko mayeha bhagavān vividha-pradhānaiś*  
*cittī-kṛtaḥ prajananāya kathaṁ nu yūyam*  
*atrāgatās tanu-bhṛtāṁ manaso 'pi dūrād*  
*brūta prasīdata mahān iha vismayo me*

**Перевод:**

**I fixed my mind upon one Lord with articles of worship, for creating progeny. Why have three of you come here, from far beyond the minds of men? Please be merciful and tell me. I am filled with bewilderment.**

**Комментарий:**

I did not worship you three. I fixed my mind on one, endowed with special powers. Concentrating on him, he would fulfill my desire. Why have you come together? I am greatly humbled. You came from far away, beyond the mind. Sambandhokti has *pradhānaiḥ.* Other versions are not acceptable.

### Text 30

*devā ūcuḥ*  
*yathā kṛtas te saṅkalpo*  
*bhāvyaṁ tenaiva nānyathā*  
*sat-saṅkalpasya te brahman*  
*yad vai dhyāyati te vayam*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***devatās*** **said: O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! Since your wish cannot be unfulfilled, as you have wished, it will come to pass, and not otherwise. We are that person upon whom you meditated.**

**Комментарий:**

They reply. You said that you surrendered to the Lord of the universe (verse 20). You did not say this was Bhagavān, full of six powers. Nor did you specify that it was the puruṣa who has power to create the worlds. Your worship merely indicated some aṁśa of secondary creation for fulfilling your desire (Śiva). According to that worship, that form has appeared. In verse 27 the indication is the person in charge of secondary creation (Brahmā). Thus the lord of the universe you invoked is merely the person endowed with powers for secondary creation (visarga). Since you worshiped everyone as one, Viṣṇu appeared, since he is the shelter of all the śaktis for creation, maintenance and destruction, though you did not specify this in your plea:

*sṛjāmi tan-niyukto ’haṁ haro harati tad-vaśaḥ \|*  
*viśvaṁ puruṣa-rūpeṇa paripāti tri-śakti-dhṛk \|\|*

**I create under his order, and Śiva destroys under his order. Holding his three energies, he protects the universe as the Paramātmā. (SB** [**2.6.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/6.32)**)**

You did also not state that the three were non-different, since their forms are different. Thus you asked which one is the supreme Lord. But you made a general statement. The person generally described cannot be understood without particulars. Thus we three have appeared.

### Text 31

*athāsmad-aṁśa-bhūtās te*  
*ātmajā loka-viśrutāḥ*  
*bhavitāro 'ṅga bhadraṁ te*  
*visrapsyanti ca te yaśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O sage! Our portions will be born as your sons, famous in the world. Good fortune to you! Your fame will spread everywhere.**

**Комментарий:**

Your efforts will bring results. Our aṁśas, empowered, but not with specific powers, will become your sons.

### Text 36

*pulastyo 'janayat patnyām*  
*agastyaṁ ca havirbhuvi*  
*so 'nya-janmani dahrāgnir*  
*viśravāś ca mahā-tapāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Pulastya begot in his wife, Havirbhū, one son named Agastya, who in another birth became Dahrāgni. Pulastya begot another son, whose name was Viśravā, endowed with great austerity.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *dakṣāgniḥ*. Citsukha has *dehāgniḥ.*

### Text 39

*krator api kriyā bhāryā*  
*vālakhilyān asūyata*  
*ṛṣīn ṣaṣṭi-sahasrāṇi*  
*jvalato brahma-tejasā*

**Перевод:**

**Kratu's wife, Kriyā, gave birth to sixty thousand great sages, named the Vālakhilyas, who were brilliant with spiritual knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *tejasaḥ.*

### Text 40

*ūrjāyāṁ jajñire putrā*  
*vasiṣṭhasya parantapa*  
*citraketu-pradhānās te*  
*sapta brahmarṣayo 'malāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The sons of Vasiṣṭha took birth in his wife, Ūrjā. These sons headed by Citraketu were the seven spotlessly sages**

**Комментарий:**

*Sapta-brahmarṣaya* is accepted by Citsukha. *Saptarṣayaḥ* is sometimes seen. Both are accepted by Śrīdhara Svāmī.

### Text 46-47

*ta ete munayaḥ kṣattar*  
*lokān sargair abhāvayan*  
*eṣa kardama-dauhitra-*  
*santānaḥ kathitas tava*  
*śṛṇvataḥ śraddadhānasya*  
*sadyaḥ pāpa-haraḥ paraḥ*  
*prasūtiṁ mānavīṁ dakṣa*  
*upayeme hy ajātmajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! These sages produced various types of population through these offspring. To you, listening with faith, I have related the topics of grandsons and descendents of Kardama, which immediately destroy all sins. Dakṣa, son of Brahmā, married Prasūti, daughter of Manu.**

**Комментарий:**

The daughters had names which indicate that they were the presiding deities of various sattvika-śaktis. Their sons were similar. The daughters of Dakṣa were similar.

### Text 49-50

*pitṛbhya ekāṁ yuktebhyo*  
*bhavāyaikāṁ bhava-cchide*  
*śraddhā maitrī dayā śāntis*  
*tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiḥ kriyonnatiḥ*  
*buddhir medhā titikṣā hrīr*  
*mūrtir dharmasya patnayaḥ*  
*śraddhāsūta śubhaṁ maitrī*  
*prasādam abhayaṁ dayā*

**Перевод:**

**He gave one daughter to the Pitṛs combined and one daughter to Śiva, who destroys material existence. The names of the thirteen daughters of Dakṣa who were given to Dharma are Śraddhā, Maitrī, Dayā, Sānti, Tuṣṭi, Puṣṭi, Kriyā, Unnati, Buddhi, Medhā, Titikṣā, Hrī and Mūrti. Śraddhā gave birth to Śubha, Maitrī produced Prasāda, Dayā gave birth to Abhaya.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *śubham, ṛtam* is seen in Citsukha’s edition.

### Text 51-52

*śāntiḥ sukhaṁ mudaṁ tuṣṭiḥ*  
*smayaṁ puṣṭir asūyata*  
*yogaṁ kriyonnatir darpam*  
*arthaṁ buddhir asūyata*  
*medhā smṛtiṁ titikṣā tu*  
*kṣemaṁ hrīḥ praśrayaṁ sutam*  
*mūrtiḥ sarva-guṇotpattir*  
*nara-nārāyaṇāv ṛṣī*

**Перевод:**

**Śānti gave birth to Sukha, Tuṣṭi gave birth to Muda, Puṣṭi gave birth to Smaya, Kriyā gave birth to Yoga, Unnati gave birth to Darpa, Buddhi gave birth to Artha, Medhā gave birth to Smṛti, Titikṣā gave birth to Kṣema, and Hrī gave birth to Praśraya. Mūrti was the source of the Lord who is the ocean of all auspicious qualities, Śrī Nara-Nārāyaṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Mūrti was the appearance place of the Lord endowed with all qualities. She was called Mūrti because she was the cause of manifestation of the form of the Lord with eternity, knowledge and bliss.

### Text 53

*yayor janmany ado viśvam*  
*abhyanandat sunirvṛtam*  
*manāṁsi kakubho vātāḥ*  
*praseduḥ sarito 'drayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**At the appearance of Nara-Nārāyaṇa, the entire world became filled of joy. Everyone's minds, all directions, the air, the rivers and the mountains became pleased.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together.

### Text 56

*devā ūcuḥ*  
*yo māyayā viracitaṁ nijayātmanīdaṁ*  
*khe rūpa-bhedam iva tat-praticakṣaṇāya*  
*etena dharma-sadane ṛṣi-mūrtinādya*  
*prāduścakāra puruṣāya namaḥ parasmai*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***devatās*** **said: We offer our respects to the Lord who has now appeared in the house of Dharma in the form of a sage to show that the universe belongs to him, is created by his energy, and is situated within him, like a cloud in the sky.**

**Комментарий:**

He has appeared to show now that the universe is situated in him like sun rays, moon rays and dust in the sky. Like the sky, he is untouched by māyā and not covered by it.

### Text 59

*tāv imau vai bhagavato*  
*harer aṁśāv ihāgatau*  
*bhāra-vyayāya ca bhuvaḥ*  
*kṛṣṇau yadu-kurūdvahau*

**Перевод:**

**These two portions of the Lord have entered Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna, best of the Yadu and Kuru dynasties for relieving the earth of its burden.**

**Комментарий:**

The two aṁśas of the Lord have entered Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna (*kṛṣṇau*), who have appeared in the Yadu dynasty (*yadu-kurūdvahau*) to remove the burden of the earth, and (*ca*) to fulfill the desires of the devotees as the main aim. The word *ca* connects the main fact with a secondary fact.

## Chapter Two

### Text 1

*vidura uvāca*  
*bhave śīlavatāṁ śreṣṭhe*  
*dakṣo duhitṛ-vatsalaḥ*  
*vidveṣam akarot kasmād*  
*anādṛtyātmajāṁ satīm*

**Перевод:**

**Vidura said: Why did Dakṣa, affectionate towards his daughter, neglect her and show hatred to Lord Śiva, who is the best among the gentle?**

**Комментарий:**

Her name was Satī, suitable to her character.

### Text 5

*tatra praviṣṭam ṛṣayo*  
*dṛṣṭvārkam iva rociṣā*  
*bhrājamānaṁ vitimiraṁ*  
*kurvantaṁ tan mahat sadaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The sages saw Dakṣa enter there like the sun, shining with lustre, making the large assemble free of darkness.**

### Text 6

*udatiṣṭhan sadasyās te*  
*sva-dhiṣṇyebhyaḥ sahāgnayaḥ*  
*ṛte viriñcāṁ śarvaṁ ca*  
*tad-bhāsākṣipta-cetasaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The assembly along with the fire-gods, except for Brahmā and Śiva, rose from their seats, influenced by that radiance.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Sometimes *ṛte viriñcāt śarvāt ca* is seen.

### Text 9

*śrūyatāṁ brahmarṣayo me*  
*saha-devāḥ sahāgnayaḥ*  
*sādhūnāṁ bruvato vṛttaṁ*  
*nājñānān na ca matsarāt*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brahmarṣis, devatās*** **and fire-gods! Hear the words I speak about the conduct of the gentle. I do not speak out of ignorance or hatred.**

**Комментарий:**

I speak about the conduct of the gentle, not out of ignorance or envy.

### Text 10

*ayaṁ tu loka-pālānāṁ*  
*yaśo-ghno nirapatrapaḥ*  
*sadbhir ācaritaḥ panthā*  
*yena stabdhena dūṣitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Being shameless, Śiva has destroyed the fame of the protectors of the planets, because the most proud Śiva has polluted the path followed by the well bred.**

**Комментарий:**

He criticized Śiva in the blindness of anger, giving up speaking about the conduct of the gentle.

### Text 11

*eṣa me śiṣyatāṁ prāpto*  
*yan me duhitur agrahīt*  
*pāṇiṁ viprāgni-mukhataḥ*  
*sāvitryā iva sādhuvat*

**Перевод:**

**He has accepted himself as my subordinate by marrying my daughter in the presence of fire and** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **posing as a well bred person.**

**Комментарий:**

He should be punishable (*śiṣyatām*) by me.

### Text 13

*lupta-kriyāyāśucaye*  
*mānine bhinna-setave*  
*anicchann apy adāṁ bālāṁ*  
*śūdrāyevośatīṁ giram*

**Перевод:**

**Though not desiring to do so, I have given my daughter to a person who does not perform rituals, is impure, is proud, and violates the rules of conduct. I have acted as if giving the message of the Vedas to a** ***śūdra.***

**Комментарий:**

Just as someone, under another’s control, gives a śūdra the Vedas, I controlled by others, have given my daughter to this great soul. But actually I gave her willingly.

### Text 14-15

*pretāvāseṣu ghoreṣu*  
*pretair bhūta-gaṇair vṛtaḥ*  
*aṭaty unmattavan nagno*  
*vyupta-keśo hasan rudan*  
*citā-bhasma-kṛta-snānaḥ*  
*preta-sraṅ-nrasthi-bhūṣaṇaḥ*  
*śivāpadeśo hy aśivo*  
*matto matta-jana-priyaḥ*  
*patiḥ pramatha-nāthānāṁ*  
*tamo-mātrātmakātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**Naked like a madman, sometimes laughing and sometimes crying, he lives in filthy places like crematoriums, and surrounded by ghosts and demons, wanders about in horrible places of the dead. Using crematorium ashes as his bath, he ornaments his body with a garland of skulls and human bones. He is inauspicious though he has the false name Śiva. He is insane, dear to the insane, the leader of leaders of the insane, the leader of the most ignorant.**

**Комментарий:**

Actually he acts in this manner out of madness of prema for the Lord. This is the conduct of siddha Vaiṣṇavas, famous in the Purāṇas.

### Text 20

*vijñāya śāpaṁ giriśānugāgraṇīr*  
*nandīśvaro roṣa-kaṣāya-dūṣitaḥ*  
*dakṣāya śāpaṁ visasarja dāruṇaṁ*  
*ye cānvamodaṁs tad-avācyatāṁ dvijāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Upon understanding that Śiva had been cursed, Nandīśvara, Lord Śiva's principal follower, with red eyes of anger, cursed Dakṣa and all the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **who had tolerated Dakṣa's curse of Śiva.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *tad avadyatām* instead of *tad-avācyatām*.

### Text 21

*ya etan martyam uddiśya*  
*bhagavaty apratidruhi*  
*druhyaty ajñaḥ pṛthag-dṛṣṭis*  
*tattvato vimukho bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**He who, identifying with the mortal body, offends Śiva, who is non-violent, will become a fool, seeing in terms of different bodies and rejecting the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

*Pṛthag-dṛṣṭiḥ* means a person who thinks Śiva is in a low position.

### Text 22

*gṛheṣu kūṭa-dharmeṣu*  
*sakto grāmya-sukhecchayā*  
*karma-tantraṁ vitanute*  
*veda-vāda-vipanna-dhīḥ*

**Перевод:**

**That person, attached to cheating rules of household life with a desire for material happiness, his intelligence spoiled by misleading followers of the Vedas, will become totally absorbed in** ***karma-khāṇda*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *vitanutām* or *vitanutāt* are seen.

### Text 23

*buddhyā parābhidhyāyinyā*  
*vismṛtātma-gatiḥ paśuḥ*  
*strī-kāmaḥ so 'stv atitarāṁ*  
*dakṣo basta-mukho 'cirāt*

**Перевод:**

**Let Dakṣa who has forgotten the spiritual goal because of accepting his body as the self, who is excessively attached to women like an animal, immediately take a head like a goat’s.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 24

*vidyā-buddhir avidyāyāṁ*  
*karmamayyām asau jaḍaḥ*  
*saṁsarantv iha ye cāmum*  
*anu śarvāvamāninam*

**Перевод:**

**The curses are suitable because he takes ignorance in the form of** ***karma*** **as knowledge, and he is foolish. Let those who follow Dakṣa, who insulted Śiva, take repeated birth and death in this world.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *asāv ajaḥ saṁsarantu* is seen.

### Text 28

*bhava-vrata-dharā ye ca*  
*ye ca tān samanuvratāḥ*  
*pāṣaṇḍinas te bhavantu*  
*sac-chāstra-paripanthinaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Let those who take vows to satisfy Lord Śiva and who follows such persons become heretics and be obstacles to the genuine scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

If the vows to satisfy Śiva were according to the Vedas, the curse would not be suitable, since he could not become a pāsaṇḍi. If he were to take shelter of non-Vedic ideas and perform vows for Śiva, then he should become a pāṣāṇḍi.

### Text 31

*eṣa eva hi lokānāṁ*  
*śivaḥ panthāḥ sanātanaḥ*  
*yaṁ pūrve cānusantasthur*  
*yat-pramāṇaṁ janārdanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas which were followed previously and in which the subject of inquiry is Janārdana, are the auspicious, eternal path for humanity.**

**Комментарий:**

It is a fault to worship devatās independently since Janārdana is the root of the Vedas. *Yat pramāṇam janārdanaḥ* means “in which the root is Janārdana.” Attaining the Lord by independent worship of devatās is denied in the Gītā:

*ye ’py anya-devatā-bhaktā yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ \|*  
*te ’pi mām eva kaunteya yajanty avidhi-pūrvakam \|\|*

**Those who are devoted to other gods and with faith worship them worship me by the wrong method, O son of Kuntī.**

One should not disrespect Brahmā, Śiva and others.

Gautamīya-tantra says:

*gopālaṁ pūjayed yas tu nindayed anya-devatām \|*  
*astu tāvat paro dharmaḥ pūrva-dharmo’pi naśyati \|\|*

**If a person who worships Kṛṣṇa criticizes the devatās whatever dharma he has accomplished is destroyed, what to speak of attaining dharma in the future.**

*hayaśīrṣā māṁ pathi deva-helanāt*

**May Hayagrīva protect me from disrespecting the devatās when going on a path. (SB** [**6.8.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/8.17)**)**

This principle is also seen in the story of Citraketu. Kapila condemned disrespecting ordinary living beings, what to speak of Śiva.

### Text 33

*maitreya uvāca*  
*tasyaivaṁ vadataḥ śāpaṁ*  
*bhṛgoḥ sa bhagavān bhavaḥ*  
*niścakrāma tataḥ kiñcid*  
*vimanā iva sānugaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: While Bhṛgu was cursing in this way, Śiva, in a morose mood, left with his followers.**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing the great anger of Nandīśvara, and fearing that he would speak badly of Janārdana and his followers, Śiva left with his followers. He seemed to be depressed, though actually he was not since he was an ātmārāma. Rejecting both the path of karma and the path of Śiva because of the curses of the two parties, the path of the Vaiṣṇavas is shown to be the purest. One can see Bhakti Sandarbha for more details.

### Text 34-35

*te 'pi viśva-sṛjaḥ satraṁ*  
*sahasra-parivatsarān*  
*saṁvidhāya maheṣvāsa*  
*yatrejya ṛṣabho hariḥ*  
*āplutyāvabhṛthaṁ yatra*  
*gaṅgā yamunayānvitā*  
*virajenātmanā sarve*  
*svaṁ svaṁ dhāma yayus tataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! The creators of the universe, after performing sacrifice for a thousand years in which the Supreme Lord was the best of those worthy of worship, and taking their** ***avabhṛtha*** **bath at the confluence of the Gaṅgā and the Yamunā, with purified minds, departed for their respective abodes.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

## Chapter Three

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*sadā vidviṣator evaṁ*  
*kālo vai dhriyamāṇayoḥ*  
*jāmātuḥ śvaśurasyāpi*  
*sumahān aticakrame*

**Перевод:**

**Considerable time passed as the father-in-law and son –in-law continued their enmity.**

**Комментарий:**

Śiva did not hate him but hated his ignorance, like a father hates the ignorance of a child.

### Text 5-7

*tad upaśrutya nabhasi*  
*khe-carāṇāṁ prajalpatām*  
*satī dākṣāyaṇī devī*  
*pitṛ-yajña-mahotsavam*  
*vrajantīḥ sarvato digbhya*  
*upadeva-vara-striyaḥ*  
*vimāna-yānāḥ sa-preṣṭhā*  
*niṣka-kaṇṭhīḥ suvāsasaḥ*  
*dṛṣṭvā sva-nilayābhyāśe*  
*lolākṣīr mṛṣṭa-kuṇḍalāḥ*  
*patiṁ bhūta-patiṁ devam*  
*autsukyād abhyabhāṣata*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing from the mouth of the gossiping Khecaras in the sky about the great sacrifice being held by her father, and seeing near her house the wives of the** ***upadevas,*** **with darting eyes, fine cloth, attractive earrings and** ***niṣkas*** **on their throats, mounted on their airplanes, going there from all directions along with their husbands, she spoke with longing to her husband, lord of the ghosts.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together.

### Text 9

*tasmin bhaginyo mama bhartṛbhiḥ svakair*  
*dhruvaṁ gamiṣyanti suhṛd-didṛkṣavaḥ*  
*ahaṁ ca tasmin bhavatābhikāmaye*  
*sahopanītaṁ paribarham arhitum*

**Перевод:**

**My sisters along with their husbands will certainly go to the sacrifice. Desiring to see my friends, along with you I also desire to accept gifts offered by my parents.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses are joined together.

### Text 14

*tan me prasīdedam amartya vāñchitaṁ*  
*kartuṁ bhavān kāruṇiko batārhati*  
*tvayātmano 'rdhe 'ham adabhra-cakṣuṣā*  
*nirūpitā mānugṛhāṇa yācitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Please grand my desire, O lord! Since you are merciful you should do this. Being full of knowledge, you have accepted my as half of your body. Therefore, being requested, please oblige me.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *arhasi* instead of *arhati*.

### Text 15

*ṛṣir uvāca*  
*evaṁ giritraḥ priyayābhibhāṣitaḥ*  
*pratyabhyadhatta prahasan suhṛt-priyaḥ*  
*saṁsmārito marma-bhidaḥ kuvāg-iṣūn*  
*yān āha ko viśva-sṛjāṁ samakṣataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Addressed in this way by his wife, Śiva, replied with a smile, while reminding his wife of the heart-piercing arrows of insults which were spoken in the presence of the creators of the universe.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *abhibhāsita* sometimes *nāthita* is seen. The insults were spoke by Dakṣa’s followers.

### Text 17

*vidyā-tapo-vitta-vapur-vayaḥ-kulaiḥ*  
*satāṁ guṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhir asattametaraiḥ*  
*smṛtau hatāyāṁ bhṛta-māna-durdṛśaḥ*  
*stabdhā na paśyanti hi dhāma bhūyasām*

**Перевод:**

**When discrimination is lost by the six qualities of the saintly, such as knowledge, austerity, wealth, body, youth and family--which become faults in the demons--when people become blinded by pride, nourished by those qualities, and become callous, they do not see the glory of great souls.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *bhṛtamāna* sometimes *hy abhimāna* is seen. *Stabdhā* is accepted by Citsukha.

### Text 18

*naitādṛśānāṁ sva-jana-vyapekṣayā*  
*gṛhān pratīyād anavasthitātmanām*  
*ye 'bhyāgatān vakra-dhiyābhicakṣate*  
*āropita-bhrūbhir amarṣaṇākṣibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One should not go to the houses of persons who have disturbed minds, who will look at the guests with crooked mind, raised brows and angry glances, while considering them to be relatives.**

**Комментарий:**

They look at guests with angry glances indicated by their brows, because of crooked minds.

### Text 19

*tathāribhir na vyathate śilīmukhaiḥ*  
*śete 'rditāṅgo hṛdayena dūyatā*  
*svānāṁ yathā vakra-dhiyāṁ duruktibhir*  
*divā-niśaṁ tapyati marma-tāḍitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Injured in the limbs by arrows fired by enemies, one does not suffer much and can sleep. However, injured by harsh words uttered by relatives of crooked mind, one suffers day and night with distressed heart.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes the person can sleep.

### Text 22

*pratyudgama-praśrayaṇābhivādanaṁ*  
*vidhīyate sādhu mithaḥ sumadhyame*  
*prājñaiḥ parasmai puruṣāya cetasā*  
*guhā-śayāyaiva na deha-mānine*

**Перевод:**

**O thin-waisted wife! Rising from one’s seat, acting affectionately and offering mutual respects should actually be done by the wise with the thought that one is offering respects to the Supreme Lord dwelling within that person, not thinking of the person’s body.**

**Комментарий:**

This statement concerns the non-Vaiṣṇava. One should say, “The Lord has as much affection for you as you have for the Lord.” The Lord says *mad-bhakta pūjābhyadhikā:* one should worship my devotee with great devotion. (SB [11.19.21](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.20)) One should not think of the external body.

### Text 23

*sattvaṁ viśuddhaṁ vasudeva-śabditaṁ*  
*yad īyate tatra pumān apāvṛtaḥ*  
*sattve ca tasmin bhagavān vāsudevo*  
*hy adhokṣajo me namasā vidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**The pure** ***antaḥ-karaṇa*** **is called Vasudeva, because there the Supreme Lord appears without coverings. In that** ***antaḥ-karaṇa*****, I worshipped the Supreme Lord Vāsudeva, beyond the material senses, by offering respects.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhagavat Sandarbha 117:**

The meaning is this. *Viśuddham* means it is devoid of any material portion since it is a function of the svarūpa-śakti. That which is sattva, which is especially pure, is called *vasudeva.* Why is this existence of the nature of *vasudeva*? It is so called because *(yat)* in that condition *(tatra*) Vāsudeva (*pumān*) manifests *(īyate).* Though beyond material vision, it is called sattva (for people’s understanding) in the first verse because of similarity to the well known sattva of this world.

However there is a second meaning. Vāsudeva means he who is perceived in *vasudeva* condition. He is famous as the supreme Lord. He is perceived in viśuddha-sattva. The meaning is explained by taking the conventional meaning of Vāsudeva.

Vāsudeva can also mean “he who illuminates the devatās (*vāsayati devam*).” Or *vasu* can mean “everything dwells in him (*vasati asmin*) and *deva* can mean he who shines or plays (*divyati*). Together, these two form the word Vāsudeva.

It is also said *dharma iṣṭaṁ dhanaṁ nṝṇāṁ:* the respectable wealth for human beings is possessing *dharma.* (SB [11.19.39](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.36)) Since *vasu* means wealth and wealth means dharma, Vāsudeva can mean “He is revealed *(deva)* by wealth of dharma (*vasu*) in the form of bhakti.”

Thus *vasudeva* means viśuddha-sattva. The vigraha, a self-manifesting light, is revealed by knowledge of the Lord.

*kaivalyaṁ sāttvikaṁ jñāna rajo vaikalpikaṁ ca yat*  
*prākṛtaṁ tāmasaṁ jñāna man-niṣṭhaṁ nirguṇaṁ smṛtam*

**Knowledge concerning the jīva apart from the body is in sattva. Knowledge with various options of doubt is in rajas. Knowledge of material life is in tamas. Knowledge concerning me, however, is understood to be beyond the guṇas. (SB** [**11.25.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/25.24)**)**

In many places in scripture, knowledge of the Lord is proved to be beyond the guṇas. That is shown by the word *viśuddha*. It is clear that it is self-manifesting, arising from the svarūpa-śakti. “Being perceived in sattva” also means that the Lord is perceived by the senses which are viśuddha-sattva.

The function of the svarūpa-śakti is described in SB [4.3.23](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/3.23) quoted above. Vāsudeva *(pumān*) appears (*īyate*) uncovered *(apāvṛtaḥ*). If he were material sattva, the Lord would merely be like a reflection. A reflection in a mirror is covered by dirt and then is uncovered by cleaning (but is still not the real form). This form in sattva is intensely contemplated (*vidhīyate*) by me in my mind. Sattva becomes one with the mind. Otherwise it would not be possible meditate on the Lord with the mind.

“One should simply meditate using the mind. Why is sattva necessary?” It is necessary because (*hi*) the Lord is beyond *(adhoḥ*) knowledge of the senses (*akṣaja*). Instead of *manasā* sometimes *namasā* (by obeisance) is seen. And instead of *hi* the word *anu* is found. The Lord who is revealed by his svarūpa śakti is served (*anuvidhīyate*) with obeisances *(namasā*). The Lord is not revealed by the mind or obeisances. The Lord who is not seen by us is served by us using obeisances. This meaning makes sense.

Since *vasudeva* and *mūrti* are the very form of viśuddha-sattva which reveals the form of the Lord, Mūrti is famous as the wife of Dharma in manifesting Nara-nārāyaṇa. Persons like Ānakadundubhi should also be considered to be *vasudeva* (basis for the Lord's appearance). By consulting the text, it is understood that those śaktis who manifest as Śraddhā, Puṣṭi etc. manifest their portions as Mūrti, taking the form of the wife of a portion of the Lord’s śakti Dharma (father of Nara-nārāyaṇa).

*turye dharma-kalā-sarge nara-nārāyaṇāv ṛṣī \|*  
*bhūtvātmopaśamopetam akarod duścaraṁ tapaḥ \|\|*

**Fourth, appearing in the wife of Dharma, he became Nara-nārāyaṇa, and performed severe austerities which give peace to the soul. (SB** [**1.3.9**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/3.9)**)**

*Dharma-kalā* here means the śakti of Dharma. The śuddha-sattva form called *vasudeva* appeared in this woman who is śakti, since the result was the appearance of the Lord named Nāra-nārāyaṇa in her. She is named Mūrti:

*mūrtiḥ sarva-guṇotpattir nara-nārāyaṇāv ṛṣī*

**Mūrti was the source of the Lord who is the ocean of all auspicious qualities, Śrī Nara-Nārāyaṇa. (SB** [**4.1.52**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/1.49)**)**

In her there was the manifestation (*utpattiḥ*) of Bhagavān with all qualities (*sarva-guṇa*). She was called Mūrti because she was the cause of the manifestation of the form of eternity, knowledge and bliss called Bhagavān. By seeing the result to be a manifestation of the Lord, it is concluded that she has the same śuddha sattva nature as Ānakadundubhi.

*vasudevaṁ hareḥ sthānaṁ vadanty ānakadundubhim*

**Vasudeva, who provided the proper place for the appearance Kṛṣṇa, was also known as Ānakadundubhi (sound of drums). (SB** [**9.24.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/24.28)**)**

If he were not viśuddha-sattva then the phrase *hareḥ sthānaḥ* would be meaningless. One should also accept that the appearance of Śrī and other śaktis takes place by viśuddha-sattva, predominated by a special porition of hlādini or other elements. They take three forms: the form of abundance ( of wealth, beauty etc.) in the Lord (displaying his qualities for his pleasure), the form that creates that abundance in persons to whom they give mercy, and the form arising from a portion of that abundance. They are of two types: without form (pure śakti) and with form, as presiding deities situated along with the Lord’s form, appearing with him when he appears on earth.

### Text 24

*tat te nirīkṣyo na pitāpi deha-kṛd*  
*dakṣo mama dviṭ tad-anuvratāś ca ye*  
*yo viśvasṛg-yajña-gataṁ varoru mām*  
*anāgasaṁ durvacasākarot tiraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Dear wife! Therefore you should not see your envious father Dakṣa who raised you, and you should not see his followers. He scolded me with harsh words when I went to the sacrifice of the creators of the universe, though I was innocent.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *tvayā* instead of *tat te.*

## Chapter Four

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*etāvad uktvā virarāma śaṅkaraḥ*  
*patny-aṅga-nāśaṁ hy ubhayatra cintayan*  
*suhṛd-didṛkṣuḥ pariśaṅkitā bhavān*  
*niṣkrāmatī nirviśatī dvidhāsa sā*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Having said this, and understanding for certain that whether he prevented her from going or whether she went, she would be destroyed, Śiva became silent. She became divided: desiring to see her relatives she left the house, and in fear of Śiva, she reentered the house.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukhaḥ has *pariśaṅkinī* instead of *pariśaṅkitā.*

### Text 2

*suhṛd-didṛkṣā-pratighāta-durmanāḥ*  
*snehād rudaty aśru-kalātivihvalā*  
*bhavaṁ bhavāny apratipūruṣaṁ ruṣā*  
*pradhakṣyatīvaikṣata jāta-vepathuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Her body shaking, Satī, crying out of affection, agitated with tears, upset by the obstacle to her seeing her relatives, glanced with anger at Śiva, who has no equal, as if to burn him up to ashes.**

**Комментарий:**

She glanced as if she were able to burn him to ashes.

### Text 3

*tato viniḥśvasya satī vihāya taṁ*  
*śokena roṣeṇa ca dūyatā hṛdā*  
*pitror agāt straiṇa-vimūḍha-dhīr gṛhān*  
*premṇātmano yo 'rdham adāt satāṁ priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Breathing heavily, with heart afflicted by grief and anger, Satī, her intelligence bewildered by the Lord’s māyā, left Śiva, dear to the saintly, who had given her half of his body out of affection. She went to the houses of her father.**

**Комментарий:**

Her intelligence was bewildered by the Lord’s māyā (*straiṇa-vimūḍha-dhīḥ*). Sambandhokti has *straiṇyā.*

### Text 5

*tāṁ sārikā-kanduka-darpaṇāmbuja-*  
*śvetātapatra-vyajana-srag-ādibhiḥ*  
*gītāyanair dundubhi-śaṅkha-veṇubhir*  
*vṛṣendram āropya viṭaṅkitā yayuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Placing her on the bull, appearing splendid with a** ***sārikā*** **bird, a ball, a mirror, a lotus, white umbrella, fan, and garland, as well as accompaniment of songs, with flute, conch and drums, they proceeded.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *sārikām*, meaning “going.”

### Text 7

*tām āgatāṁ tatra na kaścanādriyad*  
*vimānitāṁ yajña-kṛto bhayāj janaḥ*  
*ṛte svasṝr vai jananīṁ ca sādarāḥ*  
*premāśru-kaṇṭhyaḥ pariṣasvajur mudā*

**Перевод:**

**Out of fear of Dakṣa, none of the people received her when she arrived, except her mother and sisters. With love and tears of affection, they embraced her in joy, though she had not been respected by others.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *vimāninaḥ* instead of *vimānitām.* Sambandhokti has *premnā* instead of *prema.*

### Text 11

*devy uvāca*  
*na yasya loke 'sty atiśāyanaḥ priyas*  
*tathāpriyo deha-bhṛtāṁ priyātmanaḥ*  
*tasmin samastātmani mukta-vairake*  
*ṛte bhavantaṁ katamaḥ pratīpayet*

**Перевод:**

**Satī said: Who except Dakṣa would hate Śiva, who is without an enemy, the cause of the universe, to whom, the soul of beings, there is no one superior in this world, and who neither hates nor favors anyone?**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *pratīyataḥ.*

### Text 12

*doṣān pareṣāṁ hi guṇeṣu sādhavo*  
*gṛhṇanti kecin na bhavādṛśo dvija*  
*guṇāṁś ca phalgūn bahulī-kariṣṇavo*  
*mahattamās teṣv avidad bhavān agham*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! Saintly persons see that faults are capable of being transformed into good qualities, and see good qualities, not the faults. You are the opposite. The best persons magnify even meager good qualities in another person. You see only fault in others.**

**Комментарий:**

Some remarkable sādhus do not accept faults in good qualities. They consider the faults to be some aspect of the good qualities. In other words other ordinary sādhus see good qualities and faults as they are. The greatest among the sadhus make insignificant good qualities into great qualities, and see faults as good qualities (*ca*). But you are not like that. You are the opposite. You do not see good qualities or bad qualities as they really are and see good qualities as faults, and make insignificant faults into great faults. There are bad people, worse people and the worst people. You are the worst. The word *ca* from the previous statement indicates that the worst person accepts good qualities as faults. You are the opposite of the person with remarkable qualities. You are worst compared to the greatest persons. What to speak of being compared to Śiva.

### Text 13

*nāścaryam etad yad asatsu sarvadā*  
*mahad-vinindā kuṇapātma-vādiṣu*  
*serṣyaṁ mahāpūruṣa-pāda-pāṁsubhir*  
*nirasta-tejaḥsu tad eva śobhanam*

**Перевод:**

**It is not surprising that the materialists, claiming that the gross body is the soul, constantly criticize the devotees with envy and become deprived of their powers by the dust from the feet of the great devotees. That glory of that criticism is this result it gives.**

**Комментарий:**

It is not surprising that materialists criticize devotees because they claim the body is the self (*kuṇapātma-vādiṣu*). They reject the pure ātmā and think the impure body is the best. They are ridiculed. For those devoid of power, who are now as if dead, the glory is announcing the power of the devotee. They have nothing left. Their power is destroyed by a particle of power from the devotee’s feet. This indicates their insignificance.

### Text 16

*kiṁ vā śivākhyam aśivaṁ na vidus tvad anye*  
*brahmādayas tam avakīrya jaṭāḥ śmaśāne*  
*tan-mālya-bhasma-nṛkapāly avasat piśācair*  
*ye mūrdhabhir dadhati tac-caraṇāvasṛṣṭam*

**Перевод:**

**Do Brahmā and others know a Śiva who is inauspicious, who has scattered hair, lives in the crematorium, with ghosts, wears the discarded garlands, ashes and human skulls for decorations? They carry on their heads the water used to wash his feet.**

**Комментарий:**

*Vā* here indicates a question. Asking a question, is only to show that he is full of envy. If it means agreement, then it is in a mocking way, without pain. The cause of his purity is then explained.

### Text 17

*karṇau pidhāya nirayād yad akalpa īśe*  
*dharmāvitary asṛṇibhir nṛbhir asyamāne*  
*chindyāt prasahya ruśatīm asatīṁ prabhuś cej*  
*jihvām asūn api tato visṛjet sa dharmaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the protector of religion, a master, is insulted by uncontrollable people, one should leave that place, blocking one’s ears, if he cannot kill the person or he should die. If possible he should forcibly cut the tongue of the offenders who speak badly and then give up his own life. That is the correct procedure.**

**Комментарий:**

If very capable of doing so, he should cut out the tongue of the offender and give up his life. If not so capable he should block his ears and leave.

### Text 18

*atas tavotpannam idaṁ kalevaraṁ*  
*na dhārayiṣye śiti-kaṇṭha-garhiṇaḥ*  
*jagdhasya mohād dhi viśuddhim andhaso*  
*jugupsitasyoddharaṇaṁ pracakṣate*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore I shall not maintain this body produced by you, who have criticized Lord Śiva. It is said that the purification for eating poisonous food by mistake is to vomit it.**

**Комментарий:**

Since I cannot cut out your tongue for offending Śiva, then I do the next best thing. The remedy (*viśuddhim*) for eating unhealthy (*jugupsitasya*) food is vomiting.

### Text 22

*naitena dehena hare kṛtāgaso*  
*dehodbhavenālam alaṁ kujanmanā*  
*vrīḍā mamābhūt kujana-prasaṅgatas*  
*taj janma dhig yo mahatām avadya-kṛt*

**Перевод:**

**My low-born body is completely useless, since it comes from the body of a person who offended Śiva. I have been humiliated by bad association. This birth which has offended a devotee is useless.**

**Комментарий:**

*Alam* indicates opposing according to Amara-koṣa. It also means useless such as in *alam vistareṇa*: what is the use of speaking further. Repetition of the words is for intensity of expression. The negative indicates agreement. Did I not attain shame by this body? Yes, I did. Then it has no use. Or the body has become useless (*alam na*). Thus enough of maintaining this body (*etena alam*).

### Text 24

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ity adhvare dakṣam anūdya śatru-han*  
*kṣitāv udīcīṁ niṣasāda śānta-vāk*  
*spṛṣṭvā jalaṁ pīta-dukūla-saṁvṛtā*  
*nimīlya dṛg yoga-pathaṁ samāviśat*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: O Vidura! After speaking to Dakṣa in the sacrificial arena, sipping water, dressed in yellow garments, Satī sat down on the earth facing north. Closing her eyes, she entered the path of** ***yoga.***

**Комментарий:**

Even in that condition she followed proper conduct by dressing in yellow cloth. Satī is the subject of discussion.

### Text 27

*tataḥ sva-bhartuś caraṇāmbujāsavaṁ*  
*jagad-guroś cintayatī na cāparam*  
*dadarśa deho hata-kalmaṣaḥ satī*  
*sadyaḥ prajajvāla samādhijāgninā*

**Перевод:**

**Meditating on the sweetness of the lotus feet of her husband, the** ***guru*** **for the universe, she did not see anything else. Her body, whose identity as Dakṣa’s daughter was destroyed by the fire of** ***samādhi*****, suddenly lit up in a flash.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *dehe hata-kalmaṣaḥ* (having destroyed impurity in the body and heart).

### Text 30

*so 'yaṁ durmarṣa-hṛdayo brahma-dhruk ca*  
*loke 'pakīrtiṁ mahatīm avāpsyati*  
*yad-aṅgajāṁ svāṁ puruṣa-dviḍ udyatāṁ*  
*na pratyaṣedhan mṛtaye 'parādhataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Intolerant Dakṣa, violent to** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **will gain great infamy in the world, because, hating Śiva, he did not prevent his daughter when she prepared to die because of his neglect.**

**Комментарий:**

Some versions have *brahma-bandhuḥ* instead of *brahma-dhruk* and *loke ca kīrtim asatīm* instead of *loke ‘pakīrtim.* However these are not accepted, though commonly found. The word *ca* indicates that he will also go to hell.

## Chapter Five

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*bhavo bhavānyā nidhanaṁ prajāpater*  
*asat-kṛtāyā avagamya nāradāt*  
*sva-pārṣada-sainyaṁ ca tad-adhvararbhubhir*  
*vidrāvitaṁ krodham apāram ādadhe*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Hearing from Nārada about that Satī had died because of the insults to her by Dakṣa, and that soldiers had been driven away the Ṛbhus who arose from the sacrifice, Śiva became angry.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *tad-adhvara rtvig vidrāvitam.*

### Text 10

*yas tv anta-kāle vyupta-jaṭā-kalāpaḥ*  
*sva-śūla-sūcy-arpita-dig-gajendraḥ*  
*vitatya nṛtyaty uditāstra-dor-dhvajān*  
*uccāṭṭa-hāsa-stanayitnu-bhinna-dik*

**Перевод:**

**With scattered locks, piercing the directions with his trident, waving his flag-like arms holding upraised weapons, and breaking the directions by the roar of his loud laughter, Śiva dances at the time of universal destruction.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses are taken together.

### Text 11

*amarṣayitvā tam asahya-tejasaṁ*  
*manyu-plutaṁ durnirīkṣyaṁ bhru-kuṭyā*  
*karāla-daṁṣṭrābhir udasta-bhāgaṇaṁ*  
*syāt svasti kiṁ kopayato vidhātuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**After angering Śiva , terrifying in appearance, who has intolerable strength, great anger, and who scatters the planets by the his frown and protruding teeth, how can a person, even Brahmā, have good fortune?**

**Комментарий:**

After causing intolerable anger in Śiva (*amarṣayitvā*), how can a person have good fortune?

### Text 21

*pūṣṇo hy apātayad dantān*  
*kāliṅgasya yathā balaḥ*  
*śapyamāne garimaṇi*  
*yo 'hasad darśayan dataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Vīrabhadra made Pūṣā’s teeth fall out, just as Balarāma made the King of Kaliṅga’s teeth fall out. Pūṣā had smiled, showing his teeth, when Śiva was being rebuked by Dakṣa.**

**Комментарий:**

Some versions have *pūṣṇoḥ* (Pūṣā and Indra). He made Pūṣa’s teeth fall out. He had no teeth. But *śapyamāne* is in the singular. Śrīdhara Svāmī and Citsukha do not accept *pūṣṇoḥ.*

## Chapter Six

### Text 1-2

*maitreya uvāca*  
*atha deva-gaṇāḥ sarve*  
*rudrānīkaiḥ parājitāḥ*  
*śūla-paṭṭiśa-nistriṁśa-*  
*gadā-parigha-mudgaraiḥ*  
*sañchinna-bhinna-sarvāṅgāḥ*  
*sartvik-sabhyā bhayākulāḥ*  
*svayambhuve namaskṛtya*  
*kārtsnyenaitan nyavedayan*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: In great fear, all the** ***devatās,*** **the assembled guests and priests, wounded all over their bodies, defeated by the soldiers of Śiva, who had been armed with tridents, spears, swords, clubs, iron bars and hammers, offering respects to Brahmā and informed him fully of the events.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 3

*upalabhya puraivaitad*  
*bhagavān abja-sambhavaḥ*  
*nārāyaṇaś ca viśvātmā*  
*na kasyādhvaram īyatuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā and Viṣṇu, soul of the universe, knowing that this would happen, had not gone to the sacrifice of Dakṣa.**

**Комментарий:**

Brahmā was the totality of jīvas and Viṣnu was Paramātma (*viśvātmā*). Thus they knew what would happen. Citsukha has *kasya neyatur adhvaram.*

### Text 4

*tad ākarṇya vibhuḥ prāha*  
*tejīyasi kṛtāgasi*  
*kṣemāya tatra sā bhūyān*  
*na prāyeṇa bubhūṣatām*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing of the events, Brahmā spoke. Since you have committed offense to a great soul, though you desire to live with material comforts, generally, it is better that you do not live!**

**Комментарий:**

There should be no peace for those who desire to attain comforts, having committed offense. Citsukha and Puṇyāraṇya have *kṛtāgasām:* those who desire to committers of offenses. Or Satī will not have peace at all, since Dakṣa has committed offense (*kṛtāgasi*) among those who desire to be related to Śiva (*tejīyas*i).

### Text 6

*āśāsānā jīvitam adhvarasya*  
*lokaḥ sa-pālaḥ kupite na yasmin*  
*tam āśu devaṁ priyayā vihīnaṁ*  
*kṣamāpayadhvaṁ hṛdi viddhaṁ duruktaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring a revival of the sacrifice, you should quickly go and beg forgiveness from Śiva, now bereft of his wife, his heart pierced by harsh words, by whose anger the worlds and their protectors cannot exist.**

**Комментарий:**

His heart being pierced is a statement made according to external vision.

### Text 7

*nāhaṁ na yajño na ca yūyam anye*  
*ye deha-bhājo munayaś ca tattvam*  
*viduḥ pramāṇaṁ bala-vīryayor vā*  
*yasyātma-tantrasya ka upāyaṁ vidhitset*

**Перевод:**

**I, Indra, you, anyone with a body, and the sages do not know the strength, influence and the true nature of Śiva, who is completely independent. Who should provide a means to do this?**

**Комментарий:**

Yajña refers to the Indra during Svāyambhuva-manvantara. He was an aveśāvatara of Viṣṇu. As the deity of sacrifices, during the reign of Prācīnabarhi during the sixth Manvantara, he still remained. Fourteen verses describe Kailāsa.

### Text 10

*nānā-maṇimayaiḥ śṛṅgair*  
*nānā-dhātu-vicitritaiḥ*  
*nānā-druma-latā-gulmair*  
*nānā-mṛga-gaṇāvṛtaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**It is endowed with peaks of various jewels, colorful with many mineral, covered with various trees, creepers and bushes, and filled with various animals.**

**Комментарий:**

These phrases all describe the peaks.

### Text 11

*nānāmala-prasravaṇair*  
*nānā-kandara-sānubhiḥ*  
*ramaṇaṁ viharantīnāṁ*  
*ramaṇaiḥ siddha-yoṣitām*

**Перевод:**

**It is filled with many pure waterfalls and various caves and plateaus. It is enjoyed by the wives of the perfected beings who sport there with their husbands.**

**Комментарий:**

These phrases also describe the peaks.

### Text 16

*svarṇārṇa-śata-patraiś ca*  
*vara-reṇuka-jātibhiḥ*  
*kubjakair mallikābhiś ca*  
*mādhavībhiś ca maṇḍitam*

**Перевод:**

**The mountain is decorated with golden** ***śata-patra*****, oleander*****,*** **cardamom, jasmine,** ***kubja, mallikā*** **and** ***mādhavī.***

**Комментарий:**

*Arṇa* means color.

### Text 17

*panasodumbarāśvattha-*  
*plakṣa-nyagrodha-hiṅgubhiḥ*  
*bhūrjair oṣadhibhiḥ pūgai*  
*rājapūgaiś ca jambubhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Kailāsa Hill is also decorated with such trees as jackfruit,** ***pippala,*** **banyan trees,** ***plakṣas*****, asafetida, various herbs, betel nut trees, birch,** ***raja-pūga*** **and** ***jambu*** **trees.**

**Комментарий:**

*Hiṅgu* is a type of tree like the karañja.

### Text 19-20

*kumudotpala-kahlāra-*  
*śatapatra-vanarddhibhiḥ*  
*nalinīṣu kalaṁ kūjat-*  
*khaga-vṛndopaśobhitam*  
*mṛgaiḥ śākhāmṛgaiḥ kroḍair*  
*mṛgendrair ṛkṣa-śalyakaiḥ*  
*gavayaiḥ śarabhair vyāghrai*  
*rurubhir mahiṣādibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Kailāsa is decorated with forests of red lotuses, blue lotuses, white water lilies, and** ***śatapatra*** **lotuses growing** **in lakes, and with flocks of singing birds, deer, monkeys, boars, lions, bears, porcupines,** ***gayals,*** ***śarabhas,*** **tigers, antelope and buffalos.**

**Комментарий:**

Various animals are described: deer, bears, porcupines, gayals, śarabhas, tigers, rurus, buffalos etc.

### Text 21

*karṇāntraikapadāśvāsyair*  
*nirjuṣṭaṁ vṛka-nābhibhiḥ*  
*kadalī-khaṇḍa-saṁruddha-*  
*nalinī-pulina-śriyam*

**Перевод:**

**Kailāsa is filled with animals like** ***karṇāntra*** **(cow eared),** ***ekapada, aśvāsya*** **(horse headed), wolves and musk deer. There are attractive river banks covered with forests of banana trees.**

**Комментарий:**

*Karṇorṇaikapadāśvāsyair nirjuṣṭaṁ mṛganābhibhir* is another version. These are humans in form but almost living like animals.

### Text 22

*paryastaṁ nandayā satyāḥ*  
*snāna-puṇyatarodayā*  
*vilokya bhūteśa-giriṁ*  
*vibudhā vismayaṁ yayuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The mountain is surrounded by the Gaṅgā waters, made more sacred by the bathing of Satī. The** ***devatās,*** **seeing that mountain of Śiva with wonder, went there.**

**Комментарий:**

*Puṇya* means attractive (as well as sacred) according to Amara-koṣa. This means the water was fragrant. In another Manvantara, the Gaṅgā came in a different way.

### Text 27

*tāra-hema-mahāratna-*  
*vimāna-śata-saṅkulām*  
*juṣṭāṁ puṇyajana-strībhir*  
*yathā khaṁ sataḍid-ghanam*

**Перевод:**

**Alakā was filled with hundreds of airplanes made of gold, pearls and precious jewels and with women. It was like the sky filled with clouds and lightning.**

**Комментарий:**

The forest is described in five verses.

### Text 33

*tasmin mahā-yogamaye*  
*mumukṣu-śaraṇe surāḥ*  
*dadṛśuḥ śivam āsīnaṁ*  
*tyaktāmarṣam ivāntakam*

**Перевод:**

**Under that tree which was the shelter of aspirants for liberation, and which bestowed great powers, the** ***devatās*** **saw Śiva sitting, appearing like death, but seeming to have given up his anger.**

**Комментарий:**

Śiva is described in six verses.

### Text 34

*sanandanādyair mahā-siddhaiḥ*  
*śāntaiḥ saṁśānta-vigraham*  
*upāsyamānaṁ sakhyā ca*  
*bhartrā guhyaka-rakṣasām*

**Перевод:**

**He was peaceful, worshiped by the peaceful, perfected beings like Sananda and by Kubera, the protector of the Guhyakas and Rakṣasas.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *sanakādyair* instead of *sanandanādyair.*

### Text 36

*liṅgaṁ ca tāpasābhīṣṭaṁ*  
*bhasma-daṇḍa-jaṭājinam*  
*aṅgena sandhyābhra-rucā*  
*candra-lekhāṁ ca bibhratam*

**Перевод:**

**He was the symbol of the Śaivites, dressed in deer skin, with matted hair, staff, reddish limbs smeared with ashes, and bearing the crescent moon.**

**Комментарий:**

He was desired by the Śaivites (*tāpasābhīṣṭam*). Citsukha has *kakṣeṇa* (with concenteration in yoga) instead of *aṅgena.*

### Text 37

*upaviṣṭaṁ darbhamayyāṁ*  
*bṛsyāṁ brahma sanātanam*  
*nāradāya pravocantaṁ*  
*pṛcchate śṛṇvatāṁ satām*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva was seated on a** ***kuśa*** **mat among listening sages, and was giving instructions to Nārada who was asking about the eternal Bhāgavatam.**

**Комментарий:**

Nārada was asking about the eternal Bhāgavatam (*brahma sanātanam*).

### Text 38

*kṛtvorau dakṣiṇe savyaṁ*  
*pāda-padmaṁ ca jānuni*  
*bāhuṁ prakoṣṭhe 'kṣa-mālām*  
*āsīnaṁ tarka-mudrayā*

**Перевод:**

**His left foot was on his right thigh and his left hand was placed on his left knee. He had beads on his right forearm, and his right hand showed the** ***tarka-mudrā.***

**Комментарий:**

*Bāhu* here means hand.

### Text 40

*sa tūpalabhyāgatam ātma-yoniṁ*  
*surāsureśair abhivanditāṅghriḥ*  
*utthāya cakre śirasābhivandanam*  
*arhattamaḥ kasya yathaiva viṣṇuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Though most worthy of worship, Śiva, whose feet are worshipped, seeing Brahmā arrive with the** ***devatās*****, stood up and lowered his head in respect, just as Vāmana respected Kaśyapa.**

**Комментарий:**

Brahmā actually understands the position of Visnu very clearly:

*sattvaṁ viśuddhaṁ vasudeva-śabditaṁ*  
*yad īyate tatra pumān apāvṛtaḥ*  
*sattve ca tasmin bhagavān vāsudevo*  
*hy adhokṣajo me namasā vidhīyate*

**The pure** ***antaḥ-karaṇa*** **is called Vasudeva, because there the Supreme Lord appears without coverings. In that** ***antaḥ-karaṇa*****, I worshipped the Supreme Lord Vāsudeva, beyond the material senses, by offering respects. (SB** [**4.3.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/3.23)**)**

### Text 41

*tathāpare siddha-gaṇā maharṣibhir*  
*ye vai samantād anu nīlalohitam*  
*namaskṛtaḥ prāha śaśāṅka-śekharaṁ*  
*kṛta-praṇāmaṁ prahasann ivātmabhūḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Other great persons along with the sages, who were all around Śiva, offered respects to Brahmā, and Brahmā, smiling then spoke to Śiva, to whom he offered respects.**

**Комментарий:**

He offers respects to Śiva.

### Text 42

*brahmovāca*  
*jāne tvām īśaṁ viśvasya*  
*jagato yoni-bījayoḥ*  
*śakteḥ śivasya ca paraṁ*  
*yat tad brahma nirantaram*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā said: I know you are the Lord of the universe,** ***prakṛti*** **and** ***puruṣa,*** **that you are superior to** ***Śakti*** **and** ***Śiva,*** **and that you are also the Brahman without qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

Taking shelter of Śaiva ideas, he praises Śiva as supreme. The Śaivites accept the Lord, prakṛti and jīva as forms of the Sadāśiva. I know you are the form of Sadāśiva, Lord of everything material and spiritual, because I know you are superior to the seed and womb of the material world. The seed is Śiva and the womb is śakti. Impersonal Brahman which people think is the supreme cause I know to be you.

### Text 43

*tvam eva bhagavann etac*  
*chiva-śaktyoḥ svarūpayoḥ*  
*viśvaṁ sṛjasi pāsy atsi*  
*krīḍann ūrṇa-paṭo yathā*

**Перевод:**

**O lord! By your forms as Śiva and Śakti you create, maintain and destroy the universe just as a spider acts upon his web.**

**Комментарий:**

Śiva and śakti act as the efficient cause.

### Text 44

*tvam eva dharmārtha-dughābhipattaye*  
*dakṣeṇa sūtreṇa sasarjithādhvaram*  
*tvayaiva loke 'vasitāś ca setavo*  
*yān brāhmaṇāḥ śraddadhate dhṛta-vratāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O producer of** ***artha*** **and** ***dharma*****! You have created sacrifice, like a cloth with threads, through Dakṣa, for producing** ***artha.*** **You have created the rules of** ***varṇāśrama*** **for the people, which the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **follow with determination.**

**Комментарий:**

In his commentary Śrīdhara Svāmī refers to the threads. This means forms of scripture.

### Text 45

*tvaṁ karmaṇāṁ maṅgala maṅgalānāṁ*  
*kartuḥ sva-lokaṁ tanuṣe svaḥ paraṁ vā*  
*amaṅgalānāṁ ca tamisram ulbaṇaṁ*  
*viparyayaḥ kena tad eva kasyacit*

**Перевод:**

**Auspicious lord! You bestow Svarga or liberation to the performer of pious acts and terrible hell to the sinful. But why do you do the opposite to some people?**

**Комментарий:**

For persons who are great offenders or for persons who perform great worship, you give the opposite effects. It will be said:

*kurv adhvarasyoddharaṇaṁ hatasya bhoḥ*  
*tvayāsamāptasya mano prajāpateḥ*  
*na yatra bhāgaṁ tava bhāgino daduḥ*  
*kuyājino yena makho ninīyate*

**O Śiva! Revive the incomplete, sacrifice of Dakṣa, stopped by you, in which the bad priests did not offer you your share, so that you can receive your share. (SB** [**4.6.50**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/6.50)**)**

Instead of *tad eva* Puṇyāraṇya has *tu deva*.

### Text 46

*na vai satāṁ tvac-caraṇārpitātmanāṁ*  
*bhūteṣu sarveṣv abhipaśyatāṁ tava*  
*bhūtāni cātmany apṛthag-didṛkṣatāṁ*  
*prāyeṇa roṣo 'bhibhaved yathā paśum*

**Перевод:**

**It cannot be from anger, because anger as it arises in animals does not generally occur in those who have surrendered to your lotus feet, who see you in all living beings or just desire to see all beings as non-different from you.**

**Комментарий:**

They see all beings as non-different from Paramātmā (*ātmani)*.

### Text 47

*pṛthag-dhiyaḥ karma-dṛśo durāśayāḥ*  
*parodayenārpita-hṛd-rujo 'niśam*  
*parān duruktair vitudanty aruntudās*  
*tān māvadhīd daiva-vadhān bhavad-vidhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are sinful, seeing difference between you and all beings, seeing in terms of material benefit for themselves, who become angry at seeing the prosperity of others, always afflict others with harsh words and injure other beings. You do not have to kill them. They have already killed by the law of** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

You, who are incomparable (*bhavad-vidhaḥ*) do not have to kill them. Or even persons like you do not have to kill them, what to speak of your killing them.

### Text 48

*yasmin yadā puṣkara-nābha-māyayā*  
*durantayā spṛṣṭa-dhiyaḥ pṛthag-dṛśaḥ*  
*kurvanti tatra hy anukampayā kṛpāṁ*  
*na sādhavo daiva-balāt kṛte kramam*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever and wherever those who see difference, whose intelligence is controlled by Viṣṇu’s** ***māyā,*** **commit offenses to the devotees, the devotees show mercy to them and do not attack them, since** ***karma*** **will act upon them.**

**Комментарий:**

“Viṣṇu is the Lord. I am his devotee.” The devotees show mercy. This is explained in two verses. Even the ordinary devotees show mercy to them. By karma the punishment will be given to those who see difference.

### Text 49

*bhavāṁs tu puṁsaḥ paramasya māyayā*  
*durantayāspṛṣṭa-matiḥ samasta-dṛk*  
*tayā hatātmasv anukarma-cetaḥsv*  
*anugrahaṁ kartum ihārhasi prabho*

**Перевод:**

**O lord! Since your intelligence is not influenced by the Lord’s** ***māyā*** **and you see everything, you should be merciful to those whose minds have been conquered by** ***māyā*** **and are afflicted by various** ***karmas.***

**Комментарий:**

You are different from them (*tu*). You should be merciful.

### Text 50

*kurv adhvarasyoddharaṇaṁ hatasya bhoḥ*  
*tvayāsamāptasya mano prajāpateḥ*  
*na yatra bhāgaṁ tava bhāgino daduḥ*  
*kuyājino yena makho ninīyate*

**Перевод:**

**O Śiva! Revive the incomplete, sacrifice of Dakṣa, stopped by you, in which the bad priests did not offer you your share, so that you can receive your share.**

**Комментарий:**

Intead of *ku-yājinaḥ* Citsukha has *ku-spardhina.*

## Chapter Seven

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ity ajenānunītena*  
*bhavena parituṣyatā*  
*abhyadhāyi mahā-bāho*  
*prahasya śrūyatām iti*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: O Vidura! Pleased by the request made by Brahmā, Śiva, smiling said, “Listen to me.”**

### Text 2

*mahādeva uvāca*  
*nāghaṁ prajeśa bālānāṁ*  
*varṇaye nānucintaye*  
*deva-māyābhibhūtānāṁ*  
*daṇḍas tatra dhṛto mayā*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva said: O Brahmā! I do not speak about or think about the offenses committed by children, those controlled by the Lord’s** ***māyā*****. I punished them to teach them.**

**Комментарий:**

*Mahādeva uvāca* is not in all editions. I punish them--for their benefit, to teach them.

### Text 4

*pūṣā tu yajamānasya*  
*dadbhir jakṣatu piṣṭa-bhuk*  
*devāḥ prakṛta-sarvāṅgā*  
*ye ma uccheṣaṇaṁ daduḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Let Pūṣā eat through the teeth of the sponsor of the sacrifice, or let him eat ground meal, if he is alone. Let the** ***devatās,*** **who did not give me the best share, have their limbs restored.**

**Комментарий:**

The devatās who did not give me the first part of the sacrificial remnants, who had lost their limbs will have all their limbs restored.

### Text 19

*tadā sva-prabhayā teṣāṁ*  
*dyotayantyā diśo daśa*  
*muṣṇaṁs teja upānītas*  
*tārkṣyeṇa stotra-vājinā*

**Перевод:**

**Viṣṇu, diminishing the effulgence of the** ***devatās*** **by his effulgence which shone in the ten directions, arrived on Garuḍa, whose wings are the** ***Brhād-rathantara*** **verses.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe Viṣṇu.

### Text 20

*śyāmo hiraṇya-raśano 'rka-kirīṭa-juṣṭo*  
*nīlālaka-bhramara-maṇḍita-kuṇḍalāsyaḥ*  
*śaṅkhābja-cakra-śara-cāpa-gadāsi-carma-*  
*vyagrair hiraṇmaya-bhujair iva karṇikāraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord was dark in complexion, with a golden belt, a crown shining like the sun, having a face surrounded by locks of hair, black like bees and wearing earrings. He appeared like a lotus since he was holding the conch, lotus,** ***cakra*****, arrow, bow, club and shield in his gold ornamented hands.**

**Комментарий:**

*Vyagraiḥ* means “arms whose tips were equipped with conch, louts, cakra etc.”

### Text 24

*apy arvāg-vṛttayo yasya*  
*mahi tv ātmabhuv-ādayaḥ*  
*yathā-mati gṛṇanti sma*  
*kṛtānugraha-vigraham*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā and others, with meager senses compared to the greatness of the Lord, to the best of their ability praised the Lord who appeared by his mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

Their meager senses are shown in the following verses.

### Text 26

*dakṣa uvāca*  
*śuddhaṁ sva-dhāmny uparatākhila-buddhy-avasthaṁ*  
*cin-mātram ekam abhayaṁ pratiṣidhya māyām*  
*tiṣṭhaṁs tayaiva puruṣatvam upetya tasyām*  
*āste bhavān apariśuddha ivātma-tantraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Dakṣa said: You are one alone, fearless, pure, consciousness alone, beyond all material intelligence, devoid of** ***māyā,*** **situated in your spiritual abode*****.*** **Accepting the role of the** ***puruṣāvatāra*** **along with** ***māyā*** **within the material world, you remain independent, though you appear contaminated with material qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

It is not astonishing that we, connected with the guṇas, are overcome with māyā:

*sṛjāmi tan-niyukto ’haṁ haro harati tad-vaśaḥ \|*  
*viśvaṁ puruṣa-rūpeṇa paripāti tri-śakti-dhṛk \|\|*

**I create under his order, and Śiva destroys under his order. Holding his three energies, he protects the universe as the Paramātmā. (SB** [**2.6.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/6.32)**)**

Brahmā and Śiva are sometimes overcome, not being independent. However, you are not overcome even though you contact the guṇas. To eliminate his misconception Dakṣa speaks.

You are pure. You remain in your form as Bhagavān (*sva-dhāmni*), in the supreme Vaikuṇṭha, the manifestation of your svarūpa-śakti. Because you are śuddha-sattva, you reject all māyā.

*pravartate yatra rajas tamas tayoḥ*  
*sattvaṁ ca miśraṁ na ca kāla-vikramaḥ \|*  
*na yatra māyā kim utāpare harer*  
*anuvratā yatra surāsurārcitāḥ \|\|*

**In Vaikuṇṭha there is no** ***rajas*** **or** ***tamas*****, and no** ***sattva*** **mixed with** ***rajas*** **and** ***tamas.*** **There is no influence of time. There is no influence of** ***māyā*** **at all, what to speak of its products such as material elements. In Vaikuṇṭha the inhabitants are fully dedicated to the Lord and are worshippable by the** ***devas, asuras*** **and devotees. (SB** [**2.9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/9.10)**)**

By his svarūpa-śakti, the relationship with māyā is prevented. He remains pure, independent. By his aṁśa, he manifests the form of the puruṣa along with māyā. You appear as if impure in that māyā, your effect, by its restrictions. But this is only an appearance.

### Text 27

*ṛtvija ūcuḥ*  
*tattvaṁ na te vayam anañjana rudra-śāpāt*  
*karmaṇy avagraha-dhiyo bhagavan vidāmaḥ*  
*dharmopalakṣaṇam idaṁ trivṛd adhvarākhyaṁ*  
*jñātaṁ yad-artham adhidaivam ado vyavasthāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The priests said: O uncontaminated Lord! Because of the curse of Nandīśvara, we have become absorbed in** ***karma*** **and do not know the truth about you. We know you in the form of sacrifice, called** ***dharma*****, for attaining** ***artha, dharma*** **and** ***kāma*****, in which the result is an arrangement for the** ***devatās*** **share.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *vyavasya.*

### Text 28

*sadasyā ūcuḥ*  
*utpatty-adhvany aśaraṇa uru-kleśa-durge 'ntakogra-*  
*vyālānviṣṭe viṣaya-mṛga-tṛṣy ātma-gehoru-bhāraḥ*  
*dvandva-śvabhre khala-mṛga-bhaye śoka-dāve 'jña-sārthaḥ*  
*pādaukas te śaraṇada kadā yāti kāmopasṛṣṭaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The assembly said: Treading the road of** ***saṁsāra*** **without resting places, with holes of happiness and distress, full of fear of wild animals, subject to forest fires of lamentation, in the presence of the fearful snake of time, and dangerous with the five types of suffering, when will the ignorant people, afflicted by lust, carrying the heavy load of house and body arising from a thirst of sense objects, surrender to your lotus feet?**

**Комментарий:**

Understanding that the path of karma brings various sufferings, the assembly, giving that up, prayed for surrender to the Lord’s feet. Citsukha has *vyālākṛṣṭe* instead of *vyālānviṣṭe*.

### Text 29

*rudra uvāca*  
*tava varada varāṅghrāv āśiṣehākhilārthe*  
*hy api munibhir asaktair ādareṇārhaṇīye*  
*yadi racita-dhiyaṁ māvidya-loko 'paviddhaṁ*  
*japati na gaṇaye tat tvat-parānugraheṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva said: If the ignorant criticize me as impure when I meditate upon your precious lotus feet which are worshipped with devotion by even the detached sages, and which can bestow all benefits arising from desire, I do not consider that criticism significant, by your great mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

Śiva, revealing his difficult to understand secret, prays with bhakti. The feet give everything because people have unlimited desires (*āśiṣehākhilārthe*). Citsukha has *āśiṣamāśayādhy*e and *māṁ viddhi lokān api svān ajita* instead of *māvidya-loko 'paviddhaṁ japati.*

### Text 30

*bhṛgur uvāca*  
*yan māyayā gahanayāpahṛtātma-bodhā*  
*brahmādayas tanu-bhṛtas tamasi svapantaḥ*  
*nātman-śritaṁ tava vidanty adhunāpi tattvaṁ*  
*so 'yaṁ prasīdatu bhavān praṇatātma-bandhuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Bhṛgu said: Persons having material bodies, whose spiritual knowledge is covered by insurmountable** ***māyā*****, and who are afflicted by ignorance, even now do not speak the truth about you, the self-illuminated person. The friend of those are humble, you should forgive us.**

**Комментарий:**

Puṇyāraṇya has *nātmāśritam.*

### Text 31

*brahmovāca*  
*naitat svarūpaṁ bhavato 'sau padārtha-*  
*bheda-grahaiḥ puruṣo yāvad īkṣet*  
*jñānasya cārthasya guṇasya cāśrayo*  
*māyāmayād vyatirikto matas tvam*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā said: As long as one cannot see the form of the Lord by using material senses, one is merely under the shelter of the sense** ***devatās,*** **bodily parts and sense organs. You are different from these material factors.**

**Комментарий:**

Brahmā agrees with Bhṛgu’s words. As long as one knows things by the senses, one cannot know your svarūpa with its particular form. You are the shelter of senses, organs and sense objects.

*nātaḥ paraṁ parama yad bhavataḥ svarūpam*  
*ānanda-mātram avikalpam aviddha-varcaḥ*  
*paśyāmi viśva-sṛjam ekam aviśvam ātman*  
*bhūtendriyātmaka-madas ta upāśrito ’smi*

**O Supreme Lord! I do not see that you are different from your form as the Brahman which is without qualities and only bliss. O form of unrestricted light! I, full of pride in thinking I can get pleasure by body, sense and mind, take shelter of your one form which is spiritual, but which creates this universe. (SB** [**3.9.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/9.3)**)**

Therefore by your mercy, you reveal yourself. *Vyatirikto yatas tvam* is another version.

### Text 32

*indra uvāca*  
*idam apy acyuta viśva-bhāvanaṁ*  
*vapur ānanda-karaṁ mano-dṛśām*  
*sura-vidviṭ-kṣapaṇair udāyudhair*  
*bhuja-daṇḍair upapannam aṣṭabhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Indra said: Lord! Your body, endowed with eight arms carrying weapons to destroy the demons, is auspicious for the universe, can be perceived even by our material senses and mind, and gives us bliss.**

**Комментарий:**

Generally Viṣṇu appears with four arms. Here he appears with eight arms. Indra describes this remarkable form. Not only does the form give bliss, but by its many arms it destroys the demons.

### Text 33

*patnya ūcuḥ*  
*yajño 'yaṁ tava yajanāya kena sṛṣṭo*  
*vidhvastaḥ paśupatinādya dakṣa-kopāt*  
*taṁ nas tvaṁ śava-śayanābha-śānta-medhaṁ*  
*yajñātman nalina-rucā dṛśā punīhi*

**Перевод:**

**The wives said: This sacrifice created by Brahmā for worshipping you has been destroyed by Śiva because of his anger at Dakṣa. Lotus-navelled Lord! Lord of sacrifice! Purify with the glance of your lotus eyes this sacrifice where animal sacrifice has stopped.**

**Комментарий:**

The wives of the performers of sacrifice, their hearts melted by the glance of the Lord’s mercy, reveal their suffering.

### Text 34

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ*  
*ananvitaṁ te bhagavan viceṣṭitaṁ*  
*yad ātmanā carasi hi karma nājyase*  
*vibhūtaye yata upasedur īśvarīṁ*  
*na manyate svayam anuvartatīṁ bhavān*

**Перевод:**

**The sages said: O Lord! Your activities are astounding since you perform actions by yourself and do not become contaminated by them. Whereas others worship Lakṣmī for wealth, you do not accept her for wealth.**

**Комментарий:**

*Īśvarīm* refers to the material Lakṣmī. Citsukha has *anuvartanīm* instead of *anuvartatīm.*

### Text 35

*siddhā ūcuḥ*  
*ayaṁ tvat-kathā-mṛṣṭa-pīyūṣa-nadyāṁ*  
*mano vāraṇaḥ kleśa-dāvāgni-dagdhaḥ*  
*tṛṣārto 'vagāḍho na sasmāra dāvaṁ*  
*na niṣkrāmati brahma-sampannavan naḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The ātmārāmas said: Our minds, like an elephant burned from a forest fire and afflicted by thirst, become absorbed in the sweet, pure river of your pastimes, do not remember the fire of** ***saṁsāra*****, and do not come out of that river, as if having merged in Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Siddhas refers to ātmārāmas. Citsukha has *te* instead of *tvat.*

### Text 36

*yajamāny uvāca*  
*svāgataṁ te prasīdeśa tubhyaṁ namaḥ*  
*śrīnivāsa śriyā kāntayā trāhi naḥ*  
*tvām ṛte 'dhīśa nāṅgair makhaḥ śobhate*  
*śīrṣa-hīnaḥ ka-bandho yathā puruṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Prasūti, Dakṣa’s wife, said: O Lord! Supreme controller! Welcome to you! Be pleased with us! I offer respects to you. O Lord of Lakṣmī! With your beloved consort, please deliver us. Without your presence, the sacrifice is not auspicious, though endowed with all elements. It is like a headless body.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *yajamānā* instead of *yajamānī.*

### Text 37

*lokapālā ūcuḥ*  
*dṛṣṭaḥ kiṁ no dṛgbhir asad-grahais tvaṁ*  
*pratyag-draṣṭā dṛśyate yena viśvam*  
*māyā hy eṣā bhavadīyā hi bhūman*  
*yas tvaṁ ṣaṣṭhaḥ pañcabhir bhāsi bhūtaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The planetary protectors** **said: O Lord! You, the internal seer, by whom the universe is seen, and who can be directly seen, are not visible to us by material senses. That you appear to be made of the five elements is only your** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

The lokapālas reject the Lord being visible materially. He reveals himself. You appear to manifest by the five elements, by upādhis, not by yourself. That is your māyā. This is the understanding of people influenced by material māyā. Citsukha has *pratyag dṛṣṭyā* instead of *pratyag drāṣṭā.*

### Text 38

*yogeśvarā ūcuḥ*  
*preyān na te 'nyo 'sty amutas tvayi prabho*  
*viśvātmanīkṣen na pṛthag ya ātmanaḥ*  
*athāpi bhaktyeśa tayopadhāvatām*  
*ananya-vṛttyānugṛhāṇa vatsala*

**Перевод:**

**The masters of** ***yoga*** **said: O Lord! There is no one dearer to you than those who see the** ***jīvas*** **as non-different from you, the soul of the universe. However, O Lord, most affectionate to the devotees, please bestow pure** ***bhakti*** **on those serving you!**

**Комментарий:**

There is no one dearer than he who sees the jīvas in you, who knows the jīvas are non-different since they are your śakti, not independent. O Lord dear to the servant! Please be merciful to those who worship you by exclusive bhakti. The persons speaking are actually jñāna-bhaktas but express themselves as pure devotees.

### Text 39

*jagad-udbhava-sthiti-layeṣu daivato*  
*bahu-bhidyamāna-guṇayātma-māyayā*  
*racitātma-bheda-mataye sva-saṁsthayā*  
*vinivartita-bhrama-guṇātmane namaḥ*

**I offer respects to the Lord who creates different mentalities in the individuals by his** ***māyā*** **composes of** ***guṇas*** **divided in various ways by the** ***jīva’s karmas,*** **which will disappear when you manifest your form in the universe, and whose intelligence is completely devoid of delusion causes by the** ***guṇas*** **because of being situated in his** ***svarūpa.***

Since they identify themselves as leaders of yogīs, how can they identify themselves as servants of the Lord? The various identities in the jīvas are made by illusions of difference in the guṇas of māyā. By your manifestations, appearing and staying in the universe, by your mercy, the identities will disappear.

### Text 40

*brahmovāca*  
*namas te śrita-sattvāya*  
*dharmādīnāṁ ca sūtaye*  
*nirguṇāya ca yat-kāṣṭhāṁ*  
*nāhaṁ vedāpare 'pi ca*

**Перевод:**

**The Veda teaching** ***karma*** **said: I offer respects to you, the shelter of** ***sattva-guṇa*****, the source of** ***dharma, jñāna, vairāgya*** **and** ***aiśvarya,*** **who are beyond the material** ***guṇas,*** **and whom neither I nor the other Vedas know.**

**Комментарий:**

Brahmā here refers to śabda-brahma or Brahmā who previously spoke or a person qualified for sacrifice. When Brahmā previously spoke, Śrīdhara Svāmī indicates that Indra had more knowledge than him (and thus refuted his statement). Thus it is suitable that Brahmā speak again.

### Text 50

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ahaṁ brahmā ca śarvaś ca*  
*jagataḥ kāraṇaṁ param*  
*ātmeśvara upadraṣṭā*  
*svayan-dṛg aviśeṣaṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: I am the supreme cause of the universe, the Lord of the soul, the witness, and self-revealing. I, Brahmā and Śiva are non-different from each other.**

**Комментарий:**

He gives respect to Brahmā and Śiva to teach that they are his avatāras.

### Text 52

*tasmin brahmaṇy advitīye*  
*kevale paramātmani*  
*brahma-rudrau ca bhūtāni*  
*bhedenājño 'nupaśyati*

**Перевод:**

**The ignorant person sees difference between the** ***jīvas*** **and me, who am non-dual, absolute Brahman and Paramātmā, what to speak of seeing different between Brahmā, Śiva and me.**

**Комментарий:**

To respect Brahmā and Śiva, he says they are also īśvara. He speaks of non-difference just as the Brahmā-sūtras speak of non-difference of the Lord from the world. *Tad ananyatvam*: from Brahman arises the world which is non-different from it. *(*Brahma-sūtra [2.1.14](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/2.1.14))

Kaimutya is used to enforce the statement.

### Text 53

*yathā pumān na svāṅgeṣu*  
*śiraḥ-pāṇy-ādiṣu kvacit*  
*pārakya-buddhiṁ kurute*  
*evaṁ bhūteṣu mat-paraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My devotee does not see difference between himself and all living beings, just as a person does not differentiate between himself and his various limbs such as head and hand.**

**Комментарий:**

Since I am everything, the devotee sees himself in all beings just as a person sees his limbs in himself. This verse is connected to verse 50.

### Text 54

*trayāṇām eka-bhāvānāṁ*  
*yo na paśyati vai bhidām*  
*sarva-bhūtātmanāṁ brahman*  
*sa śāntim adhigacchati*

**Перевод:**

**O Dakṣa! The person who does not see difference between the three of us, the souls of all beings, who have one nature, attains peace.**

**Комментарий:**

One who seeks the three deities as non-different is successful. The three have the bhāva of the one puruṣa.

*pārthivād dāruṇo dhūmas tasmād agnis trayīmayaḥ \|*  
*tamasas tu rajas tasmāt sattvaṁ yad brahma-darśanam \|\|*

**Smoke is superior to dull wood, and fire, sacred to the Vedas, is superior to smoke. Similarly** ***rajas*** **is superior to** ***tamas*****, and** ***sattva*** **is superior to** ***rajas*****, since it is favorable for realizing the Lord. (SB** [**1.2.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.24)**)**

Acting according to the above functions, they are the source of all beings. They each instigate various effects.

### Text 56

*rudraṁ ca svena bhāgena*  
*hy upādhāvat samāhitaḥ*  
*karmaṇodavasānena*  
*somapān itarān api*  
*udavasya sahartvigbhiḥ*  
*sasnāv avabhṛthaṁ tataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With concentrated mind, he worshipped Śiva with his share. Worshipping other** ***devatās*** **by concluding rites, he then bathed along with the priests.**

### Text 57

*tasmā apy anubhāvena*  
*svenaivāvāpta-rādhase*  
*dharma eva matiṁ dattvā*  
*tridaśās te divaṁ yayuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**After giving Dakṣa intelligence to act according to** ***dharma*****, after he had already attained perfection by worshiping the Lord, the** ***devatās*** **departed for heaven.**

**Комментарий:**

This is connected with the previous verse.

### Text 61

*idaṁ pavitraṁ param īśa-ceṣṭitaṁ*  
*yaśasyam āyuṣyam aghaugha-marṣaṇam*  
*yo nityadākarṇya naro 'nukīrtayed*  
*dhunoty aghaṁ kaurava bhakti-bhāvataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! The person who, after constantly hearing Viṣṇu’s and Śiva’s pure activities of, which give fame and long life and destroy piles of sin, narrates the story with devotion destroys all his sin.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *bhakti-bhāvena* instead *of bhakti-bhavataḥ*.

## Chapter Eight

### Text 2

*mṛṣādharmasya bhāryāsīd*  
*dambhaṁ māyāṁ ca śatru-han*  
*asūta mithunaṁ tat tu*  
*nirṛtir jagṛhe 'prajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O killer of the enemy! Lord Brahmā’s son Adharma had a wife named Lying. She gave birth to twins called Cheating and Illusion. Nirṛti, who had no children, accepted them as his children.**

**Комментарий:**

Having described the daughters of Kardama who were sāttvika-śaktis, he describes those who were tāmasa-śaktis. Nirṛti is the presiding deity of the southwest direction.

### Text 18

*satyaṁ surucyābhihitaṁ bhavān me*  
*yad durbhagāyā udare gṛhītaḥ*  
*stanyena vṛddhaś ca vilajjate yāṁ*  
*bhāryeti vā voḍhum iḍaspatir mām*

**Перевод:**

**What Suruci said is true, because you have been held in the womb of me, an unfortunate person, and have grown up by breast milk of me, whom the King is ashamed to accept as his wife, or even as his servant.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *vā voḍhum,* Citsukha and Puṇyāraṇya have *vacoḍhum:* “to speak by words.”

### Text 20

*yasyāṅghri-padmaṁ paricarya viśva-*
