*vibhāvanāyātta-guṇābhipatteḥ*  
*ajo 'dhyatiṣṭhat khalu pārameṣṭhyaṁ*  
*padaṁ jitātma-śvasanābhivandyam*

**Перевод:**

**By worshipping the lotus feet of the Lord who is worshipped by those who control the mind and breath, and who assumes** ***sattva-guṇa*** **for maintaining the universe, Brahmā attained his supreme position.**

**Комментарий:**

Serving the lotus feet of the Lord who takes away (*ātta*) prakṛti (*guṇābhipatteḥ*) by his proximity, Brahmā attained his position. Instead of *padam*, Sambandhokti has *padyam*.

### Text 23

*nānyaṁ tataḥ padma-palāśa-locanād*  
*duḥkha-cchidaṁ te mṛgayāmi kañcana*  
*yo mṛgyate hasta-gṛhīta-padmayā*  
*śriyetarair aṅga vimṛgyamāṇayā*

**Перевод:**

**I do not find anyone who can extinguish your suffering except the lotus-eyed Lord, who is sought by Lakṣmī, who holds a lotus in her hand and who is sought by Brahmā and others.**

**Комментарий:**

He is sought by the goddess in charge of the material world, who holds a lotus in her hand. The lotus in her hand, similar to his lotus eyes, increases the Lord’s attractiveness. Or her lotus represents her desire to serve the Lord, by bestowing of all wealth.

### Text 25

*nāradas tad upākarṇya*  
*jñātvā tasya cikīrṣitam*  
*spṛṣṭvā mūrdhany agha-ghnena*  
*pāṇinā prāha vismitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada, hearing this, and thus understanding the activities of the boy, became astonished, and, touching the boy’s head with his hand which destroys all sins, then spoke to himself.**

**Комментарий:**

Nārada came there suddenly.

### Text 26

*aho tejaḥ kṣatriyāṇāṁ*  
*māna-bhaṅgam amṛṣyatām*  
*bālo 'py ayaṁ hṛdā dhatte*  
*yat samātur asad-vacaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**What desire for conquest of the** ***kṣatriyas*** **who are unable to tolerate a slight to their pride! Though only a boy, he has taken the unkind words of his stepmother seriously.**

**Комментарий:**

*Aho* indicates a welcome. It is clear he is speaking from the previous verse. *Tejas* means the desire to conquer.

### Text 28

*vikalpe vidyamāne 'pi*  
*na hy asantoṣa-hetavaḥ*  
*puṁso moham ṛte bhinnā*  
*yal loke nija-karmabhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Though opposites exist, the cause of dissatisfaction is men’s illusion only, since everywhere these causes of dissatisfaction are produced by one’s** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

Though opposites exist, the cause of dissatisfaction is nothing except illusion because these causes are not different from illusion. It is commonly said that happiness and distress are produced by karmas. These causes are also nothing except illusion.

### Text 29

*parituṣyet tatas tāta*  
*tāvan-mātreṇa pūruṣaḥ*  
*daivopasāditaṁ yāvad*  
*vīkṣyeśvara-gatiṁ budhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, understanding that** ***karma*** **is produced by the Lord’s will, the intelligent person should be satisfied with the amount of happiness and distress received through his** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

Receiving results is according to karma (*daivam*) which is in accordance with the Lord’s will. One should be satisfied by what the Lord arranges. Or *daivam* can mean the Lord himself.

### Text 32

*ato nivartatām eṣa*  
*nirbandhas tava niṣphalaḥ*  
*yatiṣyati bhavān kāle*  
*śreyasāṁ samupasthite*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore you should give up this useless endeavor. When the proper time comes, when you are older, you can attempt this.**

**Комментарий:**

Another meaning is “When the results are definite, start the practice again. You will attempt this in the near future, when the opportunity arises.

### Text 33

*yasya yad daiva-vihitaṁ*  
*sa tena sukha-duḥkhayoḥ*  
*ātmānaṁ toṣayan dehī*  
*tamasaḥ pāram ṛcchati*

**Перевод:**

**Satisfying himself amidst happiness and distress with these experiences which are given to him by** ***karma,*** **the embodied person surpasses** ***saṁsāra.***

**Комментарий:**

This is a person qualified for niṣkāma-karma-yoga.

### Text 35

*dhruva uvāca*  
*so 'yaṁ śamo bhagavatā*  
*sukha-duḥkha-hatātmanām*  
*darśitaḥ kṛpayā puṁsāṁ*  
*durdarśo 'smad-vidhais tu yaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Dhruva said: This peacefulness of mind which you have shown by mercy for persons afflicted by happiness and distress is difficult for a person like me to understand.**

**Комментарий:**

This is difficult for persons like me, even though you have shown it by your mercy.

### Text 36

*athāpi me 'vinītasya*  
*kṣāttraṁ ghoram upeyuṣaḥ*  
*surucyā durvaco-bāṇair*  
*na bhinne śrayate hṛdi*

**Перевод:**

**Since I am unsubmissive because of having an intolerant** ***kṣatriya*** **spirit, your instructions do not remain in my heart, which has been pierced by the arrows of Suruci’s harsh words.**

**Комментарий:**

*Athāpi* here means nevertheless.

### Text 38

*nūnaṁ bhavān bhagavato*  
*yo 'ṅgajaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ*  
*vitudann aṭate vīṇāṁ*  
*hitāya jagato 'rkavat*

**Перевод:**

**You, the son of Brahmā, playing a** ***vīṇā,*** **travel like the sun, for the welfare of the world.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *aṅgajaḥ* (son) sometimes *aṅkajaḥ* is seen. Playing the vīṇā, he wanders about for the benefit of the world.

### Text 39

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ity udāhṛtam ākarṇya*  
*bhagavān nāradas tadā*  
*prītaḥ pratyāha taṁ bālaṁ*  
*sad-vākyam anukampayā*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: The great Nārada, upon hearing the words of Dhruva and being pleased with the boy, out of mercy gave him good instructions.**

**Комментарий:**

He was pleased with Dhruva because of his determined faith in Nārada and his desire for the best sādhana and its goal.

### Text 42

*tat tāta gaccha bhadraṁ te*  
*yamunāyās taṭaṁ śuci*  
*puṇyaṁ madhuvanaṁ yatra*  
*sānnidhyaṁ nityadā hareḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O child! Good fortune to you! Go to pure Madhuvana on the bank of the Yamunā River, where the Lord resides eternally.**

**Комментарий:**

Nārada teaches him the method of easily worshipping the Lord out of his great mercy. He addresses him as child out of great compassion, and gives blessings to remove obstacles. The Lord resides in Madhuvana constantly by his nature not residing temporarily for some purpose like studying.

### Text 43

*snātvānusavanaṁ tasmin*  
*kālindyāḥ salile śive*  
*kṛtvocitāni nivasann*  
*ātmanaḥ kalpitāsanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**After bathing in the auspicious water of the Yamunā three times a day, you should perform necessary prayers to the Lord, and making an** ***āsana*****, sit upon it.**

**Комментарий:**

The method of meditation is described in eight verses.

### Text 44

*prāṇāyāmena tri-vṛtā*  
*prāṇendriya-mano-malam*  
*śanair vyudasyābhidhyāyen*  
*manasā guruṇā gurum*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the impurities of the mind, senses and life airs by three types of breathing, one should meditate on the Lord by the best mind.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *gurunāṁ* instead of *guruṇā.* It should be *gurūnām* but is shortened for metrical reasons.

### Text 46

*taruṇaṁ ramaṇīyāṅgam*  
*aruṇoṣṭhekṣaṇādharam*  
*praṇatāśrayaṇaṁ nṛmṇaṁ*  
*śaraṇyaṁ karuṇārṇavam*

**Перевод:**

**He has youthful, attractive limbs, red lips and eyes. He is the shelter of the surrendered, the treasure of men, and the ocean of mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

*Oṣṭha* is the upper lip. *Adhara* is the lower lip. *Nṛmnam* is used for metrical reasons.

### Text 47

*śrīvatsāṅkaṁ ghana-śyāmaṁ*  
*puruṣaṁ vana-mālinam*  
*śaṅkha-cakra-gadā-padmair*  
*abhivyakta-caturbhujam*

**Перевод:**

**He is ornamented with the Śrīvatsa, has a dark complexion, and wears a garland. He shows four arms with conch,** ***cakra,*** **club and lotus.**

**Комментарий:**

He is endowed with the qualities of Bhagavān (*puruṣam*). He has four arms famous for holding the conch and other items.

### Text 49

*kāñcī-kalāpa-paryastaṁ*  
*lasat-kāñcana-nūpuram*  
*darśanīyatamaṁ śāntaṁ*  
*mano-nayana-vardhanam*

**Перевод:**

**His waist is surrounded by small bells, and he wears shining golden anklets. He is pleasing to see, peaceful, and joyful for the mind and eyes.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *kalāpa-vinyastam.*

### Text 50

*padbhyāṁ nakha-maṇi-śreṇyā*  
*vilasadbhyāṁ samarcatām*  
*hṛt-padma-karṇikā-dhiṣṇyam*  
*ākramyātmany avasthitam*

**Перевод:**

**Entering into the lotus in the heart of the devotees by his two feet which shine with jewel-like nails, he appears in the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

He appears in the mind (*ātmanh avasthitam*).

### Text 51

*smayamānam abhidhyāyet*  
*sānurāgāvalokanam*  
*niyatenaika-bhūtena*  
*manasā varadarṣabham*

**Перевод:**

**One should with attachment meditate with great attention upon the smiling face of the Lord, the greatest benedicter, who has an affectionate glance.**

**Комментарий:**

One should contemplate in a special way (*abhidhyāyet*).

### Text 52

*evaṁ bhagavato rūpaṁ*  
*subhadraṁ dhyāyato manaḥ*  
*nirvṛtyā parayā tūrṇaṁ*  
*sampannaṁ na nivartate*

**Перевод:**

**The mind of the devotee meditating on the auspicious form of the Lord very soon becomes filled with the highest bliss and never leaves that form.**

**Комментарий:**

He never gives up meditating on that form.

### Text 53

*japaś ca paramo guhyaḥ*  
*śrūyatāṁ me nṛpātmaja*  
*yaṁ sapta-rātraṁ prapaṭhan*  
*pumān paśyati khecarān*

**Перевод:**

**O prince! Hear from me the highest secret** ***mantra,*** **by chanting which a person can see the associates of the Lord travelling in the sky in seven days.**

**Комментарий:**

Chanting the mantra softly or in the mind one can see the associates of the Lord. He taught the mantra in the right ear.

### Text 54

*oṁ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya*  
*mantreṇānena devasya*  
*kuryād dravyamayīṁ budhaḥ*  
*saparyāṁ vividhair dravyair*  
*deśa-kāla-vibhāgavit*

**Перевод:**

**The learned person who knows time and place should perform external worship using the mantra** ***oṁ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya*** **and various items.**

**Комментарий:**

Dhruva did not have upanāyanam, but he gave him the great mantra with *oṁ* since Vaiṣṇava mantras do not require dvija status to be chanted.

### Text 56

*labdhvā dravyamayīm arcāṁ*  
*kṣity-ambv-ādiṣu vārcayet*  
*ābhṛtātmā muniḥ śānto*  
*yata-vāṅ mita-vanya-bhuk*

**Перевод:**

**The peaceful sage, controlling mind and speech, eating limited amounts of what is available in the forest, should worship a form made of stone or other materials, or even in the earth or water.**

**Комментарий:**

He should worship a deity form (*arcām*). Citsukha has *āhṛta* instead of *ābhṛta* and *śānto mita vanya phalādi bhuk* instead of *śānto yata-vāṅ mita-vanya-bhuk.*

### Text 58

*paricaryā bhagavato*  
*yāvatyaḥ pūrva-sevitāḥ*  
*tā mantra-hṛdayenaiva*  
*prayuñjyān mantra-mūrtaye*

**Перевод:**

**One can offer all the Lord’s services as done by previous devotees to the Lord indicated in the** ***mantra*****, just by using the** ***mūla*****-*****mantra.***

**Комментарий:**

Pūrva-sevitā can mean “taught previously” as in verse 55. Or it can mean “conduct as performed by previous devotees.”

### Text 59-60

*evaṁ kāyena manasā*  
*vacasā ca mano-gatam*  
*paricaryamāṇo bhagavān*  
*bhaktimat-paricaryayā*  
*puṁsām amāyināṁ samyag*  
*bhajatāṁ bhāva-vardhanaḥ*  
*śreyo diśaty abhimataṁ*  
*yad dharmādiṣu dehinām*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, increaser of** ***prema*****, who is served by body, mind and words with devotional services, bestows what is best among** ***artha, dharma, kāma*** **and** ***mokṣa*** **to the sincere person worshipping him.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. The reason for giving material benefits is explained. By assistance, the Lord increases bhakti to himself (*bhāva-vardhanaḥ*).

### Text 61

*viraktaś cendriya-ratau*  
*bhakti-yogena bhūyasā*  
*taṁ nirantara-bhāvena*  
*bhajetāddhā vimuktaye*

**Перевод:**

**While being completely detached from enjoyment of the senses and liberation, one must worship the Lord constantly with profuse** ***bhakti*** **at the stage of** ***bhāva*** **to attain the position of an associate of the Lord in** ***prema.***

**Комментарий:**

Thus becoming detached from those objects one worships the Lord. One should worship the Lord for bhakti, special liberation (*vimuktaye*).

### Text 63

*tapo-vanaṁ gate tasmin*  
*praviṣṭo 'ntaḥ-puraṁ muniḥ*  
*arhitārhaṇako rājñā*  
*sukhāsīna uvāca tam*

**Перевод:**

**When Dhruva had gone to Tapovana, Nārada entered the inner chambers of the palace. Being suitably worshipped by the King, seated comfortably, he then spoke to the King.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *arhaṇāya* instead of *arhataḥ.*

### Text 66

*apy anāthaṁ vane brahman*  
*mā smādanty arbhakaṁ vṛkāḥ*  
*śrāntaṁ śayānaṁ kṣudhitaṁ*  
*parimlāna-mukhāmbujam*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! Have the wolves eaten the unprotected boy, who must be tired, hungry, with wilted lotus face, lying down in the forest?**

**Комментарий:**

Have wolves eaten the boy (*mā khādanti*)? Citsukha has *na khādanti*.

### Text 70

*maitreya uvāca*  
*iti devarṣiṇā proktaṁ*  
*viśrutya jagatī-patiḥ*  
*rāja-lakṣmīm anādṛtya*  
*putram evānvacintayat*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: After hearing what Nārada had said, the King gave up interest in his wealthy kingdom and thought only of his son.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *viśrabhya* instead of *viśrutya.*

### Text 78

*ādhāraṁ mahad-ādīnāṁ*  
*pradhāna-puruṣeśvaram*  
*brahma dhārayamāṇasya*  
*trayo lokāś cakampire*

**Перевод:**

**When Dhruva meditated on the Lord, the shelter of all the elements, the Lord of matter and the** ***jīvas,*** **the three worlds began to tremble.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is called *brahma* because he is the greatest, composed of all things. Thus he called the shelter of mahat-tattva etc. (*ādhāram mahad-ādīnām*). By becoming one with the Lord through meditation, when he trembled out of ecstasy for the Lord, the whole world trembled.

### Text 79

*yadaika-pādena sa pārthivārbhakas*  
*tasthau tad-aṅguṣṭha-nipīḍitā mahī*  
*nanāma tatrārdham ibhendra-dhiṣṭhitā*  
*tarīva savyetarataḥ pade pade*

**Перевод:**

**When the prince stood on one leg, the earth, pressed by his big toe, sank lower by a half** ***pradeśa,*** **just as a boat with an elephant rocks from side to side with each of its steps.**

**Комментарий:**

The portion of the earth lowered where he placed the weight of part of his body. That was quite suitable.

### Text 80

*tasminn abhidhyāyati viśvam ātmano*  
*dvāraṁ nirudhyāsum ananyayā dhiyā*  
*lokā nirucchvāsa-nipīḍitā bhṛśaṁ*  
*sa-loka-pālāḥ śaraṇaṁ yayur harim*

**Перевод:**

**When Dhruva, blocking all the doors of his body and stopping his life airs, meditated on the Lord with his intelligence merged in the total intelligence, the people of the universe along with the** ***devatās*****, becoming completely breathless, went to the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus the people became breathless.

## Chapter Nine

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ta evam utsanna-bhayā urukrame*  
*kṛtāvanāmāḥ prayayus tri-viṣṭapam*  
*sahasraśīrṣāpi tato garutmatā*  
*madhor vanaṁ bhṛtya-didṛkṣayā gataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Relieved of their fear, offering respects to the Lord, the** ***devatās*** **returned to heaven. By means of Garuḍa the Lord went to Madhuvana with a desire to see his servant.**

**Комментарий:**

This form of Viṣṇu is non-different from the presiding deity of Dhruva-loka.

### Text 2

*sa vai dhiyā yoga-vipāka-tīvrayā*  
*hṛt-padma-kośe sphuritaṁ taḍit-prabham*  
*tirohitaṁ sahasaivopalakṣya*  
*bahiḥ-sthitaṁ tad-avasthaṁ dadarśa*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing that the Lord, brilliant as lightning, manifested in the lotus of his heart by intense meditation of advanced** ***yoga*** **had disappeared suddenly, Dhruva then saw that form situated externally.**

**Комментарий:**

That shining form suddenly disappeared.

### Text 3

*tad-darśanenāgata-sādhvasaḥ kṣitāv*  
*avandatāṅgaṁ vinamayya daṇḍavat*  
*dṛgbhyāṁ prapaśyan prapibann ivārbhakaś*  
*cumbann ivāsyena bhujair ivāśliṣan*

**Перевод:**

**Agitated by seeing the Lord, Dhruva offered respects by falling flat on the earth. He seemed to drink the sweetness of the Lord with his eyes, kiss his feet by bowing his head, and embrace him with his arms by touching the tips of his feet.**

**Комментарий:**

“Seeming to drink the Lord’s sweetness etc.” indicates that he was completely absorbed in the Lord’s sweetness. Or just as he seemed to drink with his eyes, his lips trembled as if kissing the Lord’s feet and embrace him by bringing his arms together. Instead of *bhujaiḥ* sometimes *bhujena* is seen.

### Text 5

*sa vai tadaiva pratipāditāṁ giraṁ*  
*daivīṁ parijñāta-parātma-nirṇayaḥ*  
*taṁ bhakti-bhāvo 'bhyagṛṇād asatvaraṁ*  
*pariśrutoru-śravasaṁ dhruva-kṣitiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Having gained words to describe the Lord, understanding the difference between** ***jīva*** **and the Lord, and possessing only devotion as his goal, Dhruva, who would attain an indestructible abode, then praised the Lord whose fame is widespread.**

**Комментарий:**

He understood the difference between the ātmā and Bhagavān (*daivīm*). Citsukha has *dhruva-kṣitim.*

### Text 9

*nūnaṁ vimuṣṭa-matayas tava māyayā te*  
*ye tvāṁ bhavāpyaya-vimokṣaṇam anya-hetoḥ*  
*arcanti kalpaka-taruṁ kuṇapopabhogyam*  
*icchanti yat sparśajaṁ niraye 'pi nṝṇām*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship you, giver of freedom from birth and death, for insignificant results, as if you were a material desire tree, and, through a corpse-like body of human form, desire happiness arising from sense objects which is available to lower animals, are certainly cheated of their intelligence by your** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

Some worship you as some worship a desire tree. They desire happiness through a corpse like body. Taking food from fallen brāhmaṇas whose profession is at the burning ghat in Kurukṣetra and other places is well known.

### Text 10

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-*  
*dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt*  
*sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt*  
*kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt*

**Перевод:**

**The bliss for your servant available from meditating on your lotus feet or from hearing about your pastimes from the devotees is not available in your form of greatness known as Brahman, what to speak of the happiness for those who fall from the pleasures of Svarga.**

**Комментарий:**

The pure devotees consider the happiness of Brahman that arises from destruction of saṁsāra to be insignificant.

### Text 11

*bhaktiṁ muhuḥ pravahatāṁ tvayi me prasaṅgo*  
*bhūyād ananta mahatām amalāśayānām*  
*yenāñjasolbaṇam uru-vyasanaṁ bhavābdhiṁ*  
*neṣye bhavad-guṇa-kathāmṛta-pāna-mattaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O unlimited Lord! May I always have the association of great devotees with pure hearts who perform** ***bhakti*** **to you! By this association, intoxicated from drinking the nectar of your pastimes and qualities, I shall easily combat the ocean of material existence full of terrible suffering.**

**Комментарий:**

May I associate with great devotees who are niṣkāma *(amalāśayānām*) and perform bhakti. By that bhakti, which is accepted as the highest goal, without effort I will cross the ocean of great suffering. I will reject even thinking of liberation.

### Text 13

*tiryaṅ-naga-dvija-sarīsṛpa-deva-daitya-*  
*martyādibhiḥ paricitaṁ sad-asad-viśeṣam*  
*rūpaṁ sthaviṣṭham aja te mahad-ādy-anekaṁ*  
*nātaḥ paraṁ parama vedmi na yatra vādaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O unborn Lord! Previously I knew the variety of gross and subtle elements manifested as animals, plants, birds, snakes,** ***devatās,*** **demons, humans and total universal form composed** ***mahat-tattva*** **and other elements. O Supreme! But I did not know what was superior to that material form, and did not know the supreme form concerning which there are no proud discussions.**

**Комментарий:**

Realizing the Lord who appeared from somewhere, he laments his previous condition. Present tense of *vedmi* indicates a time near the present, or as if it were the present. I did not know for all that time. Thus I worshiped you for other goals. I did not know the supreme Lord in which there is no proud discussions which discern the existence or nonexistence of the material world.

### Text 14

*kalpānta etad akhilaṁ jaṭhareṇa gṛhṇan*  
*śete pumān sva-dṛg ananta-sakhas tad-aṅke*  
*yan-nābhi-sindhu-ruha-kāñcana-loka-padma-*  
*garbhe dyumān bhagavate praṇato 'smi tasmai*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting in his abdomen all the universe at the end of the** ***kalpa*****, the Lord, friend of Ananta, seeing only within himself, sleeps on the lap of Ananta. In the center of the golden lotus forming the planets, which rises from the lotus navel of the Lord, Brahmā appears. I offer respects to that Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

He describes that supreme form as the shelter of everything.

### Text 15

*tvaṁ nitya-mukta-pariśuddha-vibuddha ātmā*  
*kūṭa-stha ādi-puruṣo bhagavāṁs try-adhīśaḥ*  
*yad-buddhy-avasthitim akhaṇḍitayā sva-dṛṣṭyā*  
*draṣṭā sthitāv adhimakho vyatirikta āsse*

**Перевод:**

**You are different from the** ***jīva*** **because you are eternally liberated, completely pure, omniscient, pure soul, possessing one form over all time, the final cause, the** ***puruṣa*****, endowed with all good qualities, the controller of the three** ***guṇas*****, stable in intelligence, the constant seer through the** ***cit-śakti*****, the maintainer of the universe, and the controller of sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

“If I have a form, how am I different from the material forms of the universal form, by which you imagine unique qualities in that form?” This is explained in this verse.

You are eternally liberated and pure (*pariśuddha*) because your form does not arise from karma and ignorance. You are omniscient (*vibuddha*) since your form is śuddha-sattva. Your form is ātmā (not body) because your svarūpa-siddha-jñāna is not covered, but rather is full of bliss. You have a form that exists at all time (*kūṭasthaḥ*). Your form is that of the first human, since it is eternal and the form of everything, and it is your very nature. Your form is the shelter of all śaktis, since that is its nature. You are the controller of the śaktis of the three guṇas since you are non-different from your svarūpa-śakti, different from the material śaktis. Thus it is different from other forms and possessors of forms since it has much more power than prakṛti and exists everywhere.

### Text 16

*yasmin viruddha-gatayo hy aniśaṁ patanti*  
*vidyādayo vividha-śaktaya ānupūrvyāt*  
*tad brahma viśva-bhavam ekam anantam ādyam*  
*ānanda-mātram avikāram ahaṁ prapadye*

**Перевод:**

**I surrender to the one infinite, original Brahman, bliss alone, unchanging, the cause of the universe, within which are always seen successively various energies such as** ***vidyā,*** **having contrary natures.**

**Комментарий:**

You are always endowed with both śaktis. You are never without them. Because you possess all śaktis, you are the abode of contrary śaktis. The various śaktis, with high, medium and low natures, being mutually contradictory (*viruddha-gatayaḥ*), constantly perform their work (*patanti*), under your shelter (*yasmin*).

### Text 17

*satyāśiṣo hi bhagavaṁs tava pāda-padmam*  
*āśīs tathānubhajataḥ puruṣārtha-mūrteḥ*  
*apy evam arya bhagavān paripāti dīnān*  
*vāśreva vatsakam anugraha-kātaro 'smān*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You are the very form of all human goals, and are obtainable by those who worship you continually without material desire. Your lotus feet are the highest attainable blessing, surpassing material blessings like acquisition of a kingdom. O master! The Lord maintains even unfortunate persons like me, giving a small taste of those feet, since he is eager to give mercy, like a cow which nourishes and protects her calf.**

**Комментарий:**

Therefore you are the highest goal, since you have a form of the highest, complete bliss. It was rightly said:

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-*  
*dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt*  
*sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt*  
*kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt*

**The bliss for your servant available from meditating on your lotus feet or from hearing about your pastimes from the devotees is not available in your form of greatness known as Brahman, what to speak of the happiness for those who fall from the pleasures of Svarga. (SB** [**4.9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.10)**)**

Citsukha has *āśiṣṭaya. Arya* means a master or a vaiśya.

### Text 19

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*vedāhaṁ te vyavasitaṁ*  
*hṛdi rājanya-bālaka*  
*tat prayacchāmi bhadraṁ te*  
*durāpam api suvrata*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: O prince, determined in vows! I know what you resolved in your heart. All auspiciousness to you! I will award that place rarely achieved, and also** ***prema*****.**

**Комментарий:**

All auspiciousness to you! By attaining my rare abode, you will have special auspiciousness since your vow made long ago has been answered.

*padaṁ tri-bhuvanotkṛṣṭaṁ jigīṣoḥ sādhu vartma me*  
*brūhy asmat-pitṛbhir brahman anyair apy anadhiṣṭhitam*

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! Please tell the correct method for me, desiring the best position in the three worlds, which has not been attained by my forefathers or others. (SB** [**4.8.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/8.37)**)**

The Lord gives blessings. Though I give that, I also give other things rarely attained.

### Text 20-21

*nānyair adhiṣṭhitaṁ bhadra*  
*yad bhrājiṣṇu dhruva-kṣiti*  
*yatra graharkṣa-tārāṇāṁ*  
*jyotiṣāṁ cakram āhitam*  
*meḍhyāṁ go-cakravat sthāsnu*  
*parastāt kalpa-vāsinām*  
*dharmo 'gniḥ kaśyapaḥ śukro*  
*munayo ye vanaukasaḥ*  
*caranti dakṣiṇī-kṛtya*  
*bhramanto yat satārakāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Fortunate boy! I give you that radiant place which is eternal, not attained by anyone else. The zodiac of planets, constellations and stars revolve around it like oxen walking around a central pillar to grind grains. That places remains till the end of Brahmā’s life. Bharaṇi, Kṛttika, Cancer, Venus and the Big Dipper revolve around Dhruva-loka in clockwise direction along with the stars.**

**Комментарий:**

It will remain after those planets which remain till the daily destruction. The stars mentioned surround Dhruva-loka which is situated at the tip of the tail of all the constellations. This represents the heart of Viṣṇu. Being situated in the center of the stars is a representation of the position of the Lord for common people.

### Text 23

*tvad-bhrātary uttame naṣṭe*  
*mṛgayāyāṁ tu tan-manāḥ*  
*anveṣantī vanaṁ mātā*  
*dāvāgniṁ sā pravekṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**Your brother Uttama will die while hunting, and his mother, attached to him, searching for him in the forest, will enter a forest fire.**

**Комментарий:**

They will meet this end because of enmity with you, my devotee, having deep convictions of bhakti.

### Text 24

*iṣṭvā māṁ yajña-hṛdayaṁ*  
*yajñaiḥ puṣkala-dakṣiṇaiḥ*  
*bhuktvā cehāśiṣaḥ satyā*  
*ante māṁ saṁsmariṣyasi*

**Перевод:**

**After worshipping me, the heart of sacrifice by sacrifice and bountiful donations, after enjoying real blessings on earth, you will remember me at death.**

**Комментарий:**

The true blessings (*satyāḥ*) are enjoyments related to the Lord.

### Text 27

*so 'pi saṅkalpajaṁ viṣṇoḥ*  
*pāda-sevopasāditam*  
*prāpya saṅkalpa-nirvāṇaṁ*  
*nātiprīto 'bhyagāt puram*

**Перевод:**

**Though he had attained his desired goal from worshipping the feet of Viṣṇu through his determination, he returned home but he was not satisfied.**

**Комментарий:**

He attained more than his goal, since he would attain the abode of Viṣṇu (*sankalpa-nirvāṇam*). The result of his determination to attain Viṣṇu was his worship of Viṣṇu’s feet. Sometimes *saṁkalpa-jit* is seen instead of *saṁkalpa-jam*. He attained this by mercy. He was not pleased since he in ignorance had pursued another goal. Being in ignorance, he criticized his insignificant goal formed out of ignorance.

### Text 28

*vidura uvāca*  
*sudurlabhaṁ yat paramaṁ padaṁ harer*  
*māyāvinas tac-caraṇārcanārjitam*  
*labdhvāpy asiddhārtham ivaika-janmanā*  
*kathaṁ svam ātmānam amanyatārtha-vit*

**Перевод:**

**Vidura said: Having attained the supreme abode of the merciful Lord which is rarely attained, through worshipping the Lord’s feet, within one lifetime, though he was knowledgeable, why did he consider himself unfulfilled?**

**Комментарий:**

*Arthavit* means one who discriminates between the essence and everything else.

### Text 29

*maitreya uvāca*  
*mātuḥ sapatnyā vāg-bāṇair*  
*hṛdi viddhas tu tān smaran*  
*naicchan mukti-pater muktiṁ*  
*tasmāt tāpam upeyivān*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: He did not desire the liberation awarded by the giver of liberation since he remembered that his heart had been pierced by the harsh words of his step mother. From remembering his previous condition, he felt remorse.**

**Комментарий:**

Mukti here means being a servant of the Lord. *Viṣṇor anucaratvaṁ hi mokṣam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ:* the wise say that liberation means following Viṣṇu. (Padma Purāṇa) This agrees with Maitreya’s later statement in this chapter:

*na vai mukundasya padāravindayo*  
*rajo-juṣas tāta bhavādṛśā janāḥ*  
*vāñchanti tad-dāsyam ṛte 'rtham ātmano*  
*yadṛcchayā labdha-manaḥ-samṛddhayaḥ*

**O Vidura! Those like you who taste the dust from the lotus feet of Mukunda do not desire anything for themselves except service to him. Their minds are completely satisfied with whatever object comes to them by chance. (SB** [**4.9.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.36)**)**

### Text 30

*dhruva uvāca*  
*samādhinā naika-bhavena yat padaṁ*  
*viduḥ sanandādaya ūrdhva-retasaḥ*  
*māsair ahaṁ ṣaḍbhir amuṣya pādayoś*  
*chāyām upetyāpagataḥ pṛthaṅ-matiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Dhruva said: Having attained in six months the shade of the lotus feet of the Lord which Sanaka and others, strict** ***brahmacārīs,*** **do not attain by concentration in one lifetime, I fell from that position because of having other desires.**

**Комментарий:**

He expresses this regret. He had attained the shelter (*chāyām*) of the Lord’s feet, not having the shelter of devatās. His mind was not on the Lord but elsewhere (*pṛthaṅ-matiḥ*).

### Text 31

*aho bata mamānātmyaṁ*  
*manda-bhāgyasya paśyata*  
*bhava-cchidaḥ pāda-mūlaṁ*  
*gatvā yāce yad antavat*

**Перевод:**

**See the foolishness of this unfortunate person! Having attained the lotus feet of the Lord who destroys material existence, I prayed for what is the perishable.**

**Комментарий:**

The meaning is clear from the point of view of his ignorance. Though attaining his feet which cut saṁsāra, by his giving his feet to me, I asked for a temporary material goal. I have not given up that attachment. The Lord nourishes people like me (verse 17), but does not give service to his feet. Thus I have attained a kingdom, as an obstacle. One can consult Bhagavat Sandarbha for more details.

**Bhagavat Sandarbha 61:**

Vidura has said:

*sudurlabhaṁ yat paramaṁ padaṁ harer*  
*māyāvinas tac-caraṇārcanārjitam*  
*labdhvāpy asiddhārtham ivaika-janmanā*  
*kathaṁ svam ātmānam amanyatārtha-vit*

**Having attained the supreme abode of the merciful Lord which is rarely attained, through worshipping the Lord’s feet, within one lifetime, though he was knowledgeable, why did he consider himself unfulfilled? (SB** [**4.9.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.28)**)**

The Lord says to Dhruva:

*tato gantāsi mat-sthānaṁ sarva-loka-namaskṛtam*  
*upariṣṭād ṛṣibhyas tvaṁ yato nāvartate gataḥ*

**You will go to my abode, worshipped by all planets, higher than the Big Dipper, from which you will not return, having gone there. (SB** [**4.9.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.25)**)**

The associates of the Lord say:

*anāsthitaṁ te pitṛbhir anyair apy aṅga karhicit*  
*ātiṣṭha jagatāṁ vandyaṁ tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṁ padam*

**O Dhruva! Stay in that supreme abode of Viṣṇu, a place not achieved by your forefathers or any others, which is worshipped by the whole universe. (SB** [**4.12.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/12.26)**)**

*dhruvasya vaikuṇṭha-padādhirohaṇam*

**Dhruva ascended to Vaikuṇṭha. (SB** [**4.13.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/13.1)**)**

*ya eva lokānāṁ śam anubhāvayanto bhagavato viṣṇor*  
*yat paramaṁ padaṁ pradakṣiṇaṁ prakramanti.*

**Thinking of the welfare of the people, the sages rotate clockwise around Dhruvaloka, the abode of Viṣṇu. (SB** [**5.22.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/22.17)**)**

*atha tasmāt paratas trayodaśa-lakṣa-yojanāntarato*  
*yat tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṁ padam abhivadanti*

**1,300,000 yojanas above the planets of the seven sages is the place that learned scholars describe as the abode of Lord Viṣṇu. (SB** [**5.23.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/23.1)**)**

Though this place is situated in the material world, it is the abode of the Lord, with all its qualities. *Vikārāvarti ca tathā hi sthitim āha:* that abode is changeless (Brahma-sūtra [4.4.19](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/4.4.19)). One should understand that at pralaya there is destruction of the external coverings of that planet. At that time the planet becomes invisible (but is not destroyed). That is what is meant by Hiraṇyakaśipu’s statement:

*anyathedaṁ vidhāsye ’ham ayathā pūrvam ojasā*  
*kim anyaiḥ kāla-nirdhūtaiḥ kalpānte vaiṣṇavādibhiḥ*

**By dint of my severe austerities, I shall reverse the results of pious and impious activities. By my power, what was previously the rule will be opposite. What is the use of other places like Dhruvaloka, which will be destroyed by time at the end of the millennium? (SB** [**7.3.11**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/3.11)**)**

### Text 32

*matir vidūṣitā devaiḥ*  
*patadbhir asahiṣṇubhiḥ*  
*yo nārada-vacas tathyaṁ*  
*nāgrāhiṣam asattamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My intelligence was bewildered by** ***devatās*** **who had fallen lower than what I would fall and were intolerant. Thus being most corrupt, I did not accept the advice of Nārada.**

**Комментарий:**

He gives a reason for not giving up material attachment. I was bewildered by devatās who were also fallen.

### Text 33

*daivīṁ māyām upāśritya*  
*prasupta iva bhinna-dṛk*  
*tapye dvitīye 'py asati*  
*bhrātṛ-bhrātṛvya-hṛd-rujā*

**Перевод:**

**Taking shelter of the Lord’s** ***māyā,*** **seeing differences, pained in the heart by an enemy in the form of my brother, I suffer from illusory things, just like a person seeing a dream.**

**Комментарий:**

*Bhinna-dṛk* means a person whose goal is something other than the Lord. It is the goal, though not really existing and gives pain.

### Text 34

*mayaitat prārthitaṁ vyarthaṁ*  
*cikitseva gatāyuṣi*  
*prasādya jagad-ātmānaṁ*  
*tapasā duṣprasādanam*  
*bhava-cchidam ayāce 'haṁ*  
*bhavaṁ bhāgya-vivarjitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I prayed for useless things, which are like medicine given to a dead person. Having pleased the Lord who is the soul of the universe, difficult to please by austerities, and who can destroy material existence, I prayed for material enjoyment, since I was devoid of good fortune.**

**Комментарий:**

Six lines make the verse.

### Text 36

*maitreya uvāca*  
*na vai mukundasya padāravindayo*  
*rajo-juṣas tāta bhavādṛśā janāḥ*  
*vāñchanti tad-dāsyam ṛte 'rtham ātmano*  
*yadṛcchayā labdha-manaḥ-samṛddhayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: O Vidura! Those like you who taste the dust from the lotus feet of Mukunda do not desire anything for themselves except service to him. Their minds are completely satisfied with whatever object comes to them by chance.**

**Комментарий:**

Their minds are satisfied with what is attained easily—with such things as siddhis like aṇima or sārṣṭi.

### Text 42-43

*taṁ dṛṣṭvopavanābhyāśa*  
*āyāntaṁ tarasā rathāt*  
*avaruhya nṛpas tūrṇam*  
*āsādya prema-vihvalaḥ*  
*parirebhe 'ṅgajaṁ dorbhyāṁ*  
*dīrghotkaṇṭha-manāḥ śvasan*  
*viṣvaksenāṅghri-saṁsparśa-*  
*hatāśeṣāgha-bandhanam*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his son approaching near a grove, the King, longing for him for a long time, breathing heavily, immediately dismounted from his chariot, approached the boy, and overwhelmed with affection, embraced with his arms his son who had destroyed unlimited sins by the touch of the Lord’s feet.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 44

*athājighran muhur mūrdhni*  
*śītair nayana-vāribhiḥ*  
*snāpayām āsa tanayaṁ*  
*jātoddāma-manorathaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**His greatest desire fulfilled, the King smelled his son’s head again and again, and bathed him cold tears.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *snāta* instead of j*āta.* It means accomplished.

### Text 45

*abhivandya pituḥ pādāv*  
*āśīrbhiś cābhimantritaḥ*  
*nanāma mātarau śīrṣṇā*  
*sat-kṛtaḥ saj-janāgraṇīḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Bowing to his father’s feet, and receiving blessings of** ***mantras,*** **Dhruva, the foremost among good men, was welcomed. He then bowed to his two mothers with his head.**

**Комментарий:**

First he offered his head to his father’s feet and then to his mothers. He received blessings and conversed with them. He greeted them and was welcomed by them—even Suruci, since he was a devotee (*sajjanāgranīḥ*).

### Text 53

*lālyamānaṁ janair evaṁ*  
*dhruvaṁ sabhrātaraṁ nṛpaḥ*  
*āropya kariṇīṁ hṛṣṭaḥ*  
*stūyamāno 'viśat puram*

**Перевод:**

**Joyful and being praised, the King placed Dhruva, adored by the people, along with his brother, on an elephant and returned to his city.**

**Комментарий:**

Five verses describe the return.

### Text 58-59

*dhruvāya pathi dṛṣṭāya*  
*tatra tatra pura-striyaḥ*  
*siddhārthākṣata-dadhy-ambu-*  
*dūrvā-puṣpa-phalāni ca*  
*upajahruḥ prayuñjānā*  
*vātsalyād āśiṣaḥ satīḥ*  
*śṛṇvaṁs tad-valgu-gītāni*  
*prāviśad bhavanaṁ pituḥ*

**Перевод:**

**As they saw Dhruva approach on the road, the chaste women of the city scattered white mustard sees, barely, yogurt, water,** ***dūrva*** **grass, flowers and fruits, while uttering blessings out of affection. Hearing the attractive singing, Dhruva entered the house of his father.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 62

*yatra sphaṭika-kuḍyeṣu*  
*mahā-mārakateṣu ca*  
*maṇi-pradīpā ābhānti*  
*lalanā-ratna-saṁyutāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**There were jewel lamps embellished with figures of women made of jewels, shining on crystal and emerald walls.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe the place.

## Chapter Ten

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*prajāpater duhitaraṁ*  
*śiśumārasya vai dhruvaḥ*  
*upayeme bhramiṁ nāma*  
*tat-sutau kalpa-vatsarau*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: O Vidura! Dhruva married the daughter of Prajāpati Śiśumāra, whose name was Bhrami, and her two sons were Kalpa and Vatsara.**

**Комментарий:**

Uttānapada had gone to the forest before the marriage. Bhrami was a śakti of the Lord.

### Text 2

*ilāyām api bhāryāyāṁ*  
*vāyoḥ putryāṁ mahā-balaḥ*  
*putram utkala-nāmānaṁ*  
*yoṣid-ratnam ajījanat*

**Перевод:**

**In another wife, named Ilā, who was the daughter of Vāyu, he begot a son named Utkala and a very beautiful daughter.**

**Комментарий:**

He married Ilā previously.

### Text 11-12

*tataḥ parigha-nistriṁśaiḥ*  
*prāsaśūla-paraśvadhaiḥ*  
*śakty-ṛṣṭibhir bhuśuṇḍībhiś*  
*citra-vājaiḥ śarair api*  
*abhyavarṣan prakupitāḥ*  
*sarathaṁ saha-sārathim*  
*icchantas tat pratīkartum*  
*ayutānāṁ trayodaśa*

**Перевод:**

**130,000 Yakṣa soldiers, greatly angry, desiring to defeat Dhruva, showered him, along with his chariot and charioteer, with various types of feathered arrows, iron clubs, swords, tridents, lances, pikes, spears and** ***bhuśuṇḍī*** **weapons.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 14

*hāhā-kāras tadaivāsīt*  
*siddhānāṁ divi paśyatām*  
*hato 'yaṁ mānavaḥ sūryo*  
*magnaḥ puṇya-janārṇave*

**Перевод:**

**Cries of dismay arose from the Siddhas watching in the sky. “He has been surrounded. The grandson of Manu, the sun, has sunk in the ocean of the Yakṣas.”**

**Комментарий:**

Puṇyajana refers to Yakṣas and Rakṣasas. *Hataḥ* means falling into obstacles as well as being killed, according to Amara-koṣa. Citsukha has *hanta* instead of *hataḥ*.

### Text 15

*nadatsu yātudhāneṣu*  
*jaya-kāśiṣv atho mṛdhe*  
*udatiṣṭhad rathas tasya*  
*nīhārād iva bhāskaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**While the Yakṣas were proclaiming victory, suddenly Dhruva’s chariot appeared in the battlefield, like the sun appearing through the mist.**

**Комментарий:**

He overcame them by his śakti, just as the sun overcomes the mist.

### Text 18-19

*bhallaiḥ sañchidyamānānāṁ*  
*śirobhiś cāru-kuṇḍalaiḥ*  
*ūrubhir hema-tālābhair*  
*dorbhir valaya-valgubhiḥ*  
*hāra-keyūra-mukuṭair*  
*uṣṇīṣaiś ca mahā-dhanaiḥ*  
*āstṛtās tā raṇa-bhuvo*  
*rejur vīra-mano-harāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The battlefields, attractive to minds of heroes, were covered with Yakṣas’ heads severed by his arrows, as well as with beautiful earrings, with thighs like golden palm trees, with arms, armbands, crowns, necklaces and bracelets and valuable turbans.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 20

*hatāvaśiṣṭā itare raṇājirād*  
*rakṣo-gaṇāḥ kṣatriya-varya-sāyakaiḥ*  
*prāyo vivṛkṇāvayavā vidudruvur*  
*mṛgendra-vikrīḍita-yūthapā iva*

**Перевод:**

**The remaining Yakṣas who were not killed, their limbs almost severed by Dhruva’s arrows, fled from the battlefield like herd of elephants who had become the plaything of a lion**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *vikrīḍita* sometimes *drāvita* is seen.

### Text 22

*iti bruvaṁś citra-rathaḥ sva-sārathiṁ*  
*yattaḥ pareṣāṁ pratiyoga-śaṅkitaḥ*  
*śuśrāva śabdaṁ jaladher iveritaṁ*  
*nabhasvato dikṣu rajo 'nvadṛśyata*

**Перевод:**

**Speaking to his charioteer in this way, cautious and worrying about a counterattack by the enemy, Dhruva then heard a sound which seemed to come from the ocean. He then saw dust in all directions caused by the wind.**

**Комментарий:**

Citraratha is a name of Dhruva. Gauḍapāda has *vyadṛśyata* instead of *anvadṛśyata.*

### Text 23

*kṣaṇenācchāditaṁ vyoma*  
*ghanānīkena sarvataḥ*  
*visphurat-taḍitā dikṣu*  
*trāsayat-stanayitnunā*

**Перевод:**

**In a moment the sky became completely covered with a mass of clouds, with flashing lightning and terrifying thunder.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *churitam* instead of *kṣaṇena.*

### Text 29

*dhruve prayuktām asurais*  
*tāṁ māyām atidustarām*  
*niśamya tasya munayaḥ*  
*śam āśaṁsan samāgatāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that the Yakṣas had cast their magic, difficult to overcome, upon Dhruva, the sages arrived and prayed for his well being.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *sam āśaṁsan* instead of *śam āśaṁsan.*

## Chapter Eleven

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*niśamya gadatām evam*  
*ṛṣīṇāṁ dhanuṣi dhruvaḥ*  
*sandadhe 'stram upaspṛśya*  
*yan nārāyaṇa-nirmitam*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Here the words spoken by the sages, Dhruva**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti does not have this chapter.

### Text 3

*tasyārṣāstraṁ dhanuṣi prayuñjataḥ*  
*suvarṇa-puṅkhāḥ kalahaṁsa-vāsasaḥ*  
*viniḥsṛtā āviviśur dviṣad-balaṁ*  
*yathā vanaṁ bhīma-ravāḥ śikhaṇḍinaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When Dhruva fixed the weapon on his bow, golden arrows with feathers from swans fired out upon the enemy like the frightening sound of peacocks in the forest.**

**Комментарий:**

The sound of peacocks describes the sound of his arrows.

### Text 5

*sa tān pṛṣatkair abhidhāvato mṛdhe*  
*nikṛtta-bāhūru-śirodharodarān*  
*nināya lokaṁ param arka-maṇḍalaṁ*  
*vrajanti nirbhidya yam ūrdhva-retasaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Attacked by the Yakṣas, Dhruva cut off there their arms, thighs, necks and torsos with his arrows and sent them to planet where the** ***brahmacārīs*** **go after attaining the sun.**

**Комментарий:**

Their destination was Brahma-loka (*lokam param*).

### Text 12

*sa tvaṁ harer anudhyātas*  
*tat-puṁsām api sammataḥ*  
*kathaṁ tv avadyaṁ kṛtavān*  
*anuśikṣan satāṁ vratam*

**Перевод:**

**Always remembering the Lord, you have been recognized by the great devotees. You teach others the conduct of a saintly person. Why then do you act in this shameful way?**

**Комментарий:**

You worship the Lord but take the consent of the devotees in doing anything, being without pride. Ambarīṣa is described as *yathottamaśloka-janāśrayā ratiḥ*: he was attached to serving the devotees. (SB [9.4.20](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/4.18))

### Text 13

*titikṣayā karuṇayā*  
*maitryā cākhila-jantuṣu*  
*samatvena ca sarvātmā*  
*bhagavān samprasīdati*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord, the soul of all beings, is pleased when a devotee is tolerant of superiors, merciful to inferiors, friendly towards equals, and takes others’ happiness and distress as his own.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotee should be tolerant of enemies, merciful to those lacking and friendly to equals. He treats all beings as himself in their happiness or suffering.

### Text 14

*samprasanne bhagavati*  
*puruṣaḥ prākṛtair guṇaiḥ*  
*vimukto jīva-nirmukto*  
*brahma nirvāṇam ṛcchati*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord is satisfied, a person becomes free from the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*****, is free from the subtle body, and attains the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

He attains happiness of Bhagavān which includes the happiness of Brahman *(brahma-nirvāṇam*). There can be no other meaning since *nirvāṇa* by normal meaning cannot have any modifiers.

### Text 18

*sa khalv idaṁ bhagavān kāla-śaktyā*  
*guṇa-pravāheṇa vibhakta-vīryaḥ*  
*karoty akartaiva nihanty ahantā*  
*ceṣṭā vibhūmnaḥ khalu durvibhāvyā*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, acting as the** ***jīvas*** **entering into** ***māyā,*** **separated from himself by the agitation of** ***guṇas*** **through time, acts and does not act. He kills and does not kill. The actions of the Lord are inconceivable.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord operates through his śaktis. Thus though he is the doer, he cannot be understood in these actions, since he is also the non-doer.

### Text 20

*na vai sva-pakṣo 'sya vipakṣa eva vā*  
*parasya mṛtyor viśataḥ samaṁ prajāḥ*  
*taṁ dhāvamānam anudhāvanty anīśā*  
*yathā rajāṁsy anilaṁ bhūta-saṅghāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord has no friend or enemy. He enters all beings as death equally. The** ***jīvas,*** **dependent on** ***karma*** **and associated with matter, follow the Lord as he moves along, just like particles of dust follow the currents of the air.**

**Комментарий:**

The comparison is made based on similarity of dependence. The jīvas, dependent on the Lord and karma, move about as the dust particles move about in the wind. Citsukha has *bhūta-saṅgham.*

### Text 21

*āyuṣo 'pacayaṁ jantos*  
*tathaivopacayaṁ vibhuḥ*  
*ubhābhyāṁ rahitaḥ sva-stho*  
*duḥsthasya vidadhāty asau*

**Перевод:**

**Living beings have short or long lives. The Lord is free of both. He awards these results to those dependent on** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is absorbed in his own bliss (*sva-sthaḥ*). He is independent, not created by any other being. Thus he has no partiality or hatred towards any being.

### Text 22

*kecit karma vadanty enaṁ*  
*svabhāvam apare nṛpa*  
*eke kālaṁ pare daivaṁ*  
*puṁsaḥ kāmam utāpare*

**Перевод:**

**Some say it is** ***karma,*** **some say it is the nature of matter, some say it is time, some say it is planets and some say it is lust.**

**Комментарий:**

Some say it is karma. They ascribe the qualities like doership (giving short or long life) to karma etc. *Daivam* means planets. *Kāmam* means determination. This is the opinion of *dṛṣti-sṛṣṭivādis.*

### Text 23

*avyaktasyāprameyasya*  
*nānā-śakty-udayasya ca*  
*na vai cikīrṣitaṁ tāta*  
*ko vedātha sva-sambhavam*

**Перевод:**

**O child! No one knows the plan of the Lord who is invisible and unfathomable, from whom numerous energies arise, and from whom you are born.**

**Комментарий:**

His desire to do action arises from a portion of his icchā-śakti. But he is the shelter of all śaktis.

### Text 26

*eṣa bhūtāni bhūtātmā*  
*bhūteśo bhūta-bhāvanaḥ*  
*sva-śaktyā māyayā yuktaḥ*  
*sṛjaty atti ca pāti ca*

**Перевод:**

**The soul in all beings, the controller of all beings, the maintainer of all beings, using his energy of** ***māyā,*** **creates, maintains and destroys.**

**Комментарий:**

How does the Lord create? That is explained. He is the cause of the living beings (*bhūtasya bhūtātmā*). He is the controller of them (*bhūteśaḥ*). He has them attain certain forms (*bhūta-bhāvanaḥ*), since he is endowed with his svarūpa (*śaktyā*) and the external energy (*māyayā*). This includes ahaṅkāra.

### Text 28

*yaḥ pañca-varṣo jananīṁ tvaṁ vihāya*  
*mātuḥ sapatnyā vacasā bhinna-marmā*  
*vanaṁ gatas tapasā pratyag-akṣam*  
*ārādhya lebhe mūrdhni padaṁ tri-lokyāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up your mother when you were five years old, wounded by the words of your step mother, you went to the forest. Worshipping the Lord by austerities, you achieved the topmost position in the three worlds.**

**Комментарий:**

You who attained the topmost position, surrender to the Lord (*upehi* from previous verse).

### Text 29

*tam enam aṅgātmani mukta-vigrahe*  
*vyapāśritaṁ nirguṇam ekam akṣaram*  
*ātmānam anviccha vimuktam ātma-dṛg*  
*yasminn idaṁ bhedam asat pratīyate*

**Перевод:**

**Seek out in your mind devoid of hostility the one, indestructible, eternally liberated Lord beyond the** ***guṇas,*** **who resides within you out of affection, on seeing whom, the world filled with dualities will appear disgusting.**

**Комментарий:**

Seek out Paramātmā (*ātmānam*). When he manifests (*yasmin*), the world of dualities will be recognized or experienced as not being the puruṣārtha. Citsukha has *pratīkṣate*. The world is rejected because one cannot tolerate its contrivance.

### Text 33

*helanaṁ giriśa-bhrātur*  
*dhanadasya tvayā kṛtam*  
*yaj jaghnivān puṇya-janān*  
*bhrātṛ-ghnān ity amarṣitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Because you have in anger killed many Yakṣas, killers of your brother, you have offended Kuvera, the brother of Śiva.**

**Комментарий:**

In killing the Yakṣas you have offended (*helanam katam*) Kuvera. Śrīdhara Svāmī has *bhrātṛ-ghnā.*

### Text 34

*taṁ prasādaya vatsāśu*  
*sannatyā praśrayoktibhiḥ*  
*na yāvan mahatāṁ tejaḥ*  
*kulaṁ no 'bhibhaviṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**My son! Satisfy Kuvera immediately by offering respects, humble behavior, and kind words, so that the anger of great persons will not defeat our family.**

**Комментарий:**

*Mahatām* is in the plural to show respect to Śiva.

## Chapter Twleve

### Text 2

*bho bhoḥ kṣatriya-dāyāda*  
*parituṣṭo 'smi te 'nagha*  
*yat tvaṁ pitāmahādeśād*  
*vairaṁ dustyajam atyajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O son of a warrior! Sinless one! I am pleased with you because you have given up hatred, difficult to give up, on the instruction of your grandfather.**

**Комментарий:**

The statement is uttered by Kuvera with identity as a devatā.

### Text 4

*ahaṁ tvam ity apārthā dhīr*  
*ajñānāt puruṣasya hi*  
*svāpnīvābhāty atad-dhyānād*  
*yayā bandha-viparyayau*

**Перевод:**

**From ignorance, from absorption in the body, man’s intelligence, thinking of I and mine like a dreamer, by which bondage and suffering arise, becomes useless.**

**Комментарий:**

Thinking of me and you from identity with upādhis *(ajñānāt*), intelligence becomes useless (*apārthā*). Citsukha has *anudhyānāt* instead of *atad-dhyānāt.*

### Text 5

*tad gaccha dhruva bhadraṁ te*  
*bhagavantam adhokṣajam*  
*sarva-bhūtātma-bhāvena*  
*sarva-bhūtātma-vigraham*  
*bhajasva bhajanīyāṅghrim*  
*abhavāya bhava-cchidam*  
*yuktaṁ virahitaṁ śaktyā*  
*guṇa-mayyātma-māyayā*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore go, Dhruva. Good fortune to you. To attain the Lord who has no material existence, while thinking all beings as equal to oneself, worship the Lord, beyond the senses, who is the form of all beings, the destroyer of** ***saṁsāra*****, whose feet are worthy of worship, and who, though endowed with the material** ***śakti*** **composed of** ***guṇas*****, is unaffected by it.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Worship the Lord who is the form (*ātmā*) of all beings, since he is the highest form. He is endowed with his chief śakti and is devoid of the touch of his māyā which is dependent on him, which takes shelter of him.

### Text 8

*maitreya uvāca*  
*sa rāja-rājena varāya codito*  
*dhruvo mahā-bhāgavato mahā-matiḥ*  
*harau sa vavre 'calitāṁ smṛtiṁ yayā*  
*taraty ayatnena duratyayaṁ tamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: When Kuvera requested him to ask for a benediction, the great devotee Dhruva, of high intelligence, asked that he should have fixed remembrance of the Lord, by which he could cross the insurmountable ignorance of material existence easily.**

**Комментарий:**

Being a great devotee, he asked for fixed remembrance of the Lord. Manu had said:

*taṁ prasādaya vatsāśu sannatyā praśrayoktibhiḥ*  
*na yāvan mahatāṁ tejaḥ kulaṁ no 'bhibhaviṣyati*

**My son! Satisfy Kuvera immediately by offering respects, humble behavior, and kind words, so that the anger of great persons will not defeat our family. (SB** [**4.11.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/11.34)**)**

He thought there could be some obstacle to remembering the Lord because of this.

### Text 10

*athāyajata yajñeśaṁ*  
*kratubhir bhūri-dakṣiṇaiḥ*  
*dravya-kriyā-devatānāṁ*  
*karma karma-phala-pradam*

**Перевод:**

**By sacrifices and profuse charities he then worshipped the Lord of sacrifice, who facilitates actions related to the materials, ceremonies and** ***devatās*** **and who gives the results of sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord facilitates actions related to materials, rites and devatās of the sacrifice.

### Text 13

*ṣaṭ-triṁśad-varṣa-sāhasraṁ*  
*śaśāsa kṣiti-maṇḍalam*  
*bhogaiḥ puṇya-kṣayaṁ kurvann*  
*abhogair aśubha-kṣayam*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring to decrease his pious results by enjoyment and destroy his sinful reactions by austerity, he ruled the earth for thirty-six thousand years.**

**Комментарий:**

This statement about decreasing pious results and destroying sinful reactions is just an appearance to imitate ordinary people.

### Text 14-15

*evaṁ bahu-savaṁ kālaṁ*  
*mahātmāvicalendriyaḥ*  
*tri-vargaupayikaṁ nītvā*  
*putrāyādān nṛpāsanam*  
*manyamāna idaṁ viśvaṁ*  
*māyā-racitam ātmani*  
*avidyā-racita-svapna-*  
*gandharva-nagaropamam*

**Перевод:**

**Passing many years suitable for enjoyment of** ***artha, dharma*** **and** ***kāma,*** **with undisturbed senses, the great devotee Dhruva, realizing that this universe made by** ***māyā*** **is like a Gandharva city manufactured in a dream caused by ignorance in oneself, handed over the throne to his son.**

**Комментарий:**

He had senses which were devoid of change (*avicalendriyaḥ*). The universe is covered with māyā (*māyayā racitam*).

### Text 16

*ātma-stry-apatya-suhṛdo balam ṛddha-kośam*  
*antaḥ-puraṁ parivihāra-bhuvaś ca ramyāḥ*  
*bhū-maṇḍalaṁ jaladhi-mekhalam ākalayya*  
*kālopasṛṣṭam iti sa prayayau viśālām*

**Перевод:**

**Considering that body, wives, children, friends, military power, treasury, female quarters, beautiful parks, and the whole area surrounded by the oceans, would all be destroyed by time, he went to Badarikāśrama.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha as *ātmā-svapatya.*

### Text 23

*sunanda-nandāv ūcatuḥ*  
*bho bho rājan subhadraṁ te*  
*vācaṁ no 'vahitaḥ śṛṇu*  
*yaḥ pañca-varṣas tapasā*  
*bhavān devam atītṛpat*

**Перевод:**

**Sunanda and Nanda said: O King! All good fortune to you, who pleased the Lord by austerity when you were five years old! Attentively hear our words.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse has five lines.

### Text 24

*tasyākhila-jagad-dhātur*  
*āvāṁ devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ*  
*pārṣadāv iha samprāptau*  
*netuṁ tvāṁ bhagavat-padam*

**Перевод:**

**We, associates of the Supreme Lord, creator of all the universes and holder of the Śārṅga bow, have come to take you to the abode of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

They speak in two verses.

### Text 25

*sudurjayaṁ viṣṇu-padaṁ jitaṁ tvayā*  
*yat sūrayo 'prāpya vicakṣate param*  
*ātiṣṭha tac candra-divākarādayo*  
*graharkṣa-tārāḥ pariyanti dakṣiṇam*

**Перевод:**

**You have attained the planet of Viṣṇu, difficult to achieve, which the** ***devatās*** **see but cannot attain. Stay there. The moon, sun and other planets, the constellations and stars circumambulate that supreme place clockwise.**

**Комментарий:**

You have attained the planet of Viṣṇu, which devatās seek but do not attain since it is a form of param brahma (*param*). The planets move clockwise from the point of view of being on Dhruva-loka, though the whole sky moves counterclockwise from our point of view. The zodiac and planets rotate but Dhruva’s planet does not.

### Text 29

*parītyābhyarcya dhiṣṇyāgryaṁ*  
*pārṣadāv abhivandya ca*  
*iyeṣa tad adhiṣṭhātuṁ*  
*bibhrad rūpaṁ hiraṇmayam*

**Перевод:**

**Circumambulating and worshipping the plane, offering respects to the two associates, assuming a golden form, he desired to board the plane.**

**Комментарий:**

He received a special, shining śuddha-sattva form (*hiraṇmayam*).

### Text 33

*iti vyavasitaṁ tasya*  
*vyavasāya surottamau*  
*darśayām āsatur devīṁ*  
*puro yānena gacchatīm*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding the desire of Dhruva, the two associates of the Lord showed him that his mother was also going by an airplane in front of him.**

**Комментарий:**

She also had a shining spiritual form *(devīm*), like Dhruva.

### Text 41

*nārada uvāca*  
*nūnaṁ sunīteḥ pati-devatāyās*  
*tapaḥ-prabhāvasya sutasya tāṁ gatim*  
*dṛṣṭvābhyupāyān api veda-vādino*  
*naivādhigantuṁ prabhavanti kiṁ nṛpāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: Followers of the Vedas, what to speak of kings, seeing the goal attained by chaste Sunīti’s son who severe austerity incarnate, could not obtain the method by which he attained the goal, what to speak of the attainment.**

**Комментарий:**

Though they saw the goal, even great brahmarṣis who discussed the Vedas could not understand the secret method of Dhruva, what to speak of kings. Citsukha has *dṛṣṭvābhyupāyāti.*

### Text 42

*yaḥ pañca-varṣo guru-dāra-vāk-śarair*  
*bhinnena yāto hṛdayena dūyatā*  
*vanaṁ mad-ādeśa-karo 'jitaṁ prabhuṁ*  
*jigāya tad-bhakta-guṇaiḥ parājitam*

**Перевод:**

**When five years old he went to the forest, because his heart was burned, pierced by the sharp words of his step mother, and following my instructions, he conquered the unconquerable Lord who is conquered by the qualities of his devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *yaḥ* sometimes *kaḥ* is seen. This is accepted by Citsukha but not by Śrīdhara Svāmī.

### Text 44

*maitreya uvāca*  
*etat te 'bhihitaṁ sarvaṁ*  
*yat pṛṣṭo 'ham iha tvayā*  
*dhruvasyoddāma-yaśasaś*  
*caritaṁ sammataṁ satām*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: As you have asked, I have explained to you everything about the activities of most famous Dhruva, which are celebrated by the devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

He had asked about the activities of Dhruva.

### Text 48

*prayataḥ kīrtayet prātaḥ*  
*samavāye dvi-janmanām*  
*sāyaṁ ca puṇya-ślokasya*  
*dhruvasya caritaṁ mahat*

**Перевод:**

**One should recite this great story of famous Dhruva attentively in the morning and in the evening in the company of** ***brāhmaṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

These verses praise the story of Dhruva.

### Text 51

*jñānam ajñāta-tattvāya*  
*yo dadyāt sat-pathe 'mṛtam*  
*kṛpālor dīna-nāthasya*  
*devās tasyānugṛhṇate*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***devatās*** **do not give obstacles to the merciful protector of the wretched, who gives immortal knowledge to the ignorant on the path of truth.**

**Комментарий:**

*Sa-tpathe* means giving to persons on the spiritual path. Sometimes *yaḥ* is missing.

## Chapter Thirteen

### Text 1

*sūta uvāca*  
*niśamya kauṣāraviṇopavarṇitaṁ*  
*dhruvasya vaikuṇṭha-padādhirohaṇam*  
*prarūḍha-bhāvo bhagavaty adhokṣaje*  
*praṣṭuṁ punas taṁ viduraḥ pracakrame*

**Перевод:**

**Sūta said: After hearing about the ascent of Dhruva to the Lord’s abode from Maitreya, Vidura, his devotion to the Lord increasing, began to inquire from Maitreya again.**

**Комментарий:**

Sūta speaks the words of Śukadeva.

### Text 3

*manye mahā-bhāgavataṁ*  
*nāradaṁ deva-darśanam*  
*yena proktaḥ kriyā-yogaḥ*  
*paricaryā-vidhir hareḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I know Nārada is the great devotee who saw the Lord. He spoke the Pañcarātra scripture concerning service to the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

I know Nārada is the guru of the Vaiṣṇava sampradaya (*mahā-bhāgavatam*) who knows about Bhagavān (*deva-darśanam*) and who saw the all auspicious Lord. Or heis the cause of realizing the Lord for person to whom he shows mercy.

### Text 4

*sva-dharma-śīlaiḥ puruṣair*  
*bhagavān yajña-pūruṣaḥ*  
*ijyamāno bhaktimatā*  
*nāradeneritaḥ kila*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord enjoyer of sacrifice, worshipped by the Pracetas, who performed their duty, was praised by devoted Nārada.**

**Комментарий:**

In the same way he was praised by Nārada.

### Text 7

*sa janmanopaśāntātmā*  
*niḥsaṅgaḥ sama-darśanaḥ*  
*dadarśa loke vitatam*  
*ātmānaṁ lokam ātmani*

**Перевод:**

**From his birth Utkala was peaceful, without attachments, and saw all beings as his self. He saw the Lord pervading everything in the world and the whole world within the Lord.**

### Text 8-9

*ātmānaṁ brahma nirvāṇaṁ*  
*pratyastamita-vigraham*  
*avabodha-rasaikātmyam*  
*ānandam anusantatam*  
*avyavacchinna-yogāgni-*  
*dagdha-karma-malāśayaḥ*  
*svarūpam avarundhāno*  
*nātmano 'nyaṁ tadaikṣata*

**Перевод:**

**Realizing the** ***jīva*** **as Brahman, which was peaceful, devoid of argument, pure knowledge, bliss, and all-pervading, and having burned up all contamination of** ***karma*** **in his heart by the continuous fire of** ***yoga*****, Utkala saw nothing except the pure** ***jīva.***

**Комментарий:**

First he saw Brahman with upādhis (*ātmānam vitatam loke*). Then he realized Brhaman without upādhis (*prastyastamitam vigraham*). It was spread everywhere (*anusantatam*). It was the supreme cause (*svarūpam*). He saw nothing other than Brahman, since Brahman was pure like the jīva. This was because he searched only for the non-different aspect of the Lord.

### Text 15-16

*sa cakṣuḥ sutam ākūtyāṁ*  
*patnyāṁ manum avāpa ha*  
*manor asūta mahiṣī*  
*virajān naḍvalā sutān*  
*puruṁ kutsaṁ tritaṁ dyumnaṁ*  
*satyavantam ṛtaṁ vratam*  
*agniṣṭomam atīrātraṁ*  
*pradyumnaṁ śibim ulmukam*

**Перевод:**

**Sarvatejā, also called Cakṣu, begot a son named Cākṣuṣa Manu in his wife Ākūti. Naḍvalā, the queen of Cākṣuṣa Manu, gave birth to sons without passion: Puru, Kutsa, Trita, Dyumna, Satyavān, Ṛta, Vrata, Agniṣṭoma, Atīrātra, Pradyumna, Śibi and Ulmuka.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Another name of Caksu was Sarvatejā. He begot a son named Cakṣuṣa who became Manu.

*ṣaṣṭhaś ca cakṣuṣaḥ putraś cākṣuṣo nāma vai manuḥ*  
*pūru-pūruṣa-sudyumna- ramukhāś cākṣuṣātmajāḥ*

**The son of Cakṣu known as Cākṣuṣa was the sixth Manu. He had many sons, headed by Pūru, Pūruṣa and Sudyumna. (SB** [**8.5.7**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-8/5.7)**)**

His queen begot sons having no passion. Citsukha has *vairājam.*

### Text 22

*kiṁ vāṁho vena uddiśya*  
*brahma-daṇḍam ayūyujan*  
*daṇḍa-vrata-dhare rājñi*  
*munayo dharma-kovidāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Why did the sages, conversant with** ***dharm*****a, understanding their act would be sinful, punish and curse Vena, the King who is responsible for punishing others?**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha does not accept the version *veṇam uddiśya*.

### Text 25

*maitreya uvāca*  
*aṅgo 'śvamedhaṁ rājarṣir*  
*ājahāra mahā-kratum*  
*nājagmur devatās tasminn*  
*āhūtā brahma-vādibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Saintly King Aṅga performed a great horse sacrifice, but the** ***devatās*** **called by the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **did not come there.**

**Комментарий:**

Though because of a great offense in a previous life he had no son, a son was born, because of the power of a sacrifice to Viṣṇu. But that good fortune was spoiled by a bad son. This was the cause of the king’s renunciation. His grandson however was an avatāra.

### Text 27

*rājan havīṁṣy aduṣṭāni*  
*śraddhayāsāditāni te*  
*chandāṁsy ayāta-yāmāni*  
*yojitāni dhṛta-vrataiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! The offerings are not contaminated and were collected with faith. The** ***mantras*** **are not weak and were chanted by dedicated** ***brāhmaṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

The ingredients were all proper. However the devatās did not accept it. (verse 28)

### Text 31

*sadasas-pataya ūcuḥ*  
*nara-deveha bhavato*  
*nāghaṁ tāvan manāk sthitam*  
*asty ekaṁ prāktanam aghaṁ*  
*yad ihedṛk tvam aprajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The leaders of the assembly said: O King! In this life you do not have the slightest sin. There is one sin from previous lives by which you, though sinless now, are without a son in this life.**

**Комментарий:**

O king! You do not have any sin. But from a previous life there is one sin or offense. Citsukha has *avadyam* instead of *agham.*

### Text 43

*prāyeṇābhyarcito devo*  
*ye 'prajā gṛha-medhinaḥ*  
*kad-apatya-bhṛtaṁ duḥkhaṁ*  
*ye na vindanti durbharam*

**Перевод:**

**Generally, the Lord must have been worshipped by persons without sons, since they do not have to suffer the intolerable grief caused by a bad son.**

**Комментарий:**

This is a statement uttered out of grief only.

### Text 44

*yataḥ pāpīyasī kīrtir*  
*adharmaś ca mahān nṛṇām*  
*yato virodhaḥ sarveṣāṁ*  
*yata ādhir anantakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Because of a bad son, sinful reputation and great irreligion arise. From a bad son arises quarrel among everyone. From a bad son arises endless anxiety.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses describe the faults of household life.

## Chapter Fourteen

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*bhṛgv-ādayas te munayo*  
*lokānāṁ kṣema-darśinaḥ*  
*goptary asati vai nṝṇāṁ*  
*paśyantaḥ paśu-sāmyatām*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Bhṛgu and other sages, seers of the people’s welfare, saw that in the absence of a king men would become like animals.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 4

*sa ārūḍha-nṛpa-sthāna*  
*unnaddho 'ṣṭa-vibhūtibhiḥ*  
*avamene mahā-bhāgān*  
*stabdhaḥ sambhāvitaḥ svataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Vena, on ascending the throne, became proud because the eight powers, and proudly considered himself worthy of worship. Thus he disrespected exalted people.**

**Комментарий:**

He considered himself worthy of worship: I am brave. I am learned. Citsukha has *ātmā-sambhāvitaḥ* instead of *samvāvitaḥ svataḥ.*

### Text 5

*evam mada-andhaḥ utsiktaḥ*  
*niraṅkuśa iva dvipaḥ*  
*paryaṭan ratham āsthāya*  
*kampayann iva rodasī*

**Перевод:**

**Blind, intoxicated, proud and uncontrollable, like and elephant, he traveled on his chariot around the earth, causing the earth and sky to tremble.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses describe his sinful nature.

### Text 7

*venasyāvekṣya munayo*  
*durvṛttasya viceṣṭitam*  
*vimṛśya loka-vyasanaṁ*  
*kṛpayocuḥ sma satriṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the activities of sinful Vena and considering the danger to the people, the sages, with compassion, spoke among themselves.**

**Комментарий:**

*Satriṇaḥ* means they met together.

### Text 8

*aho ubhayataḥ prāptaṁ*  
*lokasya vyasanaṁ mahat*  
*dāruṇy ubhayato dīpte*  
*iva taskara-pālayoḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The people have great danger from both sides—from the thieves and from the King, like a log burning at both ends.**

**Комментарий:**

If they fled to the forest the thieves would attack them. If they remained in the city the king would torment them.

### Text 9

*arājaka-bhayād eṣa*  
*kṛto rājātad-arhaṇaḥ*  
*tato 'py āsīd bhayaṁ tv adya*  
*kathaṁ syāt svasti dehinām*

**Перевод:**

**Fearing a state without a King, they had made Vena King though he was not qualified. Therefore there was now fear of him. How will the people be happy?**

**Комментарий:**

Verses describe the effects of his being king.

### Text 14

*munaya ūcuḥ*  
*nṛpa-varya nibodhaitad*  
*yat te vijñāpayāma bhoḥ*  
*āyuḥ-śrī-bala-kīrtīnāṁ*  
*tava tāta vivardhanam*

**Перевод:**

**The sages said: Best of kings! Dear Sir! Please understand what we will reveal to you. You will have increase of life, wealth, strength and fame!**

**Комментарий:**

Many versions do not have *munaya ūcuḥ.*

### Text 18

*yasya rāṣṭre pure caiva*  
*bhagavān yajña-pūruṣaḥ*  
*ijyate svena dharmeṇa*  
*janair varṇāśramānvitaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In such a King’s state and cities, the Supreme Lord, master of sacrifices, is worshipped by people executing their individual duties according to** ***varṇāśrāma.***

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 26-27

*viṣṇur viriñco giriśa*  
*indro vāyur yamo raviḥ*  
*parjanyo dhanadaḥ somaḥ*  
*kṣitir agnir apāmpatiḥ*  
*ete cānye ca vibudhāḥ*  
*prabhavo vara-śāpayoḥ*  
*dehe bhavanti nṛpateḥ*  
*sarva-devamayo nṛpaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Viṣṇu, Brahmā, Śiva, Indra, Vāyu, Yama, the sun-god, the rain god, Kuvera, the moon-god, the earth deity, Agni, Varuṇa, and all other** ***devatās,*** **competent to bestow benedictions or curses, reside in the body of the King. The King is the composite of all** ***devatās.***

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 37

*vīkṣyotthitāṁs tadotpātān*  
*āhur loka-bhayaṅkarān*  
*apy abhadram anāthāyā*  
*dasyubhyo na bhaved bhuvaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Observing the increasing disturbances, a cause of fear in society, they discussed whether having no ruler would cause the misfortune in the world because of thieves.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī has *tadotpātān* but others have *mahotpātān.*

### Text 39-40

*tad upadravam ājñāya*  
*lokasya vasu lumpatām*  
*bhartary uparate tasminn*  
*anyonyaṁ ca jighāṁsatām*  
*cora-prāyaṁ jana-padaṁ*  
*hīna-sattvam arājakam*  
*lokān nāvārayañ chaktā*  
*api tad-doṣa-darśinaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Though they understood the disturbance to the population by people mutually looting wealth and killing, and understood that almost the whole population without a King had become thieves, devoid of sense control, some*****,*** **capable of preventing this, though seeing it happen, did not stop those who were looting others when the King had died.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Though some were capable of preventing this, the cause of not preventing it was the fault of lack of determination to prevent it by blessings and curses (*doṣa-darśinaḥ*).

### Text 41

*brāhmaṇaḥ sama-dṛk śānto*  
*dīnānāṁ samupekṣakaḥ*  
*sravate brahma tasyāpi*  
*bhinna-bhāṇḍāt payo yathā*

**Перевод:**

**If a peaceful** ***brāhmaṇa*** **who sees all equally but ignores others’ suffering, his spiritual power diminishes just like water flowing from a broken pot.**

**Комментарий:**

Four verses explain their perception.

### Text 42

*nāṅgasya vaṁśo rājarṣer*  
*eṣa saṁsthātum arhati*  
*amogha-vīryā hi nṛpā*  
*vaṁśe 'smin keśavāśrayāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The dynasty of Aṅga, a saintly King, should not be destroyed. Kings of unfailing strength, who took shelter of Viṣṇu, had appeared in this dynasty.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse explains what should be done.

## Chapter Fifteen

### Text 3

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ*  
*eṣa viṣṇor bhagavataḥ*  
*kalā bhuvana-pālinī*  
*iyaṁ ca lakṣmyāḥ sambhūtiḥ*  
*puruṣasyānapāyinī*

**Перевод:**

**The sages said: He is an expansion of the Supreme Lord and she, maintainer of the world, is an expansion of Lakṣmī, inseparable from the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Most versions have *ṛṣaya ūcuḥ.* However this is not accepted by Śrīdhara Svāmī since the previous verse already states that they spoke.

### Text 4

*ayaṁ tu prathamo rājñāṁ*  
*pumān prathayitā yaśaḥ*  
*pṛthur nāma mahārājo*  
*bhaviṣyati pṛthu-śravāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The first among kings, the male will increase his fame. He will be a great king, wildly known (*****prthu-śravāḥ*****), and thus will be named Pṛthu.**

**Комментарий:**

Since he is the first among future kings, his fame will be spread. Thus he will be widely known. Śrīdhara Svāmī accepts the addition of the word *yaḥ.*

### Text 5

*iyaṁ ca sudatī devī*  
*guṇa-bhūṣaṇa-bhūṣaṇā*  
*arcir nāma varārohā*  
*pṛthum evāvarundhatī*

**Перевод:**

**The elegant, divine woman with fine teeth, who enhances all ornaments and good qualities, named Arci will accept Pṛthu as her husband.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī accepts *bhūṣaṇam* since it is in a metaphor.

### Text 7

*maitreya uvāca*  
*praśaṁsanti sma taṁ viprā*  
*gandharva-pravarā jaguḥ*  
*mumucuḥ sumano-dhārāḥ*  
*siddhā nṛtyanti svaḥ-striyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: The** ***brāhmaṇas*** **praised him, Gandharvas sang, Siddhas showered flowers and the women of Svarga danced.**

**Комментарий:**

Some versions do not have *maitreya uvāca.*

### Text 9-10

*brahmā jagad-gurur devaiḥ*  
*sahāsṛtya sureśvaraiḥ*  
*vainyasya dakṣiṇe haste*  
*dṛṣṭvā cihnaṁ gadābhṛtaḥ*  
*pādayor aravindaṁ ca*  
*taṁ vai mene hareḥ kalām*  
*yasyāpratihataṁ cakram*  
*aṁśaḥ sa parameṣṭhinaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā, the** ***guru*** **of the universe, arrived with the** ***devatās.*** **Seeing the mark of the club on Pṛthu’s right hand and the lotus on his two feet, he understood that Pṛthu, whose other lines were the same as the Lord’s, was a portion of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 23

*tasmāt parokṣe 'smad-upaśrutāny alaṁ*  
*kariṣyatha stotram apīcya-vācaḥ*  
*saty uttamaśloka-guṇānuvāde*  
*jugupsitaṁ na stavayanti sabhyāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In the future, not in my presence, you will be able to praise me sufficiently. Skilful bards! Since the Supreme Lord’s pastimes should be sung, the assembly should not praise a low person like me.**

**Комментарий:**

You will praise my fame sufficiently in the future (*parokṣe*) or when I am not present, when my qualities are apparent. O persons with sweet words (*apicya-vācaḥ*)! Citsukha has *apīḍya-vācaḥ*: those who have worshipable words (*iḍya*). It is appropriate to praise the Lord whether he is present or not present.

### Text 24

*mahad-guṇān ātmani kartum īśaḥ*  
*kaḥ stāvakaiḥ stāvayate 'sato 'pi*  
*te 'syābhaviṣyann iti vipralabdho*  
*janāvahāsaṁ kumatir na veda*

**Перевод:**

**What person who has the qualities of the Lord would have others praise him? What to speak of a person who does not have the qualities! The fool does not understand that he is the butt of a joke, mocked by the promise of having such qualities in the future.**

**Комментарий:**

For others, such praise is useless.

### Text 25

*prabhavo hy ātmanaḥ stotraṁ*  
*jugupsanty api viśrutāḥ*  
*hrīmantaḥ paramodārāḥ*  
*pauruṣaṁ vā vigarhitam*

**Перевод:**

**Persons famous for good qualities, who are modest and generous, detest praise of themselves as if it were criticism of their abilities.**

**Комментарий:**

Persons who are worthy of praise (*prabhavaḥ)* despise being praised. They are known for their qualities (*viśrutāḥ*) and are modest and the greatest devotees.

## Chapter Sixteen

### Text 2

*nālaṁ vayaṁ te mahimānuvarṇane*  
*yo deva-varyo 'vatatāra māyayā*  
*venāṅga-jātasya ca pauruṣāṇi te*  
*vācas-patīnām api babhramur dhiyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**We are not capable of describing the glories of you, the best of** ***devas*****, who appear in this world by your mercy. The intelligence of eloquent persons such as Brahmā has become bewildered at the activities of you, born from the limb of Vena.**

**Комментарий:**

Though you were born of Veṇa (*ca*), your prowess cannot be described.

### Text 4

*eṣa dharma-bhṛtāṁ śreṣṭho*  
*lokaṁ dharme 'nuvartayan*  
*goptā ca dharma-setūnāṁ*  
*śāstā tat-paripanthinām*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu, the best upholder of** ***dharma*****, the protector of principles of** ***dharma*****, the punisher of the opposers of** ***dharma*****, engages the world in** ***dharma.***

**Комментарий:**

He is the best upholder of dharma. He engages the world in dharma. He is the punisher of opposers. Ht ie the protector of the principles of dharma.

### Text 5

*eṣa vai loka-pālānāṁ*  
*bibharty ekas tanau tanūḥ*  
*kāle kāle yathā-bhāgaṁ*  
*lokayor ubhayor hitam*

**Перевод:**

**For their prosperity, he alone maintains the powers of the** ***devatās*** **within his own form for the benefit of this world and the heavenly abode at all times.**

**Комментарий:**

*Tanūḥ* means powers.

### Text 10

*avyakta-vartmaiṣa nigūḍha-kāryo*  
*gambhīra-vedhā upagupta-vittaḥ*  
*ananta-māhātmya-guṇaika-dhāmā*  
*pṛthuḥ pracetā iva saṁvṛtātmā*

**Перевод:**

**His paths of entry and exit are unknown. The results of his actions are not known before they manifest. His intentions in action are unclear. His wealth and knowledge are very secret. He has the greatness, qualities and superior strength of Viṣṇu. Pṛthu, like Varuṇa, covers his qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *saṁbhṛtātmā*: he has a perfect form.

### Text 19

*ayaṁ tu sākṣād bhagavāṁs try-adhīśaḥ*  
*kūṭa-stha ātmā kalayāvatīrṇaḥ*  
*yasminn avidyā-racitaṁ nirarthakaṁ*  
*paśyanti nānātvam api pratītam*

**Перевод:**

**The King, directly the Lord, master of the three worlds, unchanging,** ***antaryāmī,*** **has appeared as portion of the Lord. In him the sages see that the appearance of variety created by ignorance is meaningless.**

**Комментарий:**

Among humans and devatās, there are good qualities. But these do not equal his qualities. He appears with śakti (*kalayā*). Śaktis have entered into him. He is Paramātmā (*ātmā*). He has all qualities to the highest degree. In the Lord the sages see that differences between men and devatās and oneself are meaningless since they are devoid of the highest goal. They are insignificant since they arise from ignorance.

### Text 21

*asmai nṛ-pālāḥ kila tatra tatra*  
*baliṁ hariṣyanti saloka-pālāḥ*  
*maṁsyanta eṣāṁ striya ādi-rājaṁ*  
*cakrāyudhaṁ tad-yaśa uddharantyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Everywhere, the kings along with the** ***devatās*** **will offer tribute, and their wives, glorifying his fame, will understand that Pṛthu with his** ***cakra*** **is the first king.**

**Комментарий:**

Even women will understand that Pṛthu is the greatest king. Instead of *uddharantyaḥ* Śrīdhara Svāmī approves of *udāharantyaḥ*.

### Text 23

*visphūrjayann āja-gavaṁ dhanuḥ svayaṁ*  
*yadācarat kṣmām aviṣahyam ājau*  
*tadā nililyur diśi diśy asanto*  
*lāṅgūlam udyamya yathā mṛgendraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When he traveled the earth during battle, twanging his irresistible bow made of cow and goat horn, like a lion with raised tail, the sinful fled away in all directions.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *svayam* sometimes *svam* is seen.

### Text 24

*eṣo 'śvamedhāñ śatam ājahāra*  
*sarasvatī prādurabhāvi yatra*  
*ahārṣīd yasya hayaṁ purandaraḥ*  
*śata-kratuś carame vartamāne*

**Перевод:**

**The King performed a hundred horse sacrifices where the Sarasvatī River appeared. During the last sacrifice, Indra, performer of a hundred sacrifices, stole the horse.**

**Комментарий:**

He performed the sacrifices where the Sarasvatī had manifested by Prthu’s power.

### Text 25

*eṣa sva-sadmopavane sametya*  
*sanat-kumāraṁ bhagavantam ekam*  
*ārādhya bhaktyālabhatāmalaṁ taj*  
*jñānaṁ yato brahma paraṁ vidanti*

**Перевод:**

**Meeting the great Sanat-kumāra alone in his palace garden and worshipping him with devotion, Pṛthu attained spotless knowledge by which the** ***yogīs*** **know the supreme Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *kurvanti* instead of *vidanti.*

### Text 27

*diśo vijityāpratiruddha-cakraḥ*  
*sva-tejasotpāṭita-loka-śalyaḥ*  
*surāsurendrair upagīyamāna-*  
*mahānubhāvo bhavitā patir bhuvaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Conquering the directions, having unchecked power, uprooting the miseries of the people by his prowess, and glorified by the leaders of the** ***devatās*** **and demons, he will become a leader of great authority.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *jayaṭam pṛthur bhave* instead of *bhavitā patir bhuvaḥ.*

## Chapter Seventeen

### Text 1
