*maitreya uvāca*  
*evaṁ sa bhagavān vainyaḥ*  
*khyāpito guṇa-karmabhiḥ*  
*chandayām āsa tān kāmaiḥ*  
*pratipūjyābhinandya ca*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Glorified by the reciters, Pṛthu then satisfied them with gifts after worshipping and glorifying them.**

**Комментарий:**

He was happy with the predictions about helping the people. His actions are described.

### Text 5

*sanat-kumārād bhagavato*  
*brahman brahma-vid-uttamāt*  
*labdhvā jñānaṁ sa-vijñānaṁ*  
*rājarṣiḥ kāṁ gatiṁ gataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! After obtaining knowledge and realization from Sanat-kumāra, the great knower of Brahman, what destination did the saintly king receive?**

**Комментарий:**

What was his destination, Brahman or Bhagavān?

### Text 6-7

*yac cānyad api kṛṣṇasya*  
*bhavān bhagavataḥ prabhoḥ*  
*śravaḥ suśravasaḥ puṇyaṁ*  
*pūrva-deha-kathāśrayam*  
*bhaktāya me 'nuraktāya*  
*tava cādhokṣajasya ca*  
*vaktum arhasi yo 'duhyad*  
*vainya-rūpeṇa gām imām*

**Перевод:**

**You should speak to me, attached to hearing, devoted to you and the Lord and you, concerning the person who milked the earth in the form of Pṛthu, and also other pure, famous topics related to the previous birth of the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

Vidura’s affection is revealed in these two verses. Speak other topics about *the āveśāvatara* (*deha*) which previously appeared *(pūrva*).

### Text 12

*maitreya uvāca*  
*pṛthuḥ prajānāṁ karuṇaṁ*  
*niśamya paridevitam*  
*dīrghaṁ dadhyau kuruśreṣṭha*  
*nimittaṁ so 'nvapadyata*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: O Vidura! Hearing the miserable lamentation the citizens, Pṛthu thought for a long time and then discovered the reason.**

**Комментарий:**

The cause as will be explained was that the earth had swallowed up the seeds.

### Text 13

*iti vyavasito buddhyā*  
*pragṛhīta-śarāsanaḥ*  
*sandadhe viśikhaṁ bhūmeḥ*  
*kruddhas tripura-hā yathā*

**Перевод:**

**Having discerned the cause with his intelligence, he became as angry as Śiva, and, taking up his bow, he aimed an arrow at the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

He determined what action to take (*vyavasitaḥ*). He became angry that the earth had hidden the seeds, even though he had been made the king.

### Text 15

*tām anvadhāvat tad vainyaḥ*  
*kupito 'tyaruṇekṣaṇaḥ*  
*śaraṁ dhanuṣi sandhāya*  
*yatra yatra palāyate*

**Перевод:**

**With red eyes, angry Pṛthu then chased the cow, aiming his arrow with his bow wherever she fled.**

**Комментарий:**

Wherever the earth fled, Prṭhu chased her there (*tat*). *Tām evānvadravat* is another version.

### Text 20

*praharanti na vai strīṣu*  
*kṛtāgaḥsv api jantavaḥ*  
*kim uta tvad-vidhā rājan*  
*karuṇā dīna-vatsalāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**People never beat even sinful women. O King! What to speak of a person like you who are merciful and affectionate to the wretched!**

**Комментарий:**

Ordinary beings, even foolish people (*jantavaḥ*), do not beat sinful women.

### Text 22

*pṛthur uvāca*  
*vasudhe tvāṁ vadhiṣyāmi*  
*mac-chāsana-parāṅ-mukhīm*  
*bhāgaṁ barhiṣi yā vṛṅkte*  
*na tanoti ca no vasu*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu said: O earth, holder of wealth! I will kill you, who are disobeying my orders. You accepted your share in the sacrifice, but did not bestow wealth to us!**

**Комментарий:**

He spoke just to frighten her.

### Text 28

*evaṁ manyumayīṁ mūrtiṁ*  
*kṛtāntam iva bibhratam*  
*praṇatā prāñjaliḥ prāha*  
*mahī sañjāta-vepathuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With folded hands, the trembling earth, bowing down, spoke to angry Pṛthu, who was like death personified.**

**Комментарий:**

She spoke to Pṛthu, who had produced (*bibhratam*) a form full of anger.

### Text 29

*dharovāca*  
*namaḥ parasmai puruṣāya māyayā*  
*vinyasta-nānā-tanave guṇātmane*  
*namaḥ svarūpānubhavena nirdhuta-*  
*dravya-kriyā-kāraka-vibhramormaye*

**Перевод:**

**The earth said: I offer respects to the Supreme Person, who expands as many forms by material energy, composed of the** ***guṇas*** **as the universal form. I offer respects to you who are understood to be completely free of the waves of** ***saṁsāra*** **created by bodies, senses and sense** ***devatās,*** **when a person realizes your spiritual energy*****.***

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *pṛthivy uvāca*. Sambandhokti has *puruṣāya bhūyase, vinyasta-māyā*.

### Text 32

*nūnaṁ bateśasya samīhitaṁ janais*  
*tan-māyayā durjayayākṛtātmabhiḥ*  
*na lakṣyate yas tv akarod akārayad*  
*yo 'neka ekaḥ parataś ca īśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The plan of the Lord, who carries out primary creation and engages others in secondary creation, who is one through himself and is many through** ***māyā,*** **cannot be understood by people whose minds are bewildered by his unconquerable** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

The plane of the Lord cannot be understood by people whose minds are bewildered by māyā with its upādhis (tat-māyayā). Svayam Bhagavān being one and many made (akarot) the universe and not associating with it, made others create the universe (akārayat). Not only is that his power. He is the Lord in Vaikuṇṭha beyond matter (parataḥ). Thus it is reasonable that his plans cannot be understood.

### Text 33

*sargādi yo 'syānuruṇaddhi śaktibhir*  
*dravya-kriyā-kāraka-cetanātmabhiḥ*  
*tasmai samunnaddha-niruddha-śaktaye*  
*namaḥ parasmai puruṣāya vedhase*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects the Supreme Lord, the creator, to you who carry out for the benefit of your devotees the creation, maintenance and destruction of the universe by your energies of** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **intelligence, bodies, senses and sense** ***devatās*****. I offer respects to you who possess great but mutually contrary energies.**

**Комментарий:**

*Kārakā* means devatās who stimulate prakṛti.

### Text 35

*apām upasthe mayi nāvy avasthitāḥ*  
*prajā bhavān adya rirakṣiṣuḥ kila*  
*sa vīra-mūrtiḥ samabhūd dharā-dharo*  
*yo māṁ payasy ugra-śaro jighāṁsasi*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring to protect the progeny situated in me, a boat on the water, you lifted me up. You have now become Pṛthu, a figure of courage, and desire to kill me for milk with sharp arrows.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *gopāyase agra-sara.*

### Text 36

*nūnaṁ janair īhitam īśvarāṇām*  
*asmad-vidhais tad-guṇa-sarga-māyayā*  
*na jñāyate mohita-citta-vartmabhis*  
*tebhyo namo vīra-yaśas-karebhyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Your desire is not known to persons like the wives or son of the** ***devatās,*** **whose minds are bewildered by ignorance arising from various bodies we assume in this world, but who have surrendered to you. I offer respects to those who bring auspiciousness.**

**Комментарий:**

The desire of even other controllers is not known to persons like us. I offer respects to those who will cause auspciousness (*vīra-yaśas-karebhyaḥ*).

## Chapter Eighteen

### Text 8

*nūnaṁ tā vīrudhaḥ kṣīṇā*  
*mayi kālena bhūyasā*  
*tatra yogena dṛṣṭena*  
*bhavān ādātum arhati*

**Перевод:**

**These plants have been situated within me for a long time in a subtle state. By the method I will explain you should take them out.**

**Комментарий:**

By the method, done by Pṛthu in previous kalpas according to scripture *(dṛṣṭena*), you should take the plants out.

### Text 9-10

*vatsaṁ kalpaya me vīra*  
*yenāhaṁ vatsalā tava*  
*dhokṣye kṣīramayān kāmān*  
*anurūpaṁ ca dohanam*  
*dogdhāraṁ ca mahā-bāho*  
*bhūtānāṁ bhūta-bhāvana*  
*annam īpsitam ūrjasvad*  
*bhagavān vāñchate yadi*

**Перевод:**

**O courageous King! O mighty-armed King! Nourisher of the living beings! If you want the desire foods producing strength, arrange for a calf so that I, being affectionate, will produce desired milk suitable for the living beings, and also arrange a pot and a milkman.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 12

*iti priyaṁ hitaṁ vākyaṁ*  
*bhuva ādāya bhūpatiḥ*  
*vatsaṁ kṛtvā manuṁ pāṇāv*  
*aduhat sakalauṣadhīḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Considering the beneficial words of the earth, the King, making Svāyambhuva the calf, milked out all the plants into his hands.**

**Комментарий:**

The milk from the earth was all the seeds (*sakalauṣadhīḥ*). Seeds have power, manifested by watering. Manu was the calf, representing the origin of all the kings. That combination made the earth develop attraction to be productive. Pṛthu’s hands were the pot, the śakti of protection, to collect the plants (milk) and protect them.

### Text 14

*ṛṣayo duduhur devīm*  
*indriyeṣv atha sattama*  
*vatsaṁ bṛhaspatiṁ kṛtvā*  
*payaś chandomayaṁ śuci*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! Making Bṛhaspati the calf, the sages obtained from the earth milk in the form of pure Vedic verses, with the senses as the pot.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as the earth as a cow produces milk, she produced Vedic verses. They manifest by perceiving them. Suitable for manifesting the verses were voice, mind and ear. Thus senses were the pot. The combination of senses with the verses produced their manifestation. Bṛhaspati is most qualified:

*purodhasāṁ vasiṣṭho ’haṁ brahmiṣṭhānāṁ bṛhaspatiḥ*  
*skando ’haṁ sarva-senānyām agraṇyāṁ bhagavān ajaḥ*

**Among priests, I am Vasiṣṭha Muni, and among those skilful in the Vedas, I am Bṛhaspati. I am Kārtikeya among military leaders, and among those who are outstanding, I am the great personality Brahmā. (SB** [**11.16.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/16.22)**)**

Thus he was made the calf, most qualified, to receive the milk.

### Text 15

*kṛtvā vatsaṁ sura-gaṇā*  
*indraṁ somam adūduhan*  
*hiraṇmayena pātreṇa*  
*vīryam ojo balaṁ payaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***devatās,*** **making Indra the calf, produced nectar with power of mind, senses and body as the milk, in a pot of gold.**

**Комментарий:**

The milk was amṛta, which produces strength (*vīryam*) etc.

### Text 16

*daiteyā dānavā vatsaṁ*  
*prahlādam asurarṣabham*  
*vidhāyādūduhan kṣīram*  
*ayaḥ-pātre surāsavam*

**Перевод:**

**The sons of Diti, the demons, making Prahlāda, best of the demonic family, the calf, produced liquor in an iron pot.**

**Комментарий:**

An iron pot indicates that the product liquor was inferior. The *amṛta* had a golden pot. Though Prahlāda would appear in the future, by the instructions of the earth, the Daityas thought of Prahlāda as the calf.

### Text 17

*gandharvāpsaraso 'dhukṣan*  
*pātre padmamaye payaḥ*  
*vatsaṁ viśvāvasuṁ kṛtvā*  
*gāndharvaṁ madhu saubhagam*

**Перевод:**

**The Gandharvas and Apsarās, making Viśvāvasu the calf, produced sweet, beautiful songs as the milk, with a lotus as the pot.**

**Комментарий:**

The milk was songs sweet with fragrance (*madhu gandharvam*), which manifested beauty and grace (*saubhagam*), the sweetness of songs. The lotus acts as a stimulus, since it is the abode of fragrance, beauty and grace.

### Text 18

*vatsena pitaro 'ryamṇā*  
*kavyaṁ kṣīram adhukṣata*  
*āma-pātre mahā-bhāgāḥ*  
*śraddhayā śrāddha-devatāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Pitṛs, the** ***devatās*** **of funeral rites, making Aryamā the calf, with faith produced offerings to the ancestors as the milk, in a pot of unbaked clay.**

**Комментарий:**

The unbaked pot was suitable since that is used for making offerings to Pitṛs.

### Text 23-24

*paśavo yavasaṁ kṣīraṁ*  
*vatsaṁ kṛtvā ca go-vṛṣam*  
*araṇya-pātre cādhukṣan*  
*mṛgendreṇa ca daṁṣṭriṇaḥ*  
*kravyādāḥ prāṇinaḥ kravyaṁ*  
*duduhuḥ sve kalevare*  
*suparṇa-vatsā vihagāś*  
*caraṁ cācaram eva ca*

**Перевод:**

**The cattle, making Nandī the calf, produced grass as the milk. Carnivorous animals, making the lion their calf, produced the flesh of other living beings as the milk with the forest as the pot. The birds, making Garuḍa the calf, produced insects and fruit as the milk, with their stomachs as the pot.**

**Комментарий:**

When speaking of the animals it means the devatās in charge of those animals. The flesh eating animals made the flesh from the bodies (*sve kalevare*) of other animals the milk. *Suparṇa* is not Garuḍa in person, but a meditational form like Prahlāda.

### Text 27

*evaṁ pṛthv-ādayaḥ pṛthvīm*  
*annādāḥ svannam ātmanaḥ*  
*doha-vatsādi-bhedena*  
*kṣīra-bhedaṁ kurūdvaha*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! In this way Pṛthu and others who eat food milked different milks, their desired foods, from the earth with different pots and different calves.**

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *manv-ādyāḥ* instead of *pṛthuv-ādayaḥ.*

## Chapter Nineteen

### Text 2

*tad abhipretya bhagavān*  
*karmātiśayam ātmanaḥ*  
*śata-kratur na mamṛṣe*  
*pṛthor yajña-mahotsavam*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking that Pṛthu would excel him, Indra could not tolerate the great sacrifice of Pṛthu.**

**Комментарий:**

Indra understood that Pṛthu, the personification of great sacrifices (*karma*), had surpassed Indra’s sacrifices.

### Text 3

*yatra yajña-patiḥ sākṣād*  
*bhagavān harir īśvaraḥ*  
*anvabhūyata sarvātmā*  
*sarva-loka-guruḥ prabhuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In that sacrifice, the master of sacrifice, the Supreme Lord, the soul of all beings, the** ***guru*** **for the whole universe, Viṣṇu, personally appeared.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 7

*yatra dharma-dughā bhūmiḥ*  
*sarva-kāma-dughā satī*  
*dogdhi smābhīpsitān arthān*  
*yajamānasya bhārata*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! At that sacrifice, the pure earth, yielding all desirable things, yielding** ***dharma*****, offered all desired objects to Pṛthu.**

**Комментарий:**

The earth’s dharma was to offer items like milk.

### Text 9

*sindhavo ratna-nikarān*  
*girayo 'nnaṁ catur-vidham*  
*upāyanam upājahruḥ*  
*sarve lokāḥ sa-pālakāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The oceans offered heaps of jewels, mountains offered four kinds of food and all the planets and their lords offered gifts.**

**Комментарий:**

The four types of foods are chewed, eating without chewing, sucked and licked.

### Text 14

*taṁ tādṛśākṛtiṁ vīkṣya*  
*mene dharmaṁ śarīriṇam*  
*jaṭilaṁ bhasmanācchannaṁ*  
*tasmai bāṇaṁ na muñcati*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing him in that dress, with matted locks and smeared in ashes, Pṛthu’s son thought he must be a religious person. He did not shoot him.**

**Комментарий:**

Because of seeing Śiva and others, using that dress one would mistake him for a religious person.

### Text 23

*yāni rūpāṇi jagṛhe*  
*indro haya-jihīrṣayā*  
*tāni pāpasya khaṇḍāni*  
*liṅgaṁ khaṇḍam ihocyate*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever forms Indra accepted for stealing the horse, those became symbols or khaṇḍa of sin. Thus those persons are called** ***pākhaṇḍas*** **or** ***pāṣaṇḍas.***

**Комментарий:**

Fools accepted those forms. Indra’s disguise was for spoiling the sacrifice. Others made their own sampradaya later on, with the intention of stealing from others.

### Text 24-25

*evam indre haraty aśvaṁ*  
*vainya-yajña-jighāṁsayā*  
*tad-gṛhīta-visṛṣṭeṣu*  
*pākhaṇḍeṣu matir nṛṇām*  
*dharma ity upadharmeṣu*  
*nagna-rakta-paṭādiṣu*  
*prāyeṇa sajjate bhrāntyā*  
*peśaleṣu ca vāgmiṣu*

**Перевод:**

**When Indra stole the horse with a desire to stop the sacrifice of Pṛthu, people’s minds became attracted to those disguises of heretics which he assumed and rejected, thinking by mistake that these resemblances to** ***dharma*****, in the form of going naked, wearing red cloth and speaking skillfully, were real** ***dharma.***

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. The upadharmas are being naked, wearing red cloth etc.

### Text 26

*tad abhijñāya bhagavān*  
*pṛthuḥ pṛthu-parākramaḥ*  
*indrāya kupito bāṇam*  
*ādattodyata-kārmukaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding this, Pṛthu, greatly courageous and angry at Indra, took up his bow and aimed his arrow.**

**Комментарий:**

Pṛthu desired to kill Indra, though he was worshipable and received the results of horse sacrifices, since Pṛthu was the original Indra, possessing special śakti of the Lord.

### Text 29

*ity āmantrya kratu-patiṁ*  
*vidurāsyartvijo ruṣā*  
*srug-ghastāñ juhvato 'bhyetya*  
*svayambhūḥ pratyaṣedhata*

**Перевод:**

**After informing the king of sacrifice, Brahmā approached the priests of Pṛthu, who, ladle in hand, were offering oblations in anger. He stopped them.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *pratyabhāṣata.*

### Text 30

*na vadhyo bhavatām indro*  
*yad yajño bhagavat-tanuḥ*  
*yaṁ jighāṁsatha yajñena*  
*yasyeṣṭās tanavaḥ surāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**You should not kill Indra because he is the body of the Lord. You wish to kill Indra by a sacrifice in which the** ***devatās,*** **part of his body, are worshipped.**

**Комментарий:**

You should not kill Indra whom you wish to kill by a sacrifice, since that sacrifice is the body of the Lord, and the devatās worshipped are parts of that body. If you kill one body, the others cannot exist. It is proper that both are protected.

### Text 33

*naivātmane mahendrāya*  
*roṣam āhartum arhasi*  
*ubhāv api hi bhadraṁ te*  
*uttamaśloka-vigrahau*

**Перевод:**

**You should not show anger to Indra, since he is non-different from you. Good fortune to both of you, who are forms of the Supreme Lord!**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *ātmanā*: you should not be angry at Indra by your mind. However this does not seem correct since *ubhau* refers to two persons: Indra and the Lord.

### Text 35

*kratur viramatām eṣa*  
*deveṣu duravagrahaḥ*  
*dharma-vyatikaro yatra*  
*pākhaṇḍair indra-nirmitaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Let this sacrifice be stopped, since it causes dissessnion among the devatās.** ***Dharma*** **has been destroyed by heretics introduced by Indra.**

**Комментарий:**

This sacrifice, causing dissension among the devatās should be stopped. What is the harm in such dissension? Dharma will be corrupted (*vyatikaraḥ*).

### Text 36

*ebhir indropasaṁsṛṣṭaiḥ*  
*pākhaṇḍair hāribhir janam*  
*hriyamāṇaṁ vicakṣvainaṁ*  
*yas te yajña-dhrug aśva-muṭ*

**Перевод:**

**See the people carried away by the attractive heretics created by Indra, who stole the horse and destroyed your sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

The strength of the heretics is shown.

### Text 37

*bhavān paritrātum ihāvatīrṇo*  
*dharmaṁ janānāṁ samayānurūpam*  
*venāpacārād avaluptam adya*  
*tad-dehato viṣṇu-kalāsi vainya*

**Перевод:**

**O Pṛthu! You have appeared in this world from Vena’s body as a portion of the Lord to deliver** ***dharma*** **suitable at this time for the people, which has become lost.**

**Комментарий:**

These heretics should not be given attention. This is explained in two verses.

### Text 41

*viprāḥ satyāśiṣas tuṣṭāḥ*  
*śraddhayā labdha-dakṣiṇāḥ*  
*āśiṣo yuyujuḥ kṣattar*  
*ādi-rājāya sat-kṛtāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Vidura! The** ***brāhmaṇas*****, whose blessings come true, and who were satisfied, having been worshipped and given donations with faith, gave blessings to the original King.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha ends the chapter here. In his version the first line is *tathā ca kṛtavān sarvam.*

## Chapter Twenty

### Text 2

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*eṣa te 'kārṣīd bhaṅgaṁ*  
*haya-medha-śatasya ha*  
*kṣamāpayata ātmānam*  
*amuṣya kṣantum arhasi*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: Here is Indra who stopped the hundredth horse sacrifice. You should forgive Indra, since he is asking forgiveness from you.**

**Комментарий:**

Another version is *eṣa te hy akarot.*

### Text 3

*sudhiyaḥ sādhavo loke*  
*naradeva narottamāḥ*  
*nābhidruhyanti bhūtebhyo*  
*yarhi nātmā kalevaram*

**Перевод:**

**O King! The most intelligent devotees, the best of humans, do not harm other living beings because they know those bodies are not the soul.**

**Комментарий:**

First he pacifies him by jñāna. Here the body refers to the gross and subtle bodies.

### Text 6

*asaṁsaktaḥ śarīre 'sminn*  
*amunotpādite gṛhe*  
*apatye draviṇe vāpi*  
*kaḥ kuryān mamatāṁ budhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**What wise person, unattached to this body, will have attachment for house, children and wealth, arising from the body?**

### Text 7

*ekaḥ śuddhaḥ svayaṁ-jyotir*  
*nirguṇo 'sau guṇāśrayaḥ*  
*sarva-go 'nāvṛtaḥ sākṣī*  
*nirātmātmātmanaḥ paraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Paramātmā is pure, self-revealing, beyond the material** ***guṇas,*** **the shelter of the** ***guṇas,*** **all-pervading, uncovered by matter, the witness of all things, independent of all others, and different from the** ***jīva*** **and his body.**

**Комментарий:**

Having taught that one should realize to some degree the pure jīva is different from the gross and subtle bodies, he teaches about realizing directly Paramātmā.

Paramātmā (*asau ātmā*) is different from the pure ātmā (*ātmanaḥ paraḥ*). He is beyond the senses of the jīva since he is one alone (*ekaḥ*) whereas the jīvas are many. He is pure (*śuddhaḥ*), not ever being touched by impurity. The jīva however is contaminated by ignorance. The Lord is self-effulgent, the principle entity, like the sun. The jīva is like the ray, dependent on the Lord. The Lord is devoid of material guṇas in all cases (*nirguṇaḥ*), whereas the jīva is bound by conceptions of self by ignorance. The Lord is like the sun, the shelter of qualities (*guṇāśrayaḥ*), but not pervaded by them. He is all pervading (*sarva-gaḥ*), whereas the jīva is atomic. Though situated in the body, he is not covered by it (*anāvṛtaḥ*). The jīva however is covered by the body. The Lord is directly the witness (*sākṣī*). The jīva is only capable of witnessing by the Lord’s mercy. The Lord does not have another ātmā within him (*nirātmā*). The jīva has the Lord within.

### Text 8

*ya evaṁ santam ātmānam*  
*ātma-sthaṁ veda pūruṣaḥ*  
*nājyate prakṛti-stho 'pi*  
*tad-guṇaiḥ sa mayi sthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The person who knows the Paramātmā situated in the self, though situated in the body, is not affected by** ***guṇas*** **of the body, since he is situated in me.**

**Комментарий:**

He who knows Paramātmā situated as the antaryāmī of the pure jīva (*ātma-stham*), who seeks to see himself as being in my shelter (*mayi sthitaḥ*), is not affected by the guṇas.

### Text 10

*parityakta-guṇaḥ samyag*  
*darśano viśadāśayaḥ*  
*śāntiṁ me samavasthānaṁ*  
*brahma kaivalyam aśnute*

**Перевод:**

**Being devoid of the influence of the** ***guṇas,*** **seeing correctly, with pure mind, he attains peace, a position of non-difference called Brahman or** ***kaivalya.***

**Комментарий:**

He realizes brahma-kaivalyam manifested with various śaktis (*me samavasthānam*).

### Text 11

*udāsīnam ivādhyakṣaṁ*  
*dravya-jñāna-kriyātmanām*  
*kūṭa-stham imam ātmānaṁ*  
*yo vedāpnoti śobhanam*

**Перевод:**

**One who knows** ***jīva*** **situated** **as the superintendent of the mind along with gross organs, subtle sense organs and sense** ***devatās*** **but indifferent** **attains release from this world.**

**Комментарий:**

*Kevalam* is shown. One who knows the ātmā, situated like a controller, but indifferent, attains liberation. Sambandhokti has *abhavam* instead of *śobhanam*. Citsukha has *abhayam.* *Śobhanam* is the Gauḍīya version.

### Text 12

*bhinnasya liṅgasya guṇa-pravāho*  
*dravya-kriyā-kāraka-cetanātmanaḥ*  
*dṛṣṭāsu sampatsu vipatsu sūrayo*  
*na vikriyante mayi baddha-sauhṛdāḥ*

**Перевод:**

***Saṁsāra*** **takes place only because of identification with the subtle body, composed of intelligence, sense objects, sense organs and sense** ***devatās.*** **The devotees, bound to me by friendship, are not disturbed on seeing the happiness and distress of this world.**

**Комментарий:**

He then pacifies him with bhakti.

### Text 13

*samaḥ samānottama-madhyamādhamaḥ*  
*sukhe ca duḥkhe ca jitendriyāśayaḥ*  
*mayopakḷptākhila-loka-saṁyuto*  
*vidhatsva vīrākhila-loka-rakṣaṇam*

**Перевод:**

**O hero! Being equal in happiness and distress, treating the high, middle and low living entities equally, controlling mind and senses, equipped with all types of persons supplied by me, please engage in protecting all the people.**

**Комментарий:**

He then instructs him what should be done. Citsukha has *sattva* instead of *loka.*

### Text 15

*evaṁ dvijāgryānumatānuvṛtta-*  
*dharma-pradhāno 'nyatamo 'vitāsyāḥ*  
*hrasvena kālena gṛhopayātān*  
*draṣṭāsi siddhān anurakta-lokaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Being the leader in** ***dharma*** **traditionally handed down and approved by the best of** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **being unattached to your position, protecting the earth, and being loved by the people, you will see great saints coming to your house in a short time.**

**Комментарий:**

He indicates his duty at a later time, with a desire to preach to the devotees fixed in his service and a desire to preach jñāna to others.

### Text 16

*varaṁ ca mat kañcana mānavendra*  
*vṛṇīṣva te 'haṁ guṇa-śīla-yantritaḥ*  
*nāhaṁ makhair vai sulabhas tapobhir*  
*yogena vā yat sama-citta-vartī*

**Перевод:**

**O King of men! Ask any benediction from me. I am controlled by your qualities and nature. I am not easily attained by sacrifices, difficult austerities or** ***yoga,*** **since I am situated in all their hearts neutrally.**

**Комментарий:**

At this time he indicates the first desire. I am controlled by your qualities and conduct which arise from bhakti (*guṇa-śīla-yantritaḥ*). Qualities include surrender to guru and gaining knowledge. Conduct includes, good nature and gentleness. Sama should arise from bhakti, not from yoga.

*nārāyaṇa-parāḥ sarve na kutaścana bibhyati*  
*svargāpavarga-narakeṣv api tulyārtha-darśinaḥ*

**Devotees solely engaged in the service of Nārāyaṇa never fear any condition of life. For them the heavenly planets, liberation and the hellish planets are all the same. (SB** [**6.17.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/17.28)**)**

*na sādhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava*  
*na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā*

**My dear Uddhava, the unalloyed devotional service rendered to me by my devotees brings me under their control. I cannot be thus controlled by those engaged in mystic yoga, Sāṅkhya philosophy, pious work, Vedic study, austerity or renunciation. (SB** [**11.14.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.20)**)**

### Text 17

*maitreya uvāca*  
*sa itthaṁ loka-guruṇā*  
*viṣvaksenena viśva-jit*  
*anuśāsita ādeśaṁ*  
*śirasā jagṛhe hareḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: When so instructed by the** ***guru*** **of the universe, the Supreme Lord, Pṛthu accepted the instructions on his head.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord gave three instructions. He should be pleased with Indra (thus protecting the people). Siddhas would agree to come to his house. He should accept blessings. Pṛthu accepted the three instructions. The last two he accepted in a special way according to his liking. The first is expressed in the next verse.

### Text 18

*spṛśantaṁ pādayoḥ premṇā*  
*vrīḍitaṁ svena karmaṇā*  
*śata-kratuṁ pariṣvajya*  
*vidveṣaṁ visasarja ha*

**Перевод:**

**Embracing Indra, who was ashamed of his actions and touched his feet with affection, he gave up hatred of Indra.**

**Комментарий:**

Indra had great affection because he had met with the Lord who is the shelter of the highest prema.

### Text 23

*pṛthur uvāca*  
*varān vibho tvad varadeśvarād budhaḥ*  
*kathaṁ vṛṇīte guṇa-vikriyātmanām*  
*ye nārakāṇām api santi dehināṁ*  
*tān īśa kaivalya-pate vṛṇe na ca*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu said: O Lord! How can an intelligent person ask for benedictions from you, the Lord who gives all benedictions? O Lord of liberation! I do not ask for those benedictions which exist for people in hellish existence, with minds which are a transformation of the** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

The special way of accepting the instructions about siddhas will be shown later. Now the way of accepting benedictions is shown. Because you are the lord of Brahmā and others who award benedictions and thus give the greatest benedictions, how can an intelligent person choose ordinary benedictions from you? Though I am foolish (*ca*) I do not choose such benedictions.

### Text 24

*na kāmaye nātha tad apy ahaṁ kvacin*  
*na yatra yuṣmac-caraṇāmbujāsavaḥ*  
*mahattamāntar-hṛdayān mukha-cyuto*  
*vidhatsva karṇāyutam eṣa me varaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I do not desire anything, and especially I do not want liberation, in which there is no nectar of your lotus feet, coming from the hearts of great devotees and falling from their mouths. Give me millions of ears. That is my request.**

**Комментарий:**

Desire for millions of ears indicates his great attachment to hearing about the Lord.

### Text 25

*sa uttamaśloka mahan-mukha-cyuto*  
*bhavat-padāmbhoja-sudhā kaṇānilaḥ*  
*smṛtiṁ punar vismṛta-tattva-vartmanāṁ*  
*kuyogināṁ no vitaraty alaṁ varaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O most praised Lord! The topics falling from the mouths of the great devotees, or even the wind touching a drop of nectar from your lotus feet, brings remembrance even for the bad** ***yogīs*** **who have forgotten the truth you have just taught. Away with all these benedictions including liberation!**

**Комментарий:**

If we are not qualified to pray in this manner since we do not have deep attachment, then let us at least hear. Some yogīs have forgotten your lotus feet, the root (*tattvam*), and knowledge of the pure ātmā following after those feet.

### Text 26

*yaśaḥ śivaṁ suśrava ārya-saṅgame*  
*yadṛcchayā copaśṛṇoti te sakṛt*  
*kathaṁ guṇa-jño viramed vinā paśuṁ*  
*śrīr yat pravavre guṇa-saṅgrahecchayā*

**Перевод:**

**Lord of auspicious fame! What person, having heard by chance, just once, your auspicious qualities in the association of devotees, what person who knows your qualities, which even Lakṣmī accepts with a desire to completely taste those qualities, gives up that glorification? Only an animal would do so.**

**Комментарий:**

How can any person, a human who knows your qualities, unless he is an animal, give up glorifying you? That person is an animal among humans. If he does not give up the glorification he is the highest knower of your qualities, even if he is an animal. Even Lakṣmī accepts those qualities with a desire to absorb them in herself.

### Text 27

*athābhaje tvākhila-pūruṣottamaṁ*  
*guṇālayaṁ padma-kareva lālasaḥ*  
*apy āvayor eka-pati-spṛdhoḥ kalir*  
*na syāt kṛta-tvac-caraṇaika-tānayoḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Having great desire like Lakṣmī, with complete purity I will worship the feet of you, the Supreme Lord, the abode of all qualities. Perhaps there will be a competition between us for one master? No, we will both be fully concentrating on serving your lotus feet.**

**Комментарий:**

Here a person qualified with dāsya-bhāva (Pṛthu) combines this rasa with incompatible ujjvala-rasa to create ābhāsa. Dāsya-bhāva is evident in the section. The combination with ujjvala-rasa is suggested by the phrase *padma-kareva lālāsa* (desire like Lakṣmī). Here is the solution. Pṛthu actually has not developed a desire for kānta-bhāva but for bhakti-maya-rasa (dāsya). He gives the example of Lakṣmī to indicate only her bhakti, not her kānta-bhāva. Competition with her is not unsuitable for one who has servitude with a vīra mood, for increasing great mercy of the Lord.

Others think as follows. He does not actually compete with Lakṣmī, but only expresses words of prema to indicate the mercy that was shown to his fallen self, since he considers himself insignificant in verse 28. Or Pṛthu will worship without competition since he will be concentrating on serving the Lord. But Lakṣmī may develop a quarrelsome attitude.

### Text 29

*bhajanty atha tvām ata eva sādhavo*  
*vyudasta-māyā-guṇa-vibhramodayam*  
*bhavat-padānusmaraṇād ṛte satāṁ*  
*nimittam anyad bhagavan na vidmahe*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees worship you, who destroy the abundant illusions arising from the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***māyā.*** **For the devotees, I do not know any result from worshipping you except remembrance of your lotus feet.**

**Комментарий:**

Taking your affection for the miserable as the main uddīpana (*ata eva*), persons with no cheating (*sādhavaḥ*) worship you. Thus they do not know any other result than remembering you.

### Text 31

*tvan-māyayāddhā jana īśa khaṇḍito*  
*yad anyad āśāsta ṛtātmano 'budhaḥ*  
*yathā cared bāla-hitaṁ pitā svayaṁ*  
*tathā tvam evārhasi naḥ samīhitum*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Since foolish people, injured by your** ***māyā*****, desire things other than worshipping you, you should choose (without asking us) what is for our benefit, just as a father acts for the benefit of his son.**

**Комментарий:**

They have been injured by your māyā, but you should not do this again. You should act for their benefit. Even if they desire other things like sons, unknowingly you should act for their benefit. They are not injured by you, but by your māyā.

### Text 32

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ity ādi-rājena nutaḥ sa viśva-dṛk*  
*tam āha rājan mayi bhaktir astu te*  
*diṣṭyedṛśī dhīr mayi te kṛtā yayā*  
*māyāṁ madīyāṁ tarati sma dustyajām*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: After being praised by Pṛthu in this way, the Lord, seer of the whole universe, said, “O King! May you have** ***bhakti*** **to me! By good fortune you have placed in me fine intelligence by which one can cross insurmountable** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

Some versions do not have the words *maitreya uvāca.* The Lord had faith that Pṛthu had fixed bhakti for the Lord, and thus had faith that he would not give false teachings. Thus he desired to give only bhakti. He speaks to reveal Pṛthu’s steadiness and to broadcast it everywhere and, by that, to preach jñāna etc. to persons qualified only for that. That is revealed in this verse. In his statements Pṛthu rejected false knowledge (*itiyād*i). Some versions have *tataruḥ sudustaram.*

### Text 35-36

*devarṣi-pitṛ-gandharva-*  
*siddha-cāraṇa-pannagāḥ*  
*kinnarāpsaraso martyāḥ*  
*khagā bhūtāny anekaśaḥ*  
*yajñeśvara-dhiyā rājñā*  
*vāg-vittāñjali-bhaktitaḥ*  
*sabhājitā yayuḥ sarve*  
*vaikuṇṭhānugatās tataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With devotion, Pṛthu worshipped the** ***devatās*****, Pitṛs, Gandharvas, Siddhas, Cāraṇas, Nāgas, Kinnaras Apsarās, human beings, birds and many other living entities, by folding his hands, by his words and by wealth, thinking of them as representatives of the Lord of sacrifice. The servants of the Lord went with the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. *Vaikuṇṭhasyānugatāḥ* means those who serve the Lord. The Lord’s followers went with Lord.

## Chapter Twenty-one

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*mauktikaiḥ kusuma-sragbhir*  
*dukūlaiḥ svarṇa-toraṇaiḥ*  
*mahā-surabhibhir dhūpair*  
*maṇḍitaṁ tatra tatra vai*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: The city was decorated with pearls, flower garlands, cloth, golden arches, and fragrant incense in various places.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe the city.

### Text 2

*candanāguru-toyārdra-*  
*rathyā-catvara-mārgavat*  
*puṣpākṣata-phalais tokmair*  
*lājair arcirbhir arcitam*

**Перевод:**

**Its roads and intersections were wet with sandalwood and** ***aguru*** **scent, and decorated with flowers, raw rice, fruits, barley shoots, popped rice and lamps.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *stomaiḥ* instead of *tokmaiḥ.*

### Text 9

*vidura uvāca*  
*so 'bhiṣiktaḥ pṛthur viprair*  
*labdhāśeṣa-surārhaṇaḥ*  
*bibhrat sa vaiṣṇavaṁ tejo*  
*bāhvor yābhyāṁ dudoha gām*

**Перевод:**

**Vidura said: Enthroned by the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **worshipped by numerous** ***devatās,*** **Pṛthu received Viṣṇu’s strength in his arms with which he milked the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *bibhrac ca* is seen instead of *bibhrat sa.* However *saḥ* is repeated in the verse.

### Text 10

*ko nv asya kīrtiṁ na śṛṇoty abhijño*  
*yad-vikramocchiṣṭam aśeṣa-bhūpāḥ*  
*lokāḥ sa-pālā upajīvanti kāmam*  
*adyāpi tan me vada karma śuddham*

**Перевод:**

**What intelligent person will not hear Pṛthu’s glories? Even now, the** ***devatās*** **and planets maintain their lives with the desirable remnants produced by his valiant action. Please tell me of his pure actions.**

**Комментарий:**

The devatās maintain their lives from what is obtained (*ucchiṣṭam*) when he is victorious.

### Text 14

*tasminn arhatsu sarveṣu*  
*sv-arciteṣu yathārhataḥ*  
*utthitaḥ sadaso madhye*  
*tārāṇām uḍurāḍ iva*

**Перевод:**

**There, Pṛthu stood up in the assembly among the great persons worthy of worship, who had been worshipped befitting to their status, like the moon among the stars.**

**Комментарий:**

His beauty is described in six verses.

### Text 16

*vyūḍha-vakṣā bṛhac-chroṇir*  
*vali-valgu-dalodaraḥ*  
*āvarta-nābhir ojasvī*  
*kāñcanorur udagra-pāt*

**Перевод:**

**He had a broad chest, large waist, an abdomen shaped like a banyan leaf with three lines, a twirled navel, golden thighs and feet raised in front.**

**Комментарий:**

His thighs were like golden pillars. Citsukha has *udagra-vāk* instead of *udagra-pāt.*

### Text 17

*sūkṣma-vakrāsita-snigdha-*  
*mūrdhajaḥ kambu-kandharaḥ*  
*mahā-dhane dukūlāgrye*  
*paridhāyopavīya ca*

**Перевод:**

**He had fine, black, curly, glossy hair. His neck was like a conch. He was wearing valuable cloth on his lower and upper body.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *parivīyopavīya ca.*

## Text 18-19-20

*vyañjitāśeṣa-gātra-śrīr*  
*niyame nyasta-bhūṣaṇaḥ*  
*kṛṣṇājina-dharaḥ śrīmān*  
*kuśa-pāṇiḥ kṛtocitaḥ*  
*śiśira-snigdha-tārākṣaḥ*  
*samaikṣata samantataḥ*  
*ūcivān idam urvīśaḥ*  
*sadaḥ saṁharṣayann iva*  
*cāru citra-padaṁ ślakṣṇaṁ*  
*mṛṣṭaṁ gūḍham aviklavam*  
*sarveṣām upakārārthaṁ*  
*tadā anuvadann iva*

**Перевод:**

**Showing unlimited beauty in his limbs, wearing ornaments expertly placed, wearing a black deer skin and shining with** ***kuśa*** **on his finger when required, the ruler of the earth, who had charming pupils in his eyes as cooling as dew, glanced around. Giving joy to the assembly, he resonantly spoke attractive, delightful words which were ornamented with beautiful syllables, faultless, full of allusions, and which were easy to understand, for the benefit of all present.**

**Комментарий:**

His speech was pleasing to hear (c*āru*) because of excellent tone and pronunciation of the syllables. Alliteration and other ornaments made the statements attractive (*citra-padam*). The words were excellent (*ślakṣṇam*), devoid of vulgarity, polite, with instructions for everyone’s benefit. The words were pure (*mṛṣṭam*), following correct grammar. The words had deep meaning (*gūḍham*), since the import was obtained by careful consideration. The words were not confusing, since there was no contradiction between previous and later statements and had unhurried delivery.

### Text 23

*tasya me tad-anuṣṭhānād*  
*yān āhur brahma-vādinaḥ*  
*lokāḥ syuḥ kāma-sandohā*  
*yasya tuṣyati diṣṭa-dṛk*

**Перевод:**

**From executing all my duties, all the places which the followers of the Vedas describe, which fulfil my desires, will be mine, since I will please the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

These words are for kings with material desires, in order to spread the teachings of the Lord, and not for his benefit. The other meaning is that he will have devotees (*lokāḥ*) who will have their desires fulfilled. The Lord who witnesses appointment of karma (*diṣṭa-dṛk*) will be satisfied with him for that.

### Text 26

*yūyaṁ tad anumodadhvaṁ*  
*pitṛ-devarṣayo 'malāḥ*  
*kartuḥ śāstur anujñātus*  
*tulyaṁ yat pretya tat phalam*

**Перевод:**

**I request you pure** ***devatās*****, Pitṛs and sages to approve of this, for after death the result is equally shared by the performer, the person who gives him the order, and the one who approves it.**

**Комментарий:**

The cause for approving the bhagavad-dharma just mentioned is explained. The result goes to the performer, the person who gives the order, and the person who permits it.

### Text 27

*asti yajña-patir nāma*  
*keṣāñcid arha-sattamāḥ*  
*ihāmutra ca lakṣyante*  
*jyotsnāvatyaḥ kvacid bhuvaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**According to some authorities, the Supreme Lord, the master of sacrifice, exists. This can be observed by the presence of beautiful bodies in this life and the next in some place.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 205:**

O great devotees! The supreme Lord, described in the śruti as the giver of all results of karma (*yajña-patiḥ*) according to persons who know the real meaning of śruti (*keśāñcit*), exists. “That cannot be true because of contrary evidence.” Confirming proof is given by the existence of variety in the universe. For instance, by perception in this life and by scripture which testifies about next life, there is sometimes, not always, beautiful bodies meant for enjoyment on earth (*bhuvaḥ*). By this one can infer the existence of the Lord.

For the same action there is also not different results. Sometimes of course this result is not given perfectly (*kvacit*). It will be arranged by the independent supreme Lord. The version *keṣaṁcit iha sattamā* is not approved by Śrīdhara Svāmī.

## Text 28-29-30

*manor uttānapādasya*  
*dhruvasyāpi mahīpateḥ*  
*priyavratasya rājarṣer*  
*aṅgasyāsmat-pituḥ pituḥ*  
*īdṛśānām athānyeṣām*  
*ajasya ca bhavasya ca*  
*prahlādasya baleś cāpi*  
*kṛtyam asti gadābhṛtā*  
*dauhitrādīn ṛte mṛtyoḥ*  
*śocyān dharma-vimohitān*  
*varga-svargāpavargāṇāṁ*  
*prāyeṇaikātmya-hetunā*

**Перевод:**

**This is also the conclusion of Manu, Uttānapāda, King Dhruva, the saintly king Priyavrata, my grandfather Aṅga, many other great persons, Brahmā, Śiva, Prahlāda and Bali—but not those lamentable persons straying from** ***dharma*** **like Vena. The achievements of** ***artha, dharma, kāma*****, Svarga and** ***mokṣa*** **are generally given by the independent Supreme Lord, holder of the club.**

**Комментарий:**

The wise have realized this also. This is proof. Three verses state this. The grandfather is Aṅga. Prahlāda and Bali are mentioned (though they appeared after the speaker Pṛthu) since they were understood from scriptures.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 205:**

The achievements are produced by the Lord (*gadābhṛtā*). Appearing internally in the heart and externally, the Lord performs necessary actions for them. They also do activities with the Lord and with no one else. Others are criticized. Veṇa and others were bewildered about dharma. The word *gadābhṛtā* indicates Viṣṇu and no one else as the supreme Lord.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 205:**

That Lord is described. He, who is one being in all entities *(ekātmyena*), generally *(prāyeṇa*) bestows the results of artha, dharma and kāma (*varga*), Svarga (the result of dharma)*.* It is said in Skanda Purāṇa:

*bandhako bhava-pāśena bhava-pāśāc ca mocakaḥ \|*  
*kaivalyadaḥ paraṁ brahma viṣṇur eva sanātanaḥ \|\|*

**Eternal Viṣṇu, the supreme Brahman, binds with the ropes of the material world, frees a person from the bondage of world and awards liberation.**

The lines of verse 31 are in reverse order in Citsukha’s and Puṇyāraṇya’s versions. But Śrīdhara Svāmī does not accept this. Citsukha has *ekārtha* instead of *aikātmya.*

### Text 31

*yat-pāda-sevābhirucis tapasvinām*  
*aśeṣa-janmopacitaṁ malaṁ dhiyaḥ*  
*sadyaḥ kṣiṇoty anvaham edhatī satī*  
*yathā padāṅguṣṭha-viniḥsṛtā sarit*

**Перевод:**

**Like the water flowing from the big toe of the Lord, a taste of service to the Lord’s feet immediately destroys the contamination in the intelligence of the performers of austerity, which has been acquired through unlimited births. Day by day that spiritual taste increases.**

**Комментарий:**

Pure devotees are special. This is described in two verse.

### Text 32

*vinirdhutāśeṣa-mano-malaḥ pumān*  
*asaṅga-vijñāna-viśeṣa-vīryavān*  
*yad-aṅghri-mūle kṛta-ketanaḥ punar*  
*na saṁsṛtiṁ kleśa-vahāṁ prapadyate*

**Перевод:**

**One who takes shelter of the Lord’s feet, with mind cleansed of unlimited dirty, and who is strong in detachment from enjoyment and realization of the beauty of the Lord’s form, does not surrender again to material life full of suffering.**

**Комментарий:**

His special knowledge (*vijñāna-viśeṣa*) included realization of the Lord.

### Text 33

*tam eva yūyaṁ bhajatātma-vṛttibhir*  
*mano-vacaḥ-kāya-guṇaiḥ sva-karmabhiḥ*  
*amāyinaḥ kāma-dughāṅghri-paṅkajaṁ*  
*yathādhikārāvasitārtha-siddhayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**You who are sincere and who have determined the goal to be achieved according to your** ***varṇa*** **and** ***āśrama*** **should worship that Lord, whose lotus feet fulfill all desires, by means of your occupational duties, your livelihood, and devotional activities involving the mind voice and body.**

**Комментарий:**

You should worship the Lord according to your qualification, including your prescribed duties.

### Text 34

*asāv ihāneka-guṇo 'guṇo 'dhvaraḥ*  
*pṛthag-vidha-dravya-guṇa-kriyoktibhiḥ*  
*sampadyate 'rthāśaya-liṅga-nāmabhir*  
*viśuddha-vijñāna-ghanaḥ svarūpataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**This sacrifice has many material qualities, such as various ingredients, colors, rituals,** ***mantras,*** **embellishments,** ***saṅkalpas, śaktis*** **and names, but it becomes devoid of material qualities in the end, having a nature of pure, condensed knowledge, Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

The sacrifice is without material qualities (*aguṇaḥ*).

### Text 35

*pradhāna-kālāśaya-dharma-saṅgrahe*  
*śarīra eṣa pratipadya cetanām*  
*kriyā-phalatvena vibhur vibhāvyate*  
*yathānalo dāruṣu tad-guṇātmakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Supplying the intelligence in the body made of** ***prakṛti*****, time, impressions, and fate so that the person offers** ***karmas*** **to the Lord, the Lord himself appears as the various results of those actions, just as fire appears in various types of woods with the qualities of those woods.**

**Комментарий:**

Supplying intelligence (*cetanām*) to the jīva, entering as the monitor, he manifests externally as the result of action, a form of his bliss, by becoming one with his śakti. The verb is reflexive.

### Text 37

*mā jātu tejaḥ prabhaven maharddhibhis*  
*titikṣayā tapasā vidyayā ca*  
*dedīpyamāne 'jita-devatānāṁ*  
*kule svayaṁ rāja-kulād dvijānām*

**Перевод:**

**Because of being in a royal family, because of great wealth, one should not exert power over the Vaiṣṇavas who are shining with tolerance, austerity and knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī mentions brāhmaṇa-bhakti. This actually refers to any Vaiṣṇava, since brāhmaṇas are included. That is understood from his explanation of the two lines.

### Text 38

*brahmaṇya-devaḥ puruṣaḥ purātano*  
*nityaṁ harir yac-caraṇābhivandanāt*  
*avāpa lakṣmīm anapāyinīṁ yaśo*  
*jagat-pavitraṁ ca mahattamāgraṇīḥ*

**Перевод:**

**By worshipping their feet, the Supreme Lord, the greatest, the oldest and the foremost person, the protector of the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **obtained eternal, undying, splendorous fame which purifies the whole universe.**

**Комментарий:**

This is just glorification of the devotees. Since Pṛthu knew the greatness of the Lord he should not have spoken in this way. Becoming absorbed, he praised in this way to teach people.

Or by showing qualities of humility by offering respects to the devotees, he attained undying wealth in the form of being the shelter of all beings. He became the object of surrender for all people. This is like the test of Bhṛgu.

### Text 39

*yat-sevayāśeṣa-guhāśayaḥ sva-rāḍ*  
*vipra-priyas tuṣyati kāmam īśvaraḥ*  
*tad eva tad-dharma-parair vinītaiḥ*  
*sarvātmanā brahma-kulaṁ niṣevyatām*

**Перевод:**

**By service to them, the unlimited Lord, dwelling in the heart, dear to the** ***brāhmaṇas*****, satisfies all desires. Therefore those who are following** ***dharma*** **and good conduct should serve the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **with all the heart.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *hitaṁ sadā* is seen instead of *sarvātmanā*.

### Text 40

*pumāḹ labhetānativelam ātmanaḥ*  
*prasīdato 'tyanta-śamaṁ svataḥ svayam*  
*yan-nitya-sambandha-niṣevayā tataḥ*  
*paraṁ kim atrāsti mukhaṁ havir-bhujām*

**Перевод:**

**By constant service to the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **a person quickly and spontaneously achieves liberation, because of a satisfied mind. What better mouth of the** ***devatās*** **exists?**

**Комментарий:**

This is explained in the next verse.

### Text 41

*aśnāty anantaḥ khalu tattva-kovidaiḥ*  
*śraddhā-hutaṁ yan-mukha ijya-nāmabhiḥ*  
*na vai tathā cetanayā bahiṣ-kṛte*  
*hutāśane pāramahaṁsya-paryaguḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, who is beyond the sense, eats what is offered to the mouth of the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **given with faith and** ***mantras*** **by the learned and does not accept to the same extent what is offered to the fire, which is devoid of consciousness.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is in the highest position. Or the Lord is such that the senses cannot operate independently of him (*paryaguḥ*). Or taking support of the Lord, the senses exist.

### Text 42-43

*yad brahma nityaṁ virajaṁ sanātanaṁ*  
*śraddhā-tapo-maṅgala-mauna-saṁyamaiḥ*  
*samādhinā bibhrati hārtha-dṛṣṭaye*  
*yatredam ādarśa ivāvabhāsate*  
*teṣām ahaṁ pāda-saroja-reṇum*  
*āryā vaheyādhi-kirīṭam āyuḥ*  
*yaṁ nityadā bibhrata āśu pāpaṁ*  
*naśyaty amuṁ sarva-guṇā bhajanti*

**Перевод:**

**Honorable people! Since the** ***brāhmaṇa,*** **by faith, austerity, proper conduct, silence, and senses control, continually supports the eternal, pure Veda in which this universe is reflected as in a mirror, in order to obtain knowledge of the truth about objects, for my whole life I pray to carry on my crown the dust from the lotus feet of the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and Vaiṣṇavas, which quickly destroys sin and awards all good qualities to the bearer.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. The singular for brāhmaṇas is used in the clause for metrical reasons. Citsukha has *yo ’dhikirīṭam āyuḥ.*

### Text 44

*guṇāyanaṁ śīla-dhanaṁ kṛta-jñaṁ*  
*vṛddhāśrayaṁ saṁvṛṇate 'nu sampadaḥ*  
*prasīdatāṁ brahma-kulaṁ gavāṁ ca*  
*janārdanaḥ sānucaraś ca mahyam*

**Перевод:**

**All wealth accrues to the person with good qualities, a wealth of good conduct, and gratitude, who takes shelter of the elders. May the Lord, along with his followers, the** ***brāhmaṇas*****, and cows, be pleased with me!**

**Комментарий:**

A wealth of knowledge etc. *(sampadaḥ*) accrues to the person who takes shelter of even one brāhmaṇa (*vṛddhāśrayam*), who is endowed also endowed with good qualities such as peacefulness (*guṇāśrayam*). The brāhmaṇas are the best since they teach all others as their mercy. May the Lord and his followers, the group of brāhmaṇas and the group of cows (*gavām*), be pleased with me.

### Text 46

*putreṇa jayate lokān*  
*iti satyavatī śrutiḥ*  
*brahma-daṇḍa-hataḥ pāpo*  
*yad veno 'tyatarat tamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Vedas speak the truth when they say “The father attains the highest planets through the son” since the sinful Vena, killed by the curse of** ***brāhmaṇas*****, was delivered from hell by his son.**

**Комментарий:**

The father attains the highest planet by a good son. Before the birth of Pṛthu, Vena had to suffer for some days in hell.

### Text 47

*hiraṇyakaśipuś cāpi*  
*bhagavan-nindayā tamaḥ*  
*vivikṣur atyagāt sūnoḥ*  
*prahlādasyānubhāvataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Hiraṇyakaśipu also, who entered hell because of criticizing the Lord, was delivered because of his son Prahlāda.**

**Комментарий:**

Hiraṇyakaśipu was delivered because of the power of bhakti (*anubhāvataḥ*) of Prahlāda, caused by the appearance of Narasiṁha. There are other causes of deliverance in other avatāras like Varāha, but here the son was the cause.

### Text 49

*aho vayaṁ hy adya pavitra-kīrte*  
*tvayaiva nāthena mukunda-nāthāḥ*  
*ya uttamaślokatamasya viṣṇor*  
*brahmaṇya-devasya kathāṁ vyanakti*

**Перевод:**

**Most glorious Pṛthu! Today we have become the possessors of a master, because you have taken the role of our master. You have explained all about the most praised Supreme Lord, affectionate to the** ***brāhmaṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

Some versions have *vyanakṣi.* Citsukha has *vyanakṣīḥ.*

## Chapter Twenty-two

### Text 2

*tāṁs tu siddheśvarān rājā*  
*vyomno 'vatarato 'rciṣā*  
*lokān apāpān kurvāṇān*  
*sānugo 'caṣṭa lakṣitān*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu and his followers saw the lords of mystic power descending from the sky, purifying all the people by their effulgence.**

**Комментарий:**

They purified all people by their effulgence. Citsukha has *kurvatyaḥ* and others have *kurvāṇān.*

### Text 3

*tad-darśanodgatān prāṇān*  
*pratyāditsur ivotthitaḥ*  
*sa-sadasyānugo vainya*  
*indriyeśo guṇān iva*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu, along with the assembly and his followers, rose to his feet, as if eager to regain his life airs which had exited his body on seeing the Kumāras, as eager as the** ***jīva*** **following sense objects.**

**Комментарий:**

*Pratyāditsuḥ* is Citsukha’s version. Others have *pratyāpitsuḥ.*

### Text 8

*kiṁ tasya durlabhataram*  
*iha loke paratra ca*  
*yasya viprāḥ prasīdanti*  
*śivo viṣṇuś ca sānugaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**What cannot be achieved in the world and the next by a person who gains the grace of the** ***brāhmaṇas*****, Śiva and Viṣṇu along with their followers?**

**Комментарий:**

They were not ordinary brāhmaṇas. The general case is stated however (*viprāḥ*).

### Text 9

*naiva lakṣayate loko*  
*lokān paryaṭato 'pi yān*  
*yathā sarva-dṛśaṁ sarva*  
*ātmānaṁ ye 'sya hetavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**People do not see you, though you travel in all the planets, just as all people, and even Brahmā and other creators of the universe, do not see Paramātmā, the witness of all things.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse refers to *vaḥ* (you) in verse 7. People do not see you just as Brahmā and others do not see Paramātmā (*ātmānam*). This indicates that the Lord has a form.

### Text 10

*adhanā api te dhanyāḥ*  
*sādhavo gṛha-medhinaḥ*  
*yad-gṛhā hy arha-varyāmbu-*  
*tṛṇa-bhūmīśvarāvarāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Poor but saintly householders whose houses give worthy reception to devotees with water, grass, earth, husband and family, are fortunate.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *tṛṇabhūmi suvāgdhārāḥ.* The following verse is also sometimes seen here.

*phala-mulais tṛṇair vāpi vāgbhir adbhir vilocanaiḥ*  
*pūjyante tīrthinaḥ sadbhir vilupta-vibhavair api*

Śrīdhara Svāmī in his commetary mentions ten verses spoken by Pṛthu but does not comment on verse 8, though there should be a commentary. He refers to verse 7 in commenting on verse 9.

### Text 11

*vyālālaya-drumā vai te’ pi*  
*ariktākhila-sampadaḥ*  
*yad-gṛhās tīrtha-pādīya-*  
*pādatīrtha-vivarjitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The houses of those who have all wealth but which are devoid of water for washing the feet of Vaiṣṇavas and which give shelter to sharp-tongued wives and sons are like trees giving shelter to snakes.**

**Комментарий:**

Having praised respect for brāhmaṇas, he now praises respect for devotees. *Drumā hy ete* is sometimes seen instead of *drumā vai te’pi.*

### Text 12

*svāgataṁ vo dvija-śreṣṭhā*  
*yad-vratāni mumukṣavaḥ*  
*caranti śraddhayā dhīrā*  
*bālā eva bṛhanti ca*

**Перевод:**

**Welcome to you! You are the best of the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **because, desiring liberation, you have undergone** ***brahmacārī*** **vows with faith, and though you are boys, you have achieved liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Though they are devotees, Pṛthu understood that they had a predominance of jñāna. Thus he praises them for that portion of their realization in three verses. Starting as infants (*balāḥ*) you undertook great vows (*bṛhanti*) of brahmacārya.

### Text 15

*tad ahaṁ kṛta-viśrambhaḥ*  
*suhṛdo vas tapasvinām*  
*sampṛcche bhava etasmin*  
*kṣemaḥ kenāñjasā bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, with confidence, I ask you well-wishers by what means will there be immediate benefit for those suffering in this world.**

**Комментарий:**

Pṛthu previously revealed his pure bhakti:

*na kāmaye nātha tad apy ahaṁ kvacin*  
*na yatra yuṣmac-caraṇāmbujāsavaḥ*  
*mahattamāntar-hṛdayān mukha-cyuto*  
*vidhatsva karṇāyutam eṣa me varaḥ*

**I do not desire anything, and especially I do not want liberation, in which there is no nectar of your lotus feet, coming from the hearts of great devotees and falling from their mouths. Give me millions of ears. That is my request. (SB** [**4.20.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.24)**)**

Just by hearing the Lord’s pastimes one gains the proper knowledge:

*sa uttamaśloka mahan-mukha-cyuto*  
*bhavat-padāmbhoja-sudhā kaṇānilaḥ*  
*smṛtiṁ punar vismṛta-tattva-vartmanāṁ*  
*kuyogināṁ no vitaraty alaṁ varaiḥ*

**O most praised Lord! The topics falling from the mouths of the great devotees, or even the wind touching a drop of nectar from your lotus feet, brings remembrance even for the bad** ***yogīs*** **who have forgotten the truth you have just taught. Away with all these benedictions including liberation! (SB** [**4.20.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.25)**)**

He accepted the Lord’s instructions with reverence:

*sa itthaṁ loka-guruṇā viṣvaksenena viśva-jit*  
*anuśāsita ādeśaṁ śirasā jagṛhe hareḥ*

**Maitreya said: When so instructed by the** ***guru*** **of the universe, the Supreme Lord, Pṛthu accepted the instructions on his head. (SB** [**4.20.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.17)**)**

He acted properly, respecting great devotees:

*tat-pāda-śauca-salilair mārjitālaka-bandhanaḥ*  
*tatra śīlavatāṁ vṛttam ācaran mānayann iva*

**He sprinkled water that had washed their feet on his head. He acted according to the conduct of the well-bred, as if teaching others. (SB** [**4.22.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/22.5)**)**

Now he was eager to hear knowledge.

### Text 16

*vyaktam ātmavatām ātmā*  
*bhagavān ātma-bhāvanaḥ*  
*svānām anugrahāyemāṁ*  
*siddha-rūpī caraty ajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**It is clear that the unborn Supreme Lord, who reveals himself to the world, who is the object of affection for the devotees, moves about the universe in his spiritual form to show mercy to his devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

He shows that the devotee and the Lord who is non-different from the devotee are causes of knowledge.

### Text 17

*maitreya uvāca*  
*pṛthos tat sūktam ākarṇya*  
*sāraṁ suṣṭhu mitaṁ madhu*  
*smayamāna iva prītyā*  
*kumāraḥ pratyuvāca ha*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of Pṛthu, which were logical, deep in meaning, concise, and sweet, the Kumāras, with joyful faces, answered with affection.**

**Комментарий:**

Heaing his excellent words (*sūktam*), which indicated his perfect knowledge, Sanatkumāra answered.

### Text 18

*sanat-kumāra uvāca*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭaṁ mahārāja*  
*sarva-bhūta-hitātmanā*  
*bhavatā viduṣā cāpi*  
*sādhūnāṁ matir īdṛśī*

**Перевод:**

**Sanatkumāra said: O King!, You, who are learned and who think of the welfare of all beings, have asked well. The minds of devotees are of this nature.**

**Комментарий:**

Pṛthu stated two causes of knowledge in his questions. Sanatkumāra now gives another reason, benefitting others, related to the Lord, while showing great respect. The devotees give jñāna to those so inclined, in an explicit manner and give bhakti to persons like Pṛthu, in a hidden manner. That is clear in Śrīdhara Svāmī’s commentary.

### Text 19

*saṅgamaḥ khalu sādhūnām*  
*ubhayeṣāṁ ca sammataḥ*  
*yat-sambhāṣaṇa-sampraśnaḥ*  
*sarveṣāṁ vitanoti śam*

**Перевод:**

**The assembly of devotees, of hearers and listeners, is desirable, because the discussion and answers spread benefit to both parties.**

**Комментарий:**

What can be said? The associating of devotees like you is approved by men. It gives the highest happiness—to both the speaker and the hearers, because of questions and answers.

### Text 20

*asty eva rājan bhavato madhudviṣaḥ*  
*pādāravindasya guṇānuvādane*  
*ratir durāpā vidhunoti naiṣṭhikī*  
*kāmaṁ kaṣāyaṁ malam antar-ātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! You have rare, fixed attachment to discussing about the qualities of the lotus feet of the Lord. That washes away the contamination of lust within the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

You have excellent rati for the Lord. This is better than the knowledge that we will teach. The teachings will destroy impurities like desire by practice, but that has already been done.

### Text 21

*śāstreṣv iyān eva suniścito nṛṇāṁ*  
*kṣemasya sadhryag-vimṛśeṣu hetuḥ*  
*asaṅga ātma-vyatirikta ātmani*  
*dṛḍhā ratir brahmaṇi nirguṇe ca yā*

**Перевод:**

**It is determined in the scriptures through thorough deliberation that the only cause of benefit for the human being is detachment from the body and attachment to the Supreme Lord who has no material qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

That statement is linked with this verse. The cause of benefit is attachment for nirguṇa Brahman, what to speak of the Lord who attracts even ātmāraṁas by his unlimited, spiritual, and most auspicious qualities.

### Text 22

*sā śraddhayā bhagavad-dharma-caryayā*  
*jijñāsayādhyātmika-yoga-niṣṭhayā*  
*yogeśvaropāsanayā ca nityaṁ*  
*puṇya-śravaḥ-kathayā puṇyayā ca*

**Перевод:**

**Attachment for the Lord takes place by faith, by being inquisitive about the process of** ***bhakti*****, by steadiness in** ***ātmā-yoga,*** **by worship of the great** ***yogīs,*** **and by pure discussions about the Lord on a daily basis.**

**Комментарий:**

Four verses explain attachment to the Lord.

### Text 23

*arthendriyārāma-sagoṣṭhy-atṛṣṇayā*  
*tat-sammatānām aparigraheṇa ca*  
*vivikta-rucyā paritoṣa ātmani*  
*vinā harer guṇa-pīyūṣa-pānāt*

**Перевод:**

**Attachment to the Lord takes place by disgust with material friendship related to persons who accumulate wealth or enjoy the senses, by not accepting those goals, and by a taste for solitary living, with satisfaction in the self, as long as one cannot relish the nectar of the Lord’s qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

As long as one cannot relish topics of the Lord, the other elements assist in developing attachment of the Lord.

### Text 24

*ahiṁsayā pāramahaṁsya-caryayā*  
*smṛtyā mukundācaritāgrya-sīdhunā*  
*yamair akāmair niyamaiś cāpy anindayā*  
*nirīhayā dvandva-titikṣayā ca*

**Перевод:**

**Attachment to the Lord appears by non-violence, by practices leading to a peaceful mind, by tasting the sweetness of the excellent pastimes of the Lord, by practicing yama** **and** ***niyama*** **with no material desires, by not criticizing others, by living simply and by tolerating dualities.**

**Комментарий:**

One does not criticize persons following scriptures. His statement also indicates meditation on the Lord.

### Text 25

*harer muhus tatpara-karṇa-pūra-*  
*guṇābhidhānena vijṛmbhamāṇayā*  
*bhaktyā hy asaṅgaḥ sad-asaty anātmani*  
*syān nirguṇe brahmaṇi cāñjasā ratiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Attachment to the nirguṇa Brahman and detachment from the good and bad of the material world quickly appear by constantly discussing with increasing** ***bhakti*** **the Lord’s qualities which are pleasing to the devotee’s ear.**

**Комментарий:**

If the qualities of the Lord have such effect, that should be the goal. Why is brahma-jñāna indicated as the sādhana? That is desired by persons desiring liberation, not by persons who desire the supreme goal. That is explained in this verse.

There will be detachment (*asaṅgaḥ*) and attachment to nirguṇa Brahman by bhakti alone, endowed with the desire for liberation, filled with descriptions (by someone else or for someone else) of the Lord’s qualities which fill the ears or are like ornaments for the devotee’s (*tat-para*) ears and which destroy everything else.

### Text 31

*bhraśyaty anusmṛtiś cittaṁ*  
*jñāna-bhraṁśaḥ smṛti-kṣaye*  
*tad-rodhaṁ kavayaḥ prāhur*  
*ātmāpahnavam ātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When ability to discriminate is lost, memory becomes destroyed. With loss of memory, knowledge is destroyed. The wise say that destruction of knowledge is called destruction of the self.**

**Комментарий:**

For the ātmā with upādhis (*ātmana*ḥ), the ātmā without upādhis becomes invisible (*ātmāpahnavam*).

### Text 33

*arthendriyārthābhidhyānaṁ*  
*sarvārthāpahnavo nṛṇām*  
*bhraṁśito jñāna-vijñānād*  
*yenāviśati mukhyatām*

**Перевод:**

**Always thinking of wealth and sense objects is the destruction of all goals, and by this, after having destroyed** ***jñāna*** **and** ***vijñāna,*** **one attains immovable life forms.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *atha* instead of *artha.*

### Text 34

*na kuryāt karhicit saṅgaṁ*  
*tamas tīvraṁ titīriṣuḥ*  
*dharmārtha-kāma-mokṣāṇāṁ*  
*yad atyanta-vighātakam*

**Перевод:**

**Those who desire to cross over the intense ignorance of** ***saṁsāra*** **should never have attachment to things which completely obstruct** ***dharma, artha, kāma*** **and** ***mokṣa.***

**Комментарий:**

Dharma, artha and kāma which are favorable for liberation are accepted, for it is said:

*harmasya hy āpavargyasya nārtho ’rthāyopakalpate \|*  
*nārthasya dharmaikāntasya kāmo lābhāya hi smṛtaḥ \|\|*

**Material results are not suitable as the goal for the person dedicated to higher spiritual goals. Attainment of material assets is not the desire of the person who is dedicated to the higher path. (SB** [**1.2.9**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.9)**)**

Sometimes *titīrṣuḥ* is seen instead of *titīriṣuḥ*. The Gauḍiyas accept *vighātanam*, but this is not accepted by Citsukha.

### Text 36

*pare 'vare ca ye bhāvā*  
*guṇa-vyatikarād anu*  
*na teṣāṁ vidyate kṣemam*  
*īśa-vidhvaṁsitāśiṣām*

**Перевод:**

**There is nothing auspicious for those whose goals are destroyed by time, and who only come into existence as Brahmā or Indra after the agitation of the** ***guṇas*****.**

**Комментарий:**

The reason for accepting liberation is given. Sambandhokti has *pare ‘vare* and Citsukha has *parāvare.*

### Text 37

*tat tvaṁ narendra jagatām atha tasthūṣāṁ ca*  
*dehendriyāsu-dhiṣaṇātmabhir āvṛtānām*  
*yaḥ kṣetravit-tapatayā hṛdi viśvag āviḥ*  
*pratyak cakāsti bhagavāṁs tam avehi so 'smi*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Therefore, for liberation, know the Paramātmā who shines in the hearts of all moving and non-moving beings covered by bodies, senses, life airs, intelligence and false ego, and who appears everywhere as the controller of the** ***jīva.*** **One should meditate, “I am he.”**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *anāvṛtānām* instead of *āvṛtānām.* Sambandhokti has *kṣetra-vit-tapatā* instead of *tapatayā*. One should meditate that one is an aṁśa of the Lord (*so ‘smi*) or that the Lord is eternally the basis of the self. This is to indicate that the Lord is the shelter of supreme prema. Another version has *so ‘asti*. This has the same meaning, combined with the phrase “dwelling in the heart as the controller of the jīva (*kṣetra-vit-tapatayā*).” The jīva is not imaginary like a pillar mistaken for a man, as in vivarta-vāda.

### Text 38

*yasminn idaṁ sad-asad-ātmatayā vibhāti*  
*māyā viveka-vidhuti sraji vāhi-buddhiḥ*  
*taṁ nitya-mukta-pariśuddha-viśuddha-tattvaṁ*  
*pratyūḍha-karma-kalila-prakṛtiṁ prapadye*

**Перевод:**

**I surrender to Paramātmā who defeats material energy contaminated by** ***karma,*** **who is ever liberated, completely pure, the highest truth, and within whom** ***māyā*** **manifests this universe as cause and effect, superior and inferior, but which can be dissolved by discrimination. Some claim the world is an illusion, like thinking a garland is a snake, and that this illusion is dissipated by knowledge of oneness.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse establishes the nature of the Lord (not as a jīva), as indicated by *so ‘asti.*

### Text 39

*yat-pāda-paṅkaja-palāśa-vilāsa-bhaktyā*  
*karmāśayaṁ grathitam udgrathayanti santaḥ*  
*tadvan na rikta-matayo yatayo 'pi ruddha-*  
*sroto-gaṇās tam araṇaṁ bhaja vāsudevam*

**Перевод:**

**Worship Vāsudeva, the shelter. By devotion to his lotus toes possessing ever-increasing beauty, the devotees untie the knot of false ego filled with impressions of** ***karma*****, which the foolish** ***sannyāsīs*** **who try to stop the senses flowing like a river cannot untie.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhakti to the Lord, which you desire, is the highest process of all. That is expressed in two verses. Citsukha has *tad-vimatayaḥ* instead of *rikta-matayaḥ.*

### Text 41

*maitreya uvāca*  
*sa evaṁ brahma-putreṇa*  
*kumāreṇātma-medhasā*  
*darśitātma-gatiḥ samyak*  
*praśasyovāca taṁ nṛpaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Pṛthu, having been thoroughly shown all about** ***ātmā*** **by Sanatkumāra, the son of Brahmā, who was well-versed in knowledge of ātmā, spoke in order to praising him.**

**Комментарий:**

Pṛthu was shown his goal (*ātma-gatiḥ*), forms of the Lord, by Sanatkumāra, who had knowledge of ātmā (*ātma-medhasā*).

### Text 42

*rājovāca*  
*kṛto me 'nugrahaḥ pūrvaṁ*  
*hariṇārtānukampinā*  
*tam āpādayituṁ brahman*  
*bhagavan yūyam āgatāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The King said: Previously the Lord, compassionate to the distressed, was merciful to me. O** ***brāhmana!*** **O Lord Sanatkumāra! You have come to fulfill that mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

He is happy to obtain teachings from a person empowered by the Lord. Those teachings indicate establishing internal bhakti for the Lord.

### Text 43

*niṣpāditaś ca kārtsnyena*  
*bhagavadbhir ghṛṇālubhiḥ*  
*sādhūcchiṣṭaṁ hi me sarvam*  
*ātmanā saha kiṁ dade*

**Перевод:**

**Everything here comes from the merciful devotees. How can I give anything I possess, since it is all remnants, including my body?**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *niṣpāditam* instead of *nispāditaḥ* and *ghṛṇālubhiḥ* instead of *ghṛṇātmabhiḥ.* Everything is remnants of the devotees of the Lord such as Bhṛgu, after the sacrifice. If I give it, it is my remnants. That is disrespectful. How can I give it to you?

### Text 44

*prāṇā dārāḥ sutā brahman*  
*gṛhāś ca sa-paricchadāḥ*  
*rājyaṁ balaṁ mahī kośa*  
*iti sarvaṁ niveditam*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! I offered everything—life airs, wives, sons, houses, furniture, kingdom, strength, the earth, and the treasury.**

**Комментарий:**

Everything is given to the devotees as an offering (*niveditam*) since they previously gave me life airs, sons etc.

### Text 45

*sainā-patyaṁ ca rājyaṁ ca*  
*daṇḍa-netṛtvam eva ca*  
*sarva lokādhipatyaṁ ca*  
*veda-śāstra-vid arhati*

**Перевод:**

**The knower of Vedic scriptures should accept to be head of the army, leader of the country, the punisher, and ruler of all planets.**

**Комментарий:**

The reason is given.

### Text 46

*svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte*  
*svaṁ vaste svaṁ dadāti ca*  
*tasyaivānugraheṇānnaṁ*  
*bhuñjate kṣatriyādayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Since the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **enjoys, wears and gives what is his, the** ***kṣatriyas, vaiśyas*** **and** ***śūdras*** **enjoy food by the mercy of the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **alone.**

**Комментарий:**

Everything should be given by the order of the brāhmaṇa. To do otherwise is a fault.

### Text 47

*yair īdṛśī bhagavato gatir ātma-vāda*  
*ekāntato nigamibhiḥ pratipāditā naḥ*  
*tuṣyantv adabhra-karuṇāḥ sva-kṛtena nityaṁ*  
*ko nāma tat pratikaroti vinoda-pātram*

**Перевод:**

**The truth concerning the Supreme Lord has been completely presented to us by knowers of the Vedas with deliberation on the** ***ātmā.*** **These saints of unlimited mercy should always be satisfied with their acts of uplifting the fallen. Who can repay them for their service? One can only offer water with folded hands.**

**Комментарий:**

The merciful devotees should be satisfied with their acts (*sva-kṛtena*) of delivering the fallen, which is their nature. One can only become the laughing stock of others in trying to repay the debt.

### Text 48

*maitreya uvāca*  
*ta ātma-yoga-pataya*  
*ādi-rājena pūjitāḥ*  
*śīlaṁ tadīyaṁ śaṁsantaḥ*  
*khe 'bhavan miṣatāṁ nṛṇām*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: The masters of** ***ātma-yoga,*** **after being worshipped by Pṛthu, and praising his character, went to Satyaloka through the sky while the people watched.**

**Комментарий:**

Some versions do not have *maitreya uvāca.* Citsukha has *yoga-gatayaḥ.*

### Text 49

*vainyas tu dhuryo mahatāṁ*  
*saṁsthityādhyātma-śikṣayā*  
*āpta-kāmam ivātmānaṁ*  
*mena ātmany avasthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Pṛthu, chief of the great souls, considered himself almost satisfied by the complete teachings about** ***ātmā,*** **but he remained situated in his nature of pure** ***bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

The word *iva* indicates that he only seemed to satisfied by the teachings about ātmā, since he was only satisfied with bhakti, since he was a pure devotee.

### Text 50

*karmāṇi ca yathā-kālaṁ*  
*yathā-deśaṁ yathā-balam*  
*yathocitaṁ yathā-vittam*  
*akarod brahma-sāt-kṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**He also (besides** ***bhakti*****) performed prescribed** ***varṇāśrama*** **duties according to time, place, qualified person, and wealth, as suitable to his** ***bhakti*****, thinking of instructions about ātmā.**

**Комментарий:**

Several verses describe his actions. The word *ca* indicates that the word *iva* from the previous verse applies to this verse also. He did his actions to teach the population, respecting the order of guru who had taught him about ātmā (*brahma-sāt-krtam*). Or he did his actions as an offering to Bhagavān.

### Text 51-52

*phalaṁ brahmaṇi sannyasya*  
*nirviṣaṅgaḥ samāhitaḥ*  
*karmādhyakṣaṁ ca manvāna*  
*ātmānaṁ prakṛteḥ param*  
*gṛheṣu vartamāno 'pi*  
*sa sāmrājya-śriyānvitaḥ*  
*nāsajjatendriyārtheṣu*  
*niraham-matir arkavat*

**Перевод:**

**Offering all results to Brahman, being unattached, attentive, recognizing the** ***antaryāmī*** **as the controller of** ***prakṛti,*** **being free false ego, like the sun, he was not contaminated by sense objects, though he remained a householder with kingdom and wealth**

**Комментарий:**

Though situated in the house he was not attached to sense objects. This is not related to the previous lines since his household life with sons like Vijitāśva was previous. Pṛthu had already achieved full knowledge by hearing the pastimes of the Lord.

*sa uttamaśloka mahan-mukha-cyuto*  
*bhavat-padāmbhoja-sudhā kaṇānilaḥ*  
*smṛtiṁ punar vismṛta-tattva-vartmanāṁ*  
*kuyogināṁ no vitaraty alaṁ varaiḥ*

**O most praised Lord! The topics falling from the mouths of the great devotees, or even the wind touching a drop of nectar from your lotus feet, brings remembrance even for the bad** ***yogīs*** **who have forgotten the truth you have just taught. Away with all these benedictions including liberation! (SB** [**4.20.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.24)**)**

*tvan-māyayāddhā jana īśa khaṇḍito*  
*yad anyad āśāsta ṛtātmano 'budhaḥ*  
*yathā cared bāla-hitaṁ pitā svayaṁ*  
*tathā tvam evārhasi naḥ samīhitum*

**O Lord! Since foolish people, injured by your** ***māyā*****, desire things other than worshipping you, you should choose (without asking us) what is for our benefit, just as a father acts for the benefit of his son. (SB** [**4.20.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.31)**)**

*ity ādi-rājena nutaḥ sa viśva-dṛk*  
*tam āha rājan mayi bhaktir astu te*  
*diṣṭyedṛśī dhīr mayi te kṛtā yayā*  
*māyāṁ madīyāṁ tarati sma dustyajām*

**After being praised by Pṛthu in this way, the Lord, seer of the whole universe, said, “O King! May you have** ***bhakti*** **to me! By good fortune you have placed in me fine intelligence by which one can cross insurmountable** ***māyā.*** **(SB** [**4.20.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/20.32)**)**

Though he was given duties, bhakti was confirmed by the Lord. This was also confirmed by the Kumāras:

*yat-pāda-paṅkaja-palāśa-vilāsa-bhaktyā*  
*karmāśayaṁ grathitam udgrathayanti santaḥ*  
*tadvan na rikta-matayo yatayo 'pi ruddha-*  
*sroto-gaṇās tam araṇaṁ bhaja vāsudevam*

**Worship Vāsudeva, the shelter. By devotion to his lotus toes possessing ever-increasing beauty, the devotees untie the knot of false ego filled with impressions of** ***karma*****, which the foolish** ***sannyāsīs*** **who try to stop the senses flowing like a river cannot untie. (SB** [**4.22.39**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/22.39)**)**

Thus the prescriptions of adhyātma-yoga were used for rejection of upādhis only. Bhakti without material desires was the real recommendation as previously.

Judging the cause by the result, he went to Vaikuṇṭha at the end. Thus he performed bhakti.

*yac ca vrajanty animiṣām ṛṣabhānuvṛttyā*  
*dūre yamā hy upari naḥ spṛhaṇīya-śīlāḥ*  
*bhartur mithaḥ suyaśasaḥ kathanānurāga-*  
*vaiklavya-bāṣpa-kalayā pulakī-kṛtāṅgāḥ*

**Those who are far above rules by following the Lord with dedication, who are superior to us, and whose qualities are desirable by us, and whose bodies develop goose bumps and uncontrolled tears through attraction to discussions of the Lord possessing most excellent qualities, enter Vaikuṇṭha. (SB** [**3.15.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.25)**)**

Thus the opinion of the Lord and the author’s opinion, by the order of guru, is confirmed.

### Text 53

*evam adhyātma-yogena*  
*karmāṇy anusamācaran*  
*putrān utpādayām āsa*  
*pañcārciṣy ātma-sammatān*

**Перевод:**

**Performing his** ***karmas*** **without attachment, he produced five sons in his wife Arci, who were esteemed like him.**

**Комментарий:**

Producing sons was done previously.

### Text 54

*vijitāśvaṁ dhūmrakeśaṁ*  
*haryakṣaṁ draviṇaṁ vṛkam*  
*sarveṣāṁ loka-pālānāṁ*  
*dadhāraikaḥ pṛthur guṇān*

**Перевод:**

**The sons were Vijitāśva, Dhūmrakeśa, Haryakṣa, Draviṇa and Vṛka. Pṛthu embodied all the qualities of the** ***devatās.***

**Комментарий:**

Vijitāśva was born previously. As previously, he was praised by the vandis. His existence continued at all times.

### Text 63

*kīrtyordhva-gītayā pumbhis*  
*trailokye tatra tatra ha*  
*praviṣṭaḥ karṇa-randhreṣu*  
*strīṇāṁ rāmaḥ satām iva*

**Перевод:**

**His fame, declared loudly by the men and women in the three worlds, entered the ears of devotees just as Rāma’s fame entered the ears of the devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

The glorification by singing, by men and by chaste women, going to the highest places, entered the ears in the three world.

## Chapter Twenty-three

### Text 1-3

*maitreya uvāca*  
*dṛṣṭvātmānaṁ pravayasam*  
*ekadā vainya ātmavān*  
*ātmanā vardhitāśeṣa-*  
*svānusargaḥ prajāpatiḥ*  
*jagatas tasthuṣaś cāpi*  
*vṛttido dharma-bhṛt satām*  
*niṣpāditeśvarādeśo*  
*yad-artham iha jajñivān*  
*ātmajeṣv ātmajāṁ nyasya*  
*virahād rudatīm iva*  
*prajāsu vimanaḥsv ekaḥ*  
*sa-dāro 'gāt tapo-vanam*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Once, seeing his body age, Pṛthu, protector of the people, maintainer of all moving and non-moving beings, who was in control of his mind, who had by himself increased the wealth of the state, and who was the upholder of** ***dharma*** **for the devotees, after having fulfilled the order of the Lord, having appeared on earth for that purpose, handed over to his sons the earth, who was lamenting out of separation from him, and went to the forest alone with his wife, while the citizens grieved.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe Pṛthu’s renunciation. He was actually an eternal associate of the Lord, and thus his following dharma and jñāna was an imitation for carrying the order of the Lord and his acceptance of a human role was for pastimes. Carrying out the order of the Lord was to teach the common people. It is clearly established that he was a pure devotee of the Lord. The citizens were confused because of crying (*vimanaḥsu*).

### Text 4

*tatrāpy adābhya-niyamo*  
*vaikhānasa-susammate*  
*ārabdha ugra-tapasi*  
*yathā sva-vijaye purā*

**Перевод:**

**There, he underwent severe austerities according to the** ***vānaprastha*** **rules, with unbroken vows, using the same effort with which he previously achieved victory over the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

*Adābhya* means free of deceit. Sambandhokti has *adhyātma.* Citsukha has *ātma-niyamān.*

### Text 5

*kanda-mūla-phalāhāraḥ*  
*śuṣka-parṇāśanaḥ kvacit*  
*ab-bhakṣaḥ katicit pakṣān*  
*vāyu-bhakṣas tataḥ param*

**Перевод:**

**He ate only roots like radish and fruits, and sometimes ate only dry leaves. He began drinking only water without food for several months at a time. Finally he only consumed only air.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe his conduct.

### Text 6

*grīṣme pañca-tapā vīro*
