*varṣāsv āsāraṣāṇ muniḥ*  
*ākaṇṭha-magnaḥ śiśire*  
*udake sthaṇḍile-śayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In the summer he placed fires around himself in the four directions, with the sun burning overhead. During the monsoon season he tolerated the torrents of rain. During the dewy season he stood up to his neck in cold water and slept on the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *vīraḥ* sometimes *dhīraḥ* is seen.

### Text 7

*titikṣur yata-vāg dānta*  
*ūrdhva-retā jitānilaḥ*  
*ārirādhayiṣuḥ kṛṣṇam*  
*acarat tapa uttamam*

**Перевод:**

**Being tolerant, controlling words and senses, controlling sexual impulse and his life airs, his only desire being to worship Kṛṣṇa, he performed the highest austerities.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *jītānilaḥ* sometimes *yatānilaḥ* is seen. He desired to worship Kṛṣṇa, following the order of the Lord, since he had appeared to preach in this manner.

### Text 8

*tena kramānusiddhena*  
*dhvasta-karmāmalāśayaḥ*  
*prāṇāyāmaiḥ sanniruddha-*  
*ṣaḍ-vargaś chinna-bandhanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**By gradual perfection of austerities he destroyed** ***karma,*** **developed a joyful heart, controlled the mind and sense by** ***prāṇāyāma,*** **and cut his bondage to the material world.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Instead of *siddhena* sometimes *aviruddhena* is seen. Destroying karma is merely imitation (since he had no karma).

### Text 9

*sanat-kumāro bhagavān*  
*yad āhādhyātmikaṁ param*  
*yogaṁ tenaiva puruṣam*  
*abhajat puruṣarṣabhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The best of men worshipped the Supreme Lord by the highest process called** ***adhyātma-yoga*** **(*****bhakti-yoga*****) which Sanatkumāra had taught.**

**Комментарий:**

He imitated jñāna as he did karma, in order to worship the Lord.

### Text 10

*bhagavad-dharmiṇaḥ sādhoḥ*  
*śraddhayā yatataḥ sadā*  
*bhaktir bhagavati brahmaṇy*  
*ananya-viṣayābhavat*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly endeavoring with faith, dedicated to the Lord, Pṛthu performed** ***bhakti*** **to the Supreme Lord alone.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of that was bhakti. Instead of *sadā* sometimes *tadā* is seen.

### Text 11

*tasyānayā bhagavataḥ parikarma-śuddha-*  
*sattvātmanas tad-anusaṁsmaraṇānupūrtyā*  
*jñānaṁ viraktimad abhūn niśitena yena*  
*ciccheda saṁśaya-padaṁ nija-jīva-kośam*

**Перевод:**

**By** ***bhakti*** **filled with continual remembrance of the Lord, Pṛthu, his mind becoming** ***śuddha-sattva*** **by service, developed intense** ***jñāna*** **endowed with detachment, by which he destroyed the subtle body, which was a cause of doubt for Pṛthu.**

**Комментарий:**

By such practice of bhakti, with jñāna and vairagya, he gave up upādhis. Citsukha has *anupūrva* instead of *anupūrtyā.*

### Text 12

*chinnānya-dhīr adhigatātma-gatir nirīhas*  
*tat tatyaje 'cchinad idaṁ vayunena yena*  
*tāvan na yoga-gatibhir yatir apramatto*  
*yāvad gadāgraja-kathāsu ratiṁ na kuryāt*

**Перевод:**

**Having destroyed the concept of being his body, having realizing the form of Paramātmā, and being without desire for** ***siddhis*****, Pṛthu destroyed his subtle body and then rejected** ***jñāna.*** **As long as one does not have attraction for the topics of the Lord, the** ***jñānī*** **will be attached to** ***yoga siddhis*** **and liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

He had destroyed the upādhis in his understanding (*chinnānya-dhīḥ*). He had knowledge of his form as an associate of the Lord (*adhigatātma-gatiḥ*). He was devoid of any other desire (*nirīhaḥ*). By that knowledge he destroyed doubts and then rejected jñāna. He did not perform jñāna-yoga. By bestowal of knowledge of his spiritual form he was able to attain the goal of bhakti. If one does not have natural attraction (*ratiḥ*) for topics of the Lord, then one has attraction for yoga goals. The goal of Vaikuṇṭha is not like the yoga siddhis.

### Text 13

*evaṁ sa vīra-pravaraḥ*  
*saṁyojyātmānam ātmani*  
*brahma-bhūto dṛḍhaṁ kāle*  
*tatyāja svaṁ kalevaram*

**Перевод:**

**The best of heroes, joining his determined mind with his spiritual body and becoming purely spiritual, gave up his body at that time.**

### Text 14

*sampīḍya pāyuṁ pārṣṇibhyāṁ*  
*vāyum utsārayañ chanaiḥ*  
*nābhyāṁ koṣṭheṣv avasthāpya*  
*hṛd-uraḥ-kaṇṭha-śīrṣaṇi*

**Перевод:**

**Pressing the anus with both ankles, he gradually led the air from the** ***mūlādhara-cakra*** **to the** ***svādhiṣṭhāna-cakra, maṇipūraka-cakra, anāhata-cakra, viśuddhi*****-*****cakra*** **and** ***ājñā-cakra*****.**

**Комментарий:**

He gave up contemplation and directly freed himself from his body. Sometimes *dhīra* is seen instead of *vīra.* He merged his mind (*ātmānam*) into his form as an associate (*ātmani*). Being a pure spiritual form (*brahma-bhūtaḥ*), at the time that the Lord desires (*kale*), determined to not take birth again, he gave up the upādhi which he had accepted (*kalevaram*). Three verses describe the process. Citsukha has *utsarpan* instead of *utsārayan*.

### Text 18

*taṁ sarva-guṇa-vinyāsaṁ*  
*jīve māyāmaye nyadhāt*  
*taṁ cānuśayam ātma-stham*  
*asāv anuśayī pumān*  
*jñāna-vairāgya-vīryeṇa*  
*svarūpa-stho 'jahāt prabhuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He placed the** ***mahat-tattva*** **into** ***prakṛti*****, the covering on the** ***jīva.*** **Pṛthu, fully capable, the shelter of** ***prakṛti,*** **then gave up** ***prakṛti*** **situated beside him, by the power of** ***jñāna*** **and** ***vairāgya*****, though he was already situated in his spiritual body.**

**Комментарий:**

By bhakti-yoga which gives power (*vīrya)* to jñāna and vairāgyam, he gave up prakṛti. He was situated in his form as an associate (*svarūpa-sthaḥ*), being most capable (*prabhuḥ*). Some versions have *avyadhāt* (destroyed) instead of *jahāt.* Śrīdhara Svāmī has that version.

### Text 20

*atīva bhartur vrata-dharma-niṣṭhayā*  
*śuśrūṣayā cārṣa-deha-yātrayā*  
*nāvindatārtiṁ parikarśitāpi sā*  
*preyaskara-sparśana-māna-nirvṛtiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Although she became thin, she did not experience pain by living like a sage, serving with determination the vows of her intent husband, taking pleasure in his respect and touch of his hand.**

**Комментарий:**

She felt pleasure in his worship and the touch of his hand when doing service. Citsukha has *nirvṛtāḥ.*

### Text 21

*dehaṁ vipannākhila-cetanādikaṁ*  
*patyuḥ pṛthivyā dayitasya cātmanaḥ*  
*ālakṣya kiñcic ca vilapya sā satī*  
*citām athāropayad adri-sānuni*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing that the body of her merciful husband, master of the earth, was without life symptoms, after lamenting a little, she placed it on a pyre on a summit of a hill.**

**Комментарий:**

She lamented out of affection but gave that up on considering with intelligence.

### Text 26

*saiṣā nūnaṁ vrajaty ūrdhvam*  
*anu vainyaṁ patiṁ satī*  
*paśyatāsmān atītyārcir*  
*durvibhāvyena karmaṇā*

**Перевод:**

**That must be her traveling in the sky. See her following her husband Pṛthu. Arci has surpassed us in inconceivable actions.**

**Комментарий:**

The wives of the devatās point her out in the sky. She is going to Vaikuṇṭha (*ūrdhvam*), following after her husband.

### Text 27

*teṣāṁ durāpaṁ kiṁ tv anyan*  
*martyānāṁ bhagavat-padam*  
*bhuvi lolāyuṣo ye vai*  
*naiṣkarmyaṁ sādhayanty uta*

**Перевод:**

**What else is difficult to obtain for human beings of short lifespan on earth who attain the abode of the Lord, which is free of material action and reaction?**

**Комментарий:**

In his commentary Śrīdhara Svāmī mentions *jñānam* for attaining the Lord. This actually means jñāna which is an aṅga of bhakti. *Naiṣkarmyam* can also mean they attain Vaikuṇṭha, which cannot be attained by karma. Citsukha has *vidyullolāyuṣa* (as fickle as lightning).

### Text 28

*sa vañcito batātma-dhruk*  
*kṛcchreṇa mahatā bhuvi*  
*labdhvāpavargyaṁ mānuṣyaṁ*  
*viṣayeṣu viṣajjate*

**Перевод:**

**Having with great difficulty attained a human form of life, suitable for liberation, a person who becomes attached to sense objects is certainly cheated and a killer of the self.**

**Комментарий:**

*Apavargyam mānuṣyam* means human life which gives siddhis like sālokya or bhakti-yoga.

### Text 29

*maitreya uvāca*  
*stuvatīṣv amara-strīṣu*  
*pati-lokaṁ gatā vadhūḥ*  
*yaṁ vā ātma-vidāṁ dhuryo*  
*vainyaḥ prāpācyutāśrayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: While the women were glorifying her, Arci reached the planet where her husband had gone. There, Pṛthu, the best of knowers of the Lord, attained the shelter of the Lord**

**Комментарий:**

She went to the planet that her husband had attained (*pati-lokam*). It was not material. The best knower of the Lord, who had taken shelter of Acyuta attained that planet. Because he preached knowledge of the Lord, he naturally took shelter of the Lord, who shelters such persons.

### Text 39

*anudinam idam ādareṇa śṛṇvan*  
*pṛthu-caritaṁ prathayan vimukta-saṅgaḥ*  
*bhagavati bhava-sindhu-pota-pāde*  
*sa ca nipuṇāṁ labhate ratiṁ manuṣyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever hears or recites this story of Pṛthu daily with respect, avoiding material association, will attain** ***rati*** **to the Lord whose feet are a boat for crossing the ocean of material existence.**

**Комментарий:**

Anyone who is a human being (*manuṣyaḥ*), who hears or recites the story of Pṛthu, will attain rati.

## Chapter Twenty-four

### Text 6

*rājñāṁ vṛttiṁ karādāna-*  
*daṇḍa-śulkādi-dāruṇām*  
*manyamāno dīrgha-sattra-*  
*vyājena visasarja ha*

**Перевод:**

**Antardhāna gave up the duties of the King such as receiving taxing, punishment and fines, since he thought they were giving suffering to the people, on the plea of performing a long sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

If he directly gave up the duties, he would be breaking the conduct of a king.

### Text 11

*sāmudrīṁ devadevoktām*  
*upayeme śatadrutim*  
*yāṁ vīkṣya cāru-sarvāṅgīṁ*  
*kiśorīṁ suṣṭhv-alaṅkṛtām*  
*parikramantīm udvāhe*  
*cakame 'gniḥ śukīm iva*

**Перевод:**

**He married the daughter of the ocean named Śatadruti under the instruction of Brahmā. Agni, seeing her with beautiful limbs and youth, and well decorated, as she went around the fire at the marriage ceremony, became attracted to her as he had become attracted to Śukī.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse has six lines.

### Text 13

*prācīnabarhiṣaḥ putrāḥ*  
*śatadrutyāṁ daśābhavan*  
*tulya-nāma-vratāḥ sarve*  
*dharma-snātāḥ pracetasaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Prācīnabarhi begot ten children in the womb of Śatadruti called the Pracetās, who were all equal to their names in vows and were religious.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *dhanuḥ-snātā*. This means they were dedicated to following Dhanurveda.

### Text 18

*ātmārāmo 'pi yas tv asya*  
*loka-kalpasya rādhase*  
*śaktyā yukto vicarati*  
*ghorayā bhagavān bhavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Though he appears to the sages by their meditation, he also appears in the world to bestow boons to those desiring material benefits.**

**Комментарий:**

He moves about to bestow boons to the people by his energy, which is terrifying for the wicked (*ghorayā*).

### Text 20

*samudram upa vistīrṇam*  
*apaśyan sumahat saraḥ*  
*mahan-mana iva svacchaṁ*  
*prasanna-salilāśayam*

**Перевод:**

**They saw a huge lake, wide like the small ocean, clear like the mind of a saint, which was filled with pleasing water.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe the lake. It was near the ocean (*upa*). The place was clean (*svaccham*) and the water was pleasing.

*matta-bhramara-sausvarya-*  
*hṛṣṭa-roma-latāṅghripam*  
*padma-kośa-rajo dikṣu*  
*vikṣipat-pavanotsavam*

**It was surrounded with trees and creepers, whose shoots stood up like hairs standing on end because of the humming of intoxicated bees humming. The wind threw lotus pollen everywhere in a great festival. The wind threw plenty of lotus pollen in all directions. (**[**4.24.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/24.22)**)**

### Text 23

*tatra gāndharvam ākarṇya*  
*divya-mārga-manoharam*  
*visismyū rāja-putrās te*  
*mṛdaṅga-paṇavādy anu*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the sound of drums followed by attractive, celestial songs in classical style, the sons of King Prācīnabarhiṣat became astonished.**

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *paṇavādyavat.*

### Text 24-25

*tarhy eva sarasas tasmān*  
*niṣkrāmantaṁ sahānugam*  
*upagīyamānam amara-*  
*pravaraṁ vibudhānugaiḥ*  
*tapta-hema-nikāyābhaṁ*  
*śiti-kaṇṭhaṁ tri-locanam*  
*prasāda-sumukhaṁ vīkṣya*  
*praṇemur jāta-kautukāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**At that moment, they saw emerging from the lake the chief of the** ***devatās,*** **beautiful Śiva, effulgent like a pile of melted golden, with blue throat, three eyes, and brilliant face, accompanied by his followers, while being glorified by the followers of the** ***devatās.*** **With curiosity aroused, they offered respects.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *hema-nikāyārṇam*: his complexion was like a pile of gold.

### Text 26

*sa tān prapannārti-haro*  
*bhagavān dharma-vatsalaḥ*  
*dharma-jñān śīla-sampannān*  
*prītaḥ prītān uvāca ha*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva, who takes away the distress of the surrendered and who has affect for** ***dharma*****, pleased with the Pracetās who knew** ***dharma*** **and had good conduct, spoke to them, to their delight.**

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *prapannān giriśaḥ* instead of *prpannārti-haraḥ.*

### Text 28

*yaḥ paraṁ rahasaḥsākṣāt*  
*tri-guṇāj jīva-saṁjñitāt*  
*bhagavantaṁ vāsudevaṁ*  
*prapannaḥ sa priyo hi me*

**Перевод:**

**He who has completely surrendered to Vāsudeva, who is superior to** ***māyā,*** **to the** ***jīva-śakti*** **and Brahman*****,*** **is dear to me.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is superior to Brahman (*rahasaḥ*) as in *brahmaṇo hi pratiṣṭhāham*: I am the basis of Brahman. Or the Lord is the supreme shelter (*param*) of prema (*rahasaḥ*) as in the catuḥ-ślokī. Some versions have the mistake *raṁhasa.* Śrīdhara Svāmī in his commentary accepts *raṁhasa* with the meaning “subtle.” But this is not natural. Citsukha has *tamasa* instead.

### Text 29

*sva-dharma-niṣṭhaḥ śata-janmabhiḥ pumān*  
*viriñcatām eti tataḥ paraṁ hi mām*  
*avyākṛtaṁ bhāgavato 'tha vaiṣṇavaṁ*  
*padaṁ yathāhaṁ vibudhāḥ kalātyaye*

**Перевод:**

**A person fixed in** ***dharma*** **attains the post of Brahmā after a hundred births, and by more pious acts than that a person attains me, Śiva. But the devotee attains the abode of Vaikuṇṭha beyond the material world after leaving the body. Similarly I in another form reside there, and the** ***devatās*** **who are qualified go there after destroying their subtle bodies.**

**Комментарий:**

What is the cause of the devotee’s greatness? That is explained. Citsukha has *vibudhaiḥ* (with the devatās) instead of *vibudhā.*

### Text 33

*śrī-rudra uvāca*  
*jitaṁ ta ātma-vid-varya-*  
*svastaye svastir astu me*  
*bhavatārādhasā rāddhaṁ*  
*sarvasmā ātmane namaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva said: You, the Lord, have revealed yourself as supreme for producing the most auspicious condition for the best of** ***ātmārāmas.*** **Let me also have that auspicious condition! Let me have perfection by worshipping you. I offer respects to you, who are everyone, including** ***guru.***

**Комментарий:**

The root of the verb *as* (to be) can be expresses as *asti* according to the rule *ik śatipau* *dhātu-nirdeśaḥ.* (Pāṇini 3.3.108, *Vārtikā*) Your manifestation (*jitam*) of excellent qualities is for attainment of qualities of the pure devotee (*svastaye*) by most excellent of knowers of ātmā (*ātmavit-vārya*).

*muktānām api siddhānāṁ nārāyaṇa-parāyaṇaḥ*  
*su-durlabhaḥ praśāntātmā koṭiṣv api mahā-mune*

**O great sage! Greater than those who are** ***jīvanmukta*** **and greater than those who attain liberation is the devotee of Lord Nārāyaṇa. Such a devotee, who is in** ***śānta*** **or other** ***rasas,*** **is very rare, even among ten million people. (SB** [**6.1.45**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/1.45)**)**

That is the puruṣārtha.

I pray for those good qualities (*svastir astu me*). “What is your sādhana?” I offer respects to you (*namas te*): I am not independent. I depend on you. Offering respects is described. The perfection (*rāddham*) attained by sādhanas of all worshippers (*rādhasā*) is the antaryāmī (*ātmane*), the form of everything (*sarvasmai*). Or the Lord is *svasti*, a form suitable for low minds. Thus I offer respects to you.

### Text 34

*namaḥ paṅkaja-nābhāya*  
*bhūta-sūkṣmendriyātmane*  
*vāsudevāya śāntāya*  
*kūṭa-sthāya sva-rociṣe*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to the controller of** ***tan-mātras*** **and senses, to the Lord with a lotus sprouting from his navel, to Vāsudeva, the deity of** ***citta,*** **to the unchangeable form of beauty, to the Lord who is capable of making my consciousness steady.**

**Комментарий:**

Vāsudeva and others are expansions of Padmanābha. These are not the original forms such as Mahā-vāsudeva. The form is without change (*kuṭasthāya*). The other forms like Saṅkarṣaṇa are also expansions.

### Text 40

*artha-liṅgāya nabhase*  
*namo 'ntar-bahir-ātmane*  
*namaḥ puṇyāya lokāya*  
*amuṣmai bhūri-varcase*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to ether, which reveals meaning through sound, which supports everything internally and externally. I offer respects to the highest planet, Vaikuṇṭha, with the greatest effulgence.**

**Комментарий:**

Two statements are made using *namaḥ.*

### Text 41

*pravṛttāya nivṛttāya*  
*pitṛ-devāya karmaṇe*  
*namo 'dharma-vipākāya*  
*mṛtyave duḥkha-dāya ca*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to you who inspire people to enjoy and to renounce through** ***karmas*****, and who give the results of worshipping Pitṛs. I offer respects to you who give results to those committing sinful acts, who award death and sorrow.**

**Комментарий:**

This is one statement.

### Text 42

*namas ta āśiṣām īśa*  
*manave kāraṇātmane*  
*namo dharmāya bṛhate*  
*kṛṣṇāyākuṇṭha-medhase*  
*puruṣāya purāṇāya*  
*sāṅkhya-yogeśvarāya ca*

**Перевод:**

**O bestower of Svarga! I offer respects to you. I offer respects to the form of all** ***mantras*****, the cause of all actions. I offer respects the Lord who is** ***dharma*** **personified, to Kṛṣṇa, the omniscient, perfect Lord, who preaches Sāṅkhya and** ***yoga*** **in various** ***avatāras.***

**Комментарий:**

He praises the Lord with prayers to the three deities, thinking of them as non-different from Padmanābha, with the form of Viṣṇu. I offer respects the great puruṣa Padmanābha (*puruṣāya purāṇāya*), who breathed out the Vedas. I offer respects to the personification of great dharma (*dharmāya bṛhate*), who is bhagavad-dharma, the source of all other dharmas. I offer respects to Kapila and Dattātreya who inaugurated sāṅkhya and yoga.

I offer respects to Kṛṣṇa, with unlimited knowledge, parabrahman who protects the universe by spreading knowledge. The wrong order of the descriptions in the text is not improper, since it reflects the will of the speaker.

### Text 43

*śakti-traya-sametāya*  
*mīḍhuṣe 'haṅkṛtātmane*  
*ceta-ākūti-rūpāya*  
*namo vāco vibhūtaye*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Śiva, endowed with the three energies (*****adhidaiva, adhyātma*** **and** ***adhibhauta*****), the lord of** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **the form of knowledge and action. I offer respects to the revealer of the Vedas (Brahmā).**

**Комментарий:**

Śiva and Brahmā are praised for their limited powers.

### Text 44

*darśanaṁ no didṛkṣūṇāṁ*  
*dehi bhāgavatārcitam*  
*rūpaṁ priyatamaṁ svānāṁ*  
*sarvendriya-guṇāñjanam*

**Перевод:**

**Show us the form worshipped by the devotees, the beautiful form most treasured by your devotees, endowed with all qualities and senses, for we desire to see it.**

**Комментарий:**

Having described the Lord as all forms, by such recitation, he develops special bhakti. He describes his desired form and develops special desires. Show me the form worshipped by the devotees as the highest form, that form which is dear to your associates, and which is endowed with all qualities and senses. “Since I am all forms, you can see me at all times.” Eight and half verses describe that form.

### Text 45-46

*snigdha-prāvṛḍ-ghana-śyāmaṁ*  
*sarva-saundarya-saṅgraham*  
*cārv-āyata-catur-bāhu*  
*sujāta-rucirānanam*  
*padma-kośa-palāśākṣaṁ*  
*sundara-bhru sunāsikam*  
*sudvijaṁ sukapolāsyaṁ*  
*sama-karṇa-vibhūṣaṇam*

**Перевод:**

**He shines, dark like a rain cloud, the sum of all beauty, with four long arms and noble, attractive face. He has lotus eyes, graceful brows and nose, fine teeth and forehead, and well-proportioned ears.**

**Комментарий:**

*Samakarṇa* means having proper size which is beautiful.

### Text 49

*siṁha-skandha-tviṣo bibhrat*  
*saubhaga-grīva-kaustubham*  
*śriyānapāyinyā kṣipta-*  
*nikaṣāśmorasollasat*

**Перевод:**

**His lion-like shoulders glitter with reflections from his jewels. The Kaustubha jewel enhances his neck with additional beauty. His chest glows with the mark of imperishable Lakṣmī, deriding the line on a gold-testing stone.**

**Комментарий:**

Sambandhokti has *tulām* instead of *tviṣa.*

### Text 50

*pūra-recaka-saṁvigna-*  
*vali-valgu-dalodaram*  
*pratisaṅkrāmayad viśvaṁ*  
*nābhyāvarta-gabhīrayā*

**Перевод:**

**His abdomen is shaped like a banyan leaf, whose lines move with his inhaling and exhaling. The universe seems to enter his body through his deep, twirled navel.**

**Комментарий:**

The universe seems to enter his navel, attracted by its beauty. Or the universe comes out of his navel at creation and enters it at destruction. This shows its power and depth. Citsukha has *pradarśayad* instead of *pratisaṅkrāmayat.*

### Text 53

*etad rūpam anudhyeyam*  
*ātma-śuddhim abhīpsatām*  
*yad-bhakti-yogo 'bhayadaḥ*  
*sva-dharmam anutiṣṭhatām*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring purification of the** ***jīva,*** **one should repeatedly meditate on this form. Devotion to this form gives freedom from fear of death, even to those practicing** ***varṇāśrama*** **duties.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of meditation on this form is described. From this meditation (*yat*) prema (*bhakti-yogaḥ*) manifests. This meditations arises from deep faith. Without that faith, a semblance of bhakti arises, for those depending on sva-dharma. That just gives freedom from fear of saṁsāra.

### Text 54

*bhavān bhaktimatā labhyo*  
*durlabhaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*svārājyasyāpy abhimata*  
*ekāntenātma-vid-gatiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Desired by Brahmā, sought by the Kumāras and other knowers of** ***ātmā,*** **who have given up material aspirations, you who are rarely attained by any living being are attained by your devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

You also are attained by intense meditation of kaivalya endowed with excellent bhakti. You are hard to attain by ordinary beings and even by jīvanmuktas. You are desired even by persons who have attained sālokya. You are the goal of person who know only you, (*ātmavidām*), by bhakti-yoga endowed with disregard for sālokya etc. (*ekāntena*). You are the highest goal.

### Text 55

*taṁ durārādhyam ārādhya*  
*satām api durāpayā*  
*ekānta-bhaktyā ko vāñchet*  
*pāda-mūlaṁ vinā bahiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Who would desire the happiness of Svarga, devoid of your lotus feet which are difficult to attain, but attained by worshipping you by** ***pure bhakti,*** **which also difficult to attain?**

**Комментарий:**

Who would desire the happiness of Svarga, without your lotus feet, worshipped by pure bhakti, difficult to attain even by jñānīs (*satām*)?

### Text 56

*yatra nirviṣṭam araṇaṁ*  
*kṛtānto nābhimanyate*  
*viśvaṁ vidhvaṁsayan vīrya-*  
*śaurya-visphūrjita-bhruvā*

**Перевод:**

**Death, who destroys the universe by his brow which quivers with power and determination, cannot consider as his possession the person who has surrendered to those feet.**

**Комментарий:**

There is no fear as in Svarga.

*martyo mṛtyu-vyāla-bhītaḥ palāyan*  
*lokān sarvān nirbhayaṁ nādhyagacchat*  
*tvat pādābjaṁ prāpya yadṛcchayādya*  
*susthaḥ śete mṛtyur asmād apaiti*

**No one in this material world, fearful of the snake of time, has become free from fear, even by fleeing to various planets. But now that you have appeared, death is fleeing in fear of you, and the living entities, having obtained shelter at your lotus feet by your mercy, are fearless and tranquil. (SB** [**10.3.27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/3.27)**)**

### Text 57

*kṣaṇārdhenāpi tulaye*  
*na svargaṁ nāpunar-bhavam*  
*bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasya*  
*martyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**What to speak of any blessings on this earth, or on Svarga, even the blessing of liberation cannot compare with even half a moment’s association with the devotee of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

I cannot compare anything to devotee association since it is distinct from trivial or profound blessings.

### Text 58

*athānaghāṅghres tava kīrti-tīrthayor*  
*antar-bahiḥ-snāna-vidhūta-pāpmanām*  
*bhūteṣv anukrośa-susattva-śīlināṁ*  
*syāt saṅgamo 'nugraha eṣa nas tava*

**Перевод:**

**From this, we will attain association with persons who have been purified externally and internally by the famous Gaṅgā arising from your lotus foot which destroys sin, and with person who have proper conduct, pure hearts and show mercy to all beings. This is your mercy to us.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotees also purify the universe of all the sins (*vidhūta-pāpmanām*).

*vāg gadgadā dravate yasya cittaṁ*  
*rudaty abhīkṣṇaṁ hasati kvacic ca*  
*vilajja udgāyati nṛtyate ca*  
*mad-bhakti-yukto bhuvanaṁ punāti*

**A devotee whose speech is choked up, whose heart is melted, who cries continually and sometimes laughs, sometimes feels ashamed, sometimes sings loudly and sometimes dances—a devotee thus fixed in loving service to me purifies the entire universe. (SB** [**11.1.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/1.24)**)**

### Text 59

*na yasya cittaṁ bahir-artha-vibhramaṁ*  
*tamo-guhāyāṁ ca viśuddham āviśat*  
*yad-bhakti-yogānugṛhītam añjasā*  
*munir vicaṣṭe nanu tatra te gatim*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee whose heart is pure, not agitated by external objects, and not subject to falling asleep during worship, having been blessed by Bhakti-devī herself, certainly sees the Lord’s pastimes and beauty with ease.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotee easily sees your svarūpa (*gatim*) filled with spiritual qualities and pastimes.

### Text 60

*yatredaṁ vyajyate viśvaṁ*  
*viśvasminn avabhāti yat*  
*tat tvaṁ brahma paraṁ jyotir*  
*ākāśam iva vistṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**You are the Brahman, the supreme light, spread everywhere like ether within which the universe is manifested. You are also manifest within the universe.**

**Комментарий:**

Without the mercy of bhakti, you are hard to understand, hard to worship, hard to bring under control and hard to show capabilities, in manifesting your internal and external energies, with different forms of Brahman, Bhagavān and Paramātmā. This is expressed in seven verses. First Brahman is described. Without bhakti-yoga this is difficult to understand.

### Text 61

*yo māyayedaṁ puru-rūpayāsṛjad*  
*bibharti bhūyaḥ kṣapayaty avikriyaḥ*  
*yad-bheda-buddhiḥ sad ivātma-duḥsthayā*  
*tam ātma-tantraṁ bhagavan pratīmahi*

**Перевод:**

**You, unchanging, through many forms, have created this universe, maintain it and destroy it repeatedly, using** ***māyā,*** **which cannot bewilder you, and by which knowledge of variety, which appears so attractive, arises in this universe. O Bhagavān! We pray to realize you, who are independent.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhagavān is described. We pray to realize you by whom (*yat)* arises the thought that jīvas are independent of you (*bheda-buddhiḥ*). You create the universe by māyā, which shows itself inferior to you, Bhagavān (*ātma-duḥsthayā*).

### Text 62

*kriyā-kalāpair idam eva yoginaḥ*  
*śraddhānvitāḥ sādhu yajanti siddhaye*  
*bhūtendriyāntaḥ-karaṇopalakṣitaṁ*  
*vede ca tantre ca ta eva kovidāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**By various actions,** ***yogīs*** **expert in the Vedas and Pañcarātra, filled with faith and aiming for perfection, worship the Lord who has a form with his body, senses and** ***antaḥkaraṇa*** **under his control.**

**Комментарий:**

Paramātmā is described. The yogīs worship this, which has three forms (*idam eva*). Those who are expert at seeing Paramātmā on the path of karma, are also expert at seeing Bhagavān. What to say then of worshippers following the path of bhakti?

### Text 63

*tvam eka ādyaḥ puruṣaḥ supta-śaktis*  
*tayā rajaḥ-sattva-tamo vibhidyate*  
*mahān ahaṁ khaṁ marud agni-vār-dharāḥ*  
*surarṣayo bhūta-gaṇā idaṁ yataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**You are the one original person, endowed with a sleeping energy** ***māyā.*** **By this energy,** ***rajas, sattva*** **and** ***tamas*** **are differentiated, and from the** ***guṇas*** **arise the universe,** ***mahat-tattva, ahaṅkāra*****, ether, air, fire, water, earth, the** ***devatās*****, sages, and all living beings.**

**Комментарий:**

Paramātmā or antaryāmī is established in two verses. The first verse describes the aṁśī (Mahā-viṣṇu) and the second verse describes the aṁśa (the jīvas). Śrīdhara Svāmī explains this in his commentary.

### Text 65

*sa eṣa lokān aticaṇḍa-vego*  
*vikarṣasi tvaṁ khalu kālayānaḥ*  
*bhūtāni bhūtair anumeya-tattvo*  
*ghanāvalīr vāyur ivāviṣahyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, possessing great force, disturbs the places of enjoyment and destroys living beings by other living beings using the invisible form of time, just an unbearable wind destroys the clouds.**

**Комментарий:**

Time is described in two verses. The Lord, through time, instills fear, by his mercy, to make the jīvas favorable to him, since he is the destroyer of all suffering and giver of all happiness.

### Text 67

*kas tvat-padābjaṁ vijahāti paṇḍito*  
*yas te 'vamāna-vyayamāna-ketanaḥ*  
*viśaṅkayāsmad-gurur arcati sma yad*  
*vinopapattiṁ manavaś caturdaśa*

**Перевод:**

**What intelligent person, whose body will be destroyed by disrespecting you, would reject your lotus feet, which Brahmā worshipped out of fear of rebirth and the fourteen Manus worship with strong faith?**

**Комментарий:**

Thus bhakti is the puruṣārtha. That is summarized in two verses. Who would give up your lotus feet out of fear (*viśaṅkhayā*)? Brahma and the fourteen Manus, and devotees as well, worship those feet without argument (*vinā upapattim*), naturally. Citsukha and Sambandhokti have *cetanaḥ* instead of *ketanaḥ.*

### Text 68

*atha tvam asi no brahman*  
*paramātman vipaścitām*  
*viśvaṁ rudra-bhaya-dhvastam*  
*akutaścid-bhayā gatiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Brahman! Paramātmā of the wise! You are our goal in which there is no fear. The whole universe of ignorant** ***jīvas*** **is destroyed by fear of time.**

**Комментарий:**

O Paramātmā of the wise! You are our goal, in which there is no fear. You are dear to us by nature. Without you, the world is destroyed by fear.

### Text 69

*idaṁ japata bhadraṁ vo*  
*viśuddhā nṛpa-nandanāḥ*  
*sva-dharmam anutiṣṭhanto*  
*bhagavaty arpitāśayāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O princes! Being pure, offering your minds to the Lord, perform your prescribed duties and chant this prayer. It is auspicious for you.**

**Комментарий:**

First karma-miśra-bhakti is presented. Later pure bhakti is presented.

### Text 73

*te vayaṁ noditāḥ sarve*  
*prajā-sarge prajeśvarāḥ*  
*anena dhvasta-tamasaḥ*  
*sisṛkṣmo vividhāḥ prajāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Ordered by Brahmā, as controllers of the progeny, we all destroyed ignorance by this prayer and created a variety of progeny at the time of secondary creation.**

**Комментарий:**

The version with *sisṛkṣmaḥ* is also seen.

### Text 75

*śreyasām iha sarveṣāṁ*  
*jñānaṁ niḥśreyasaṁ param*  
*sukhaṁ tarati duṣpāraṁ*  
*jñāna-naur vyasanārṇavam*

**Перевод:**

**Of all auspicious items in this world, knowledge is supremely auspicious. It is the supreme bliss. The boat of knowledge crosses the ocean of** ***saṁsāra*** **which is hard to cross.**

**Комментарий:**

Jñāna is praised as the best. But the jñāna is knowledge of Bhagavān.

### Text 77

*vindate puruṣo 'muṣmād*  
*yad yad icchaty asatvaram*  
*mad-gīta-gītāt suprītāc*  
*chreyasām eka-vallabhāt*

**Перевод:**

**From the Lord, the sole object of affection, the steady person attains the best of whatever he desires from singing my song, which pleases the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

*Asatvaram* means steadily.

### Text 79

*gītaṁ mayedaṁ naradeva-nandanāḥ*  
*parasya puṁsaḥ paramātmanaḥ stavam*  
*japanta ekāgra-dhiyas tapo mahat*  
*caradhvam ante tata āpsyathepsitam*

**Перевод:**

**O princes! With concentration, while chanting these prayers concerning the Supreme Lord, Paramātmā, sung by me, undertake great austerities, and in the end you will achieve the desired result.**

**Комментарий:**

The austerity mentioned here is worship of the Lord only.

## Chapter Twenty-five

### Text 2

*rudra-gītaṁ bhagavataḥ*  
*stotraṁ sarve pracetasaḥ*  
*japantas te tapas tepur*  
*varṣāṇām ayutaṁ jale*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting Śiva’s song concerning the Lord, the Pracetās performed austerities for ten thousand years in the water.**

**Комментарий:**

The austerity was chanting Śiva’s song.

### Text 4

*śreyas tvaṁ katamad rājan*  
*karmaṇātmana īhase*  
*duḥkha-hāniḥ sukhāvāptiḥ*  
*śreyas tan neha ceṣyate*

**Перевод:**

**O King! What benefit do you want for yourself by doing these** ***karmas*****? It is not possible to destroy suffering and attain happiness by performance of** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

Destruction of suffering and attainment of happiness is the goal of life, but cannot be attained by karmas. The happiness produced by karmas is mixed with suffering and ends with suffering. Citsukha has *neha yeṣyāte.*

### Text 5

*rājovāca*  
*na jānāmi mahā-bhāga*  
*paraṁ karmāpaviddha-dhīḥ*  
*brūhi me vimalaṁ jñānaṁ*  
*yena mucyeya karmabhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The King said: O great fortunate saint! Since my intelligence is bewildered by** ***karma*** **I do not know what is beneficial. Tell me the pure knowledge by which I can become free from these duties.**

**Комментарий:**

My mind being bewildered by karma, I do not know what is the sādhana and the best goal. Please tell me about pure knowledge which includes the sādhana and the goal.

### Text 9

*atra te kathayiṣye 'mum*  
*itihāsaṁ purātanam*  
*purañjanasya caritaṁ*  
*nibodha gadato mama*

**Перевод:**

**I will relate to you this ancient, historical story of Purañjana. Please understand it as I speak.**

**Комментарий:**

He narrates this story to teach the bhakti mixed with jñāna (which produces liberation), considering that the king was not qualified for pure bhakti. The story was ancient, and not false teachings invented by Nārada. The descriptions generally conforms to adhyātma-vāda (distinguishing soul from body) and are thus consistent with the talks.

### Text 10

*āsīt purañjano nāma*  
*rājā rājan bṛhac-chravāḥ*  
*tasyāvijñāta-nāmāsīt*  
*sakhāvijñāta-ceṣṭitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! There was a very famous king named Purañjana. He had an unknown friend, whose name and activities were unknown.**

**Комментарий:**

The friend was called *avijñāta* because he could not be known.

### Text 13

*sa ekadā himavato*  
*dakṣiṇeṣv atha sānuṣu*  
*dadarśa navabhir dvārbhiḥ*  
*puraṁ lakṣita-lakṣaṇām*

**Перевод:**

**Once, the King saw on the southern ridge of the Himalayas a city with nine gates, which he understood had good qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has this version, with *sa ekadā.*

### Text 17-19

*puryās tu bāhyopavane*  
*divya-druma-latākule*  
*nadad-vihaṅgāli-kula-*  
*kolāhala-jalāśaye*  
*hima-nirjhara-vipruṣmat-*  
*kusumākara-vāyunā*  
*calat-pravāla-viṭapa-*  
*nalinī-taṭa-sampadi*  
*nānāraṇya-mṛga-vrātair*  
*anābādhe muni-vrataiḥ*  
*āhūtaṁ manyate pāntho*  
*yatra kokila-kūjitaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Outside the city in groves of beautiful trees and creepers, with ponds resounding with the cries of flocks of birds and humming of bees, with lakes having banks abundant with lotuses and trees, whose branches moved in the spring wind laden with water drops from cool waterfalls, where cuckoos warbled as if calling out to travelers, there were various non-violent forest animals free of troubles, and no violent animals.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together. Though intelligence and other elements of the subtle body accompany the jīva at all times, when one attains a human body, intelligence etc. become exceptional. Attaining this intelligence is astonishing.

### Text 20

*yadṛcchayāgatāṁ tatra*  
*dadarśa pramadottamām*  
*bhṛtyair daśabhir āyāntīm*  
*ekaika-śata-nāyakaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In that place, he saw a very beautiful woman who had arrived on her own with ten servants, each with unlimited courtesans.**

**Комментарий:**

By arrangement of the Lord (*yadṛcchayā*) a beautiful woman arrived. She is described in six and a half verses.

### Text 25

*tām āha lalitaṁ vīraḥ*  
*savrīḍa-smita-śobhanām*  
*snigdhenāpāṅga-puṅkhena*  
*spṛṣṭaḥ premodbhramad-bhruvā*

**Перевод:**

**Pierced by the arrow shaft of her affectionate glance and the lusty movement of her brow, the brave King spoke to that beautiful woman with a shy smile.**

**Комментарий:**

He was pierced by the tip of the arrow, her affectionate glance.

### Text 30

*yad eṣa māpāṅga-vikhaṇḍitendriyaṁ*  
*savrīḍa-bhāva-smita-vibhramad-bhruvā*  
*tvayopasṛṣṭo bhagavān mano-bhavaḥ*  
*prabādhate 'thānugṛhāṇa śobhane*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful woman! Since powerful Cupid, inspired by your bewildering brow and your shy, affectionate smile, is harassing me, whose senses are smashed by your glance, please be merciful to me!**

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *yad eṣa te ‘pāṅga, athopasṛṣtaḥ,* and *prabādhate mānugṛhāna*. Śrīdhara Svāmī accepts this version. Citsukha approves of *tvayopasṛṣṭaḥ.*

### Text 33

*na vidāma vayaṁ samyak*  
*kartāraṁ puruṣarṣabha*  
*ātmanaś ca parasyāpi*  
*gotraṁ nāma ca yat-kṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**O best of men! I do not know who produced me, and I do not know my name or family, nor the names and family of others accompanying me.**

**Комментарий:**

I do not know who produced me for Purañjana’s enjoyment.

### Text 36

*diṣṭyāgato 'si bhadraṁ te*  
*grāmyān kāmān abhīpsase*  
*udvahiṣyāmi tāṁs te 'haṁ*  
*sva-bandhubhir arindama*

**Перевод:**

**O killer of the enemy! By fortune, you have come here. All fortune to you! You desire material enjoyment. With my friends, I will supply that to you.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *tāṁs te ‘ham* sometimes *tān snehān* is seen.

### Text 54

*andhāv amīṣāṁ paurāṇāṁ*  
*nirvāk-peśaskṛtāv ubhau*  
*akṣaṇvatām adhipatis*  
*tābhyāṁ yāti karoti ca*

**Перевод:**

**Among the inhabitants of this city, there were two blind men named Nirvāk and Peśaskṛt. Although King Purañjana was the ruler of the gates, he used to perform actions and go places with these two blind men.**

**Комментарий:**

*Andhau* means they were devoid of knowledge.

### Text 62

*vipralabdho mahiṣyaivaṁ*  
*sarva-prakṛti-vañcitaḥ*  
*necchann anukaroty ajñaḥ*  
*klaibyāt krīḍā-mṛgo yathā*

**Перевод:**

**Specifically conditioned by the Queen, cheated of his own nature, the foolish King, though he did not want to, followed her like a pet animal, because of falling under another’s control.**

**Комментарий:**

He became cheated. Though the jīva has power, it is minimized:

*parābhidhyānāt tu tirohitaṁ tato hy asya bandha viparyayau*

**Dreams disappear by the will of the Lord. This is not surprising since he is also the cause of bondage and liberation. (Brahma-sūtra** [**3.2.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/3.2.5)**)**

## Chapter Twenty-six

### Text 1-3

*nārada uvāca*  
*sa ekadā maheṣvāso*  
*rathaṁ pañcāśvam āśu-gam*  
*dvīṣaṁ dvi-cakram ekākṣaṁ*  
*tri-veṇuṁ pañca-bandhuram*  
*eka-raśmy eka-damanam*  
*eka-nīḍaṁ dvi-kūbaram*  
*pañca-praharaṇaṁ sapta-*  
*varūthaṁ pañca-vikramam*  
*haimopaskaram āruhya*  
*svarṇa-varmākṣayeṣudhiḥ*  
*ekādaśa-camū-nāthaḥ*  
*pañca-prastham agād vanam*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: Once upon a time, the King took up his great bow, and equipped with golden armor and an inexhaustible quiver, accompanied by a commander among eleven, he sat on his chariot driven by five swift horses and went to the forest. The gold chariot had two shafts, two wheels, one axle, three flags, five supports, one rein, one chariot driver, one sitting place, two poles to which the yoke was fixed, five weapons and seven protective coverings. The chariot of widespread exploits moved to five destinations.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together. The driver (*damana*) is the intelligence, but without power of discrimination. The five destinations are the five objects of the senses such as sound, represented by dead animals, and also the desires for the enjoyment.

### Text 14

*antaḥpura-striyo 'pṛcchad*  
*vimanā iva vediṣat*  
*api vaḥ kuśalaṁ rāmāḥ*  
*seśvarīṇāṁ yathā purā*

**Перевод:**

**O King Prācīnabarhi! Somewhat dejected, he asked the women of the household, “O women! Are you who attend my Queen in good health as previously?”**

**Комментарий:**

This is connected with the first part of the next verse.

### Text 19

*sāntvayan ślakṣṇayā vācā*  
*hṛdayena vidūyatā*  
*preyasyāḥ sneha-saṁrambha-*  
*liṅgam ātmani nābhyagāt*

**Перевод:**

**Pacifying her with sweet words filled with regret, he did not find in her any symptoms of anger caused by her affection.**

**Комментарий:**

He did not see her anger because it was very deep.

### Text 24

*tasmin dadhe damam ahaṁ tava vīra-patni*  
*yo 'nyatra bhūsura-kulāt kṛta-kilbiṣas tam*  
*paśye na vīta-bhayam unmuditaṁ tri-lokyām*  
*anyatra vai mura-ripor itaratra dāsāt*

**Перевод:**

**O wife of a hero! Except for the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **and the Vaiṣṇava, I will punish anyone who has offended you. I do not see anyone in the three worlds who can remain fearless and joyful in my presence other than these two.**

**Комментарий:**

He states that he will punish all except those fixed in dharma (*bhūsura-kulāt*).

## Chapter Twenty-seven

### Text 4

*śayāna unnaddha-mado mahā-manā*  
*mahārha-talpe mahiṣī-bhujopadhiḥ*  
*tām eva vīro manute paraṁ yatas*  
*tamo-'bhibhūto na nijaṁ paraṁ ca yat*

**Перевод:**

**Lying down on a valuable bed on the pillow of his wife’s arms, under extreme illusion, generous in giving charity, he thought his wife, not the** ***jīva*** **or the Supreme Lord, to be the ultimate goal in life, , because he was defeated by ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī has *amanita*. But that is not seen elsewhere.

### Text 7

*duhitṝr daśottara-śataṁ*  
*pitṛ-mātṛ-yaśaskarīḥ*  
*śīlaudārya-guṇopetāḥ*  
*paurañjanyaḥ prajā-pate*

**Перевод:**

**O Prajāpati! He begot a hundred and ten daughters. They were generous, full of good qualities and behavior, giving fame to their father and mother.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *bhrātṛ* is seen instead *of mātṛ*. This is not approved by Citsukha.

### Text 8

*sa pañcāla-patiḥ putrān*  
*pitṛ-vaṁśa-vivardhanān*  
*dāraiḥ saṁyojayām āsa*  
*duhitṝḥ sadṛśair varaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**King Purañjana, lord of Pañcāla, in order to increase the descendants of his paternal family, got his sons married with wives and got his daughters married with husbands.**

**Комментарий:**

He married his sons with suitable wives, matching good form and good fragrance with good form and fragrance.

### Text 10

*teṣu tad-riktha-hāreṣu*  
*gṛha-kośānujīviṣu*  
*nirūḍhena mamatvena*  
*viṣayeṣv anvabadhyata*

**Перевод:**

**He became attached to enjoyment, retinue, treasury, house and his plundering sons because of deep-rooted possessiveness.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes a*nvaruddhyata* is seen instead of *anvabadhyata*. However this is not accepted by Citsukha.

### Text 11

*īje ca kratubhir ghorair*  
*dīkṣitaḥ paśu-mārakaiḥ*  
*devān pitṝn bhūta-patīn*  
*nānā-kāmo yathā bhavān*

**Перевод:**

**Having many desires, he worshipped the** ***devatās,*** **Pitṛs, leaders of ghosts, using violent sacrifices with animal slaughter, just like you.**

**Комментарий:**

The phrase “just like you” can be applied to other verses as well.

### Text 12

*yukteṣv evaṁ pramattasya*  
*kuṭumbāsakta-cetasaḥ*  
*āsasāda sa vai kālo*  
*yo 'priyaḥ priya-yoṣitām*

**Перевод:**

**Inattentive to beneficial acts and absorbed in family life, he reached old age, which is disliked by the women.**

**Комментарий:**

Instead of *yo ‘priyaḥ* sometimes *yo ‘vara* is seen.

### Text 13-14

*caṇḍavega iti khyāto*  
*gandharvādhipatir nṛpa*  
*gandharvās tasya balinaḥ*  
*ṣaṣṭy-uttara-śata-trayam*  
*gandharvyas tādṛśīr asya*  
*maithunyaś ca sitāsitāḥ*  
*parivṛttyā vilumpanti*  
*sarva-kāma-vinirmitām*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Caṇḍavega, King of the Gandharvas and his 360 powerful Gandharva soldiers, paired with female Gandharvas, some of whom were white and some of whom were black, circled the city built to fulfill all desires and plundered it.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Another version has *tādṛśī.*

### Text 16

*sa saptabhiḥ śatair eko*  
*viṁśatyā ca śataṁ samāḥ*  
*purañjana-purādhyakṣo*  
*gandharvair yuyudhe balī*

**Перевод:**

**The five-hooded serpent, the superintendent of the city of King Purañjana, actually fought alone with the 720 Gandharvas for one hundred years.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *viṁśadbhiḥ* instead *viṁśatyā. Viṁśakena* is another version.

### Text 24

*ṛṣabhaṁ yavanānāṁ tvāṁ*  
*vṛṇe vīrepsitaṁ patim*  
*saṅkalpas tvayi bhūtānāṁ*  
*kṛtaḥ kila na riṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**O hero! I accept you, best of the Yavanas, as my desired husband, since the desires of the people should not be destroyed.**

**Комментарий:**

The desire you have for other beings should not be destroyed. Thus I should not be fully dedicated to you alone?

### Text 29

*tvam avyakta-gatir bhuṅkṣva*  
*lokaṁ karma-vinirmitam*  
*yā hi me pṛtanā-yuktā*  
*prajā-nāśaṁ praṇeṣyasi*

**Перевод:**

**With invisible movement, enjoy this world made of** ***karma.*** **Assisted by my troops, you will destroy the population.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *prajvārastvām* instead of *prajā-nāśam*.

### Text 30

*prajvāro 'yaṁ mama bhrātā*  
*tvaṁ ca me bhaginī bhava*  
*carāmy ubhābhyāṁ loke 'sminn*  
*avyakto bhīma-sainikaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**This is my brother Prajvāra. Become my sister. I shall move about with you two in this world, invisibly, with my fearful soldiers.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *aprajo ‘yam* instead of *prajvāro ‘yam.*

## Chapter Twenty-eight

### Text 1

*nārada uvāca*  
*sainikā bhaya-nāmno ye*  
*barhiṣman diṣṭa-kāriṇaḥ*  
*prajvāra-kāla-kanyābhyāṁ*  
*vicerur avanīm imām*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: O King Prācīnabarhiṣat! The soldiers of Fear, the fulfillers of** ***karma,*** **along with Prajvāra and the old age, began to travel the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses go together.

### Text 9

*kāmān abhilaṣan dīno*  
*yāta-yāmāṁś ca kanyayā*  
*vigatātma-gati-snehaḥ*  
*putra-dārāṁś ca lālayan*

**Перевод:**

**Though he desired to eat, the foods were useless. By the influence of old age, he was miserable, having lost a higher status in next life and having lost the affection of his sons and others in this life. But he remained affectionate to sons and wife.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Citsukha has *sukhi* instead of *gati.*

### Text 10

*gandharva-yavanākrāntāṁ*  
*kāla-kanyopamarditām*  
*hātuṁ pracakrame rājā*  
*tāṁ purīm anikāmataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Though he did not desire to do, the King began to abandon the city which was being crushed by old age and attacked by the Gandharvas and Yavanas.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *anicchayāp*i instead of *anikāmataḥ.*

### Text 16

*duhitṝḥ putra-pautrāṁś ca*  
*jāmi-jāmātṛ-pārṣadān*  
*svatvāvaśiṣṭaṁ yat kiñcid*  
*gṛha-kośa-paricchadam*  
*ahaṁ mameti svīkṛtya*  
*gṛheṣu kumatir gṛhī*  
*dadhyau pramadayā dīno*  
*viprayoga upasthite*

**Перевод:**

**Since he was a foolish householder, accepting me and mine concerning his household, he became miserable at impending separation from his wife. He began to think of his daughters, sons, grandsons, daughters-in-law, sons-in-law, followers, remaining property, house, treasury and furniture.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 20

*prabodhayati māvijñaṁ*  
*vyuṣite śoka-karśitā*  
*vartmaitad gṛha-medhīyaṁ*  
*vīra-sūr api neṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**She would instruct me when I was foolish. When I was away, she became thin because of lamentation. Having many sons, how will she will be able to perform household duties?**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *māṁ vijñam* instead of *māvijñam.*

### Text 27-28

*ananta-pāre tamasi*  
*magno naṣṭa-smṛtiḥ samāḥ*  
*śāśvatīr anubhūyārtiṁ*  
*pramadā-saṅga-dūṣitaḥ*  
*tām eva manasā gṛhṇan*  
*babhūva pramadottamā*  
*anantaraṁ vidarbhasya*  
*rāja-siṁhasya veśmani*

**Перевод:**

**Merged in unending darkness, devoid of memory, experiencing pain for infinite years, and, contaminated by association with a woman, he remembered in his mind that woman, and finally was born in the house of a great king of Vidarbha as the best of women.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. He attained a mind fixed in dharma as previously. He was born in a house practicing dharma.

### Text 29

*upayeme vīrya-paṇāṁ*  
*vaidarbhīṁ malayadhvajaḥ*  
*yudhi nirjitya rājanyān*  
*pāṇḍyaḥ para-purañjayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Intelligent Malayadhvaja, the conqueror of enemy cities, after defeating other kings in battle, married the daughter of the King of Vidarbha, as prize for his chivalry.**

**Комментарий:**

In debate *(yudhi*) Malayadhvaja had conquered kings who were following karma-kāṇḍa.

### Text 30

*tasyāṁ sa janayāṁ cakra*  
*ātmajām asitekṣaṇām*  
*yavīyasaḥ sapta sutān*  
*sapta draviḍa-bhūbhṛtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In her, he begot a daughter named Asiteksaṇā and seven younger sons who became kings of seven southern provinces.**

**Комментарий:**

Seven indicates seven devotional processes. Sakhya and ātma-nivedana are attained later by qualification.

### Text 32

*agastyaḥ prāg duhitaram*  
*upayeme dhṛta-vratām*  
*yasyāṁ dṛḍhacyuto jāta*  
*idhmavāhātmajo muniḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The sage Agastya married the first-born daughter of Malayadhvaja, who had firm vows, in whom one son named Dṛḍhacyuta was born. Dṛḍhacyuta had Idhmavāha as his son.**

**Комментарий:**

Agastya a great devotee who was there, or someone equal to him, married the first daughter, who was absorbed in serving Kṛṣṇa. He agreed to this by his own will. She had rati by there were firm vows of sense control etc. Thus there was impossibility of obstacles (*dṛdha-cyutah*). This refers to the appearance of bhakti-yoga which bathes all the senses. The cause of the absence of obstacles was contemplation (*muniḥ*). Thus there was a son called Idhmavāhaḥ. He produced many vaiṣṇavas as disciples or assistants.

### Text 33

*vibhajya tanayebhyaḥ kṣmāṁ*  
*rājarṣir malayadhvajaḥ*  
*ārirādhayiṣuḥ kṛṣṇaṁ*  
*sa jagāma kulācalam*

**Перевод:**

**The saintly king Malayadhvaja, desiring to worship Kṛṣṇa after dividing the earth among his sons, departed to Kulācala.**

**Комментарий:**

Like Pṛthu he later performed austerity and jñāna, though his interest was worshipping Kṛṣṇa. Kulācalam means Malayam.

### Text 34

*hitvā gṛhān sutān bhogān*  
*vaidarbhī madirekṣaṇā*  
*anvadhāvata pāṇḍyeśaṁ*  
*jyotsneva rajanī-karam*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the house, sons and enjoyments, the daughter of King Vidarbha with attractive eyes followed her husband, just as moon light follows the moon.**

**Комментарий:**

Madirekṣaṇā means she saw the form of the Lord (*madira*), who gives joy. She followed her husband, accepting him as guru and the Lord (*pāṇḍyeśam*).

### Text 35-36

*tatra candravasā nāma*  
*tāmraparṇī vaṭodakā*  
*tat-puṇya-salilair nityam*  
*ubhayatrātmano mṛjan*  
*kandāṣṭibhir mūla-phalaiḥ*  
*puṣpa-parṇais tṛṇodakaiḥ*  
*vartamānaḥ śanair gātra-*  
*karśanaṁ tapa āsthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**At Kulācala the King bathed daily internally and externally in the Candravaṣa, Tāmraparṇī and Vaṭodakā Rivers. Undergoing austerities, subsisting on roots, seeds, bulbs, fruits, flowers, leaves, grass and water, his body eventually became thin.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. Citsukha has *candravatām nāma*. Citsukha and Puṇyāraṇya have *kandādibhiḥ* instead of *kandāṣṭibhiḥ.*

### Text 38

*tapasā vidyayā pakva-*  
*kaṣāyo niyamair yamaiḥ*  
*yuyuje brahmaṇy ātmānaṁ*  
*vijitākṣānilāśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Burning all impurities by austerity, knowledge,** ***niyama*** **and** ***yama,*** **and conquering his senses, life air and mind, he fixed his mind on Vāsudeva.**

**Комментарий:**

He fixed the pure jīva *(ātmānam*) on Bhagavān, the shelter of all (*brahmaṇi*). This means he remembered the Lord as his only shelter, just as the ray or photon takes shelter of the sun.

### Text 39

*āste sthāṇur ivaikatra*  
*divyaṁ varṣa-śataṁ sthiraḥ*  
*vāsudeve bhagavati*  
*nānyad vedodvahan ratim*

**Перевод:**

**For a hundred years of the** ***devatās*** **he remained fixed in one place. Possessing** ***rati*** **for the Supreme Lord Vāsudeva, he was not aware of anything else.**

**Комментарий:**

By that remembrance he developed continuous rati for the Lord.

### Text 40

*sa vyāpakatayātmānaṁ*  
*vyatiriktatayātmani*  
*vidvān svapna ivāmarśa-*  
*sākṣiṇaṁ virarāma ha*

**Перевод:**

**Momentarily realizing the Lord externally in all things and internally as well, he then fainted. It was like seeing a dream, whose witness is internal reflection.**

**Комментарий:**

Realization of the Lord developed, which was different from anything else. Knowing the Paramātmā (*ātmānam*), the basis of the pure jīva (*ātmani*) as an all pervading form he rejected what he previously knew. An example of the Lord’s all pervasive nature and distinct nature is given. He saw that the Lord was like the seer of the whole world in a dream. Sambandhokti has *āmṛśya* instead of *āmarśa.*

### Text 41-42

*sākṣād bhagavatoktena*  
*guruṇā hariṇā nṛpa*  
*viśuddha-jñāna-dīpena*  
*sphuratā viśvato-mukham*  
*pare brahmaṇi cātmānaṁ*  
*paraṁ brahma tathātmani*  
*vīkṣamāṇo vihāyekṣām*  
*asmād upararāma ha*

**Перевод:**

**O King! By direct vision of the Lord who takes away all suffering, shining brightly everywhere, by the lamp of pure knowledge taught by** ***guru*** **concerning the Lord’s sweetness, he saw himself possessing love for the Lord and saw the Lord possessing love for him, and after losing that vision because of fainting, he gave up all connection with the gross and subtle bodies.**

**Комментарий:**

By that realization he attained the state of jīvanmukta. By that he obtained complete liberation. He saw himself taking shelter of the Lord (*pare brahmaṇi).* He saw the Lord in the pure ātmā, as its deity. He gave up efforts at seeing the world and became indifferent to the gross and subtle upādhis (*asmāt*). But he did not give up seeing the Lord caused by the appearance of the cit-śakti. As time and air remain when other elements disappear, so the cit-śakti and the Lord remained.

### Text 43

*patiṁ parama-dharma-jñaṁ*  
*vaidarbhī malayadhvajam*  
*premṇā paryacarad dhitvā*  
*bhogān sā pati-devatā*

**Перевод:**

**Vaidarbhī, seeing her husband as the Lord, served her husband, the highest knower of** ***dharma,*** **with devotion, giving up all enjoyment.**

**Комментарий:**

Vaidharbhī, who was Purañjana, attained the goal by serving her husband.

### Text 45

*ajānatī priyatamaṁ*  
*yadoparatam aṅganā*  
*susthirāsanam āsādya*  
*yathā-pūrvam upācarat*

**Перевод:**

**When he passed away, she approached her dear husband sitting in a fixed position and served him as before, unaware of his passing.**

**Комментарий:**

She served him as previously, offering foot water etc. though he has not moving, like a deity form.

### Text 50

*citiṁ dārumayīṁ citvā*  
*tasyāṁ patyuḥ kalevaram*  
*ādīpya cānumaraṇe*  
*vilapantī mano dadhe*

**Перевод:**

**After piling up wood and lighting fire on the body of her husband on the pyre, in a state of lamentation, she decided to die with her husband.**

**Комментарий:**

From the spiritual point of view, the actions as a woman etc. are metaphorical only.

### Text 51

*tatra pūrvataraḥ kaścit*  
*sakhā brāhmaṇa ātmavān*  
*sāntvayan valgunā sāmnā*  
*tām āha rudatīṁ prabho*

**Перевод:**

**In that place, as she wailed, “O master!” a previous** ***brāhmaṇa*** **friend, with composed nature, pacifying her with sweet words, spoke.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord took the form of a brāhmaṇa and spoke.

### Text 53

*api smarasi cātmānam*  
*avijñāta-sakhaṁ sakhe*  
*hitvā māṁ padam anvicchan*  
*bhauma-bhoga-rato gataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Do you remember yourself, who had an unknown friend? O friend! Giving me up, desiring a place, you became engaged in material enjoyment.**

**Комментарий:**

He speaks so that she (Purañjana) can remember himself as a jīva.

### Text 54

*haṁsāv ahaṁ ca tvaṁ cārya*  
*sakhāyau mānasāyanau*  
*abhūtām antarā vaukaḥ*  
*sahasra-parivatsarān*

**Перевод:**

**O noble one! I and you who are two swans, friends in Mānasa Lake, previously, for a thousand years, were without a house.**

**Комментарий:**

In Paramātmā Sandarbha it is explained that non-difference of jīva and the Lord is described for persons desiring jñāna.

*ahaṁ bhavān na cānyas tvaṁ tvam evāhaṁ vicakṣva bhoḥ*  
*na nau paśyanti kavayaś chidraṁ jātu manāg api*

**I and you are different, but you are not something different from me. Ah! See without anger! The wise never see difference in us at all. (SB** [**4.28.62**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/28.62)**)**

However, since that is not satisfactory, here an explanation is given indicating bhakti. Friendship between the Lord and jīva is established. At the time of creation, we two friends were both at Mānasa Lake. At the time of mahā-pralaya (final destruction) however, the two were without upādhis (*okaḥ*). A thousand years represents the period of mahā-pralaya.

### Text 55

*sa tvaṁ vihāya māṁ bandho*  
*gato grāmya-matir mahīm*  
*vicaran padam adrākṣīḥ*  
*kayācin nirmitaṁ striyā*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! Leaving me, with material consciousness you went to the earth. Wandering about, you saw a place made by some woman, by** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

The reason for forgetting the friend is described in six and half verses. This verse is paired with the next. Giving me up at the time of creation, you, covered by aham and mama, went to the place of waking and dream states (*mahīm*). Pondering (*vicaran*), you saw a place.

### Text 57

*pañcendriyārthā ārāmā*  
*dvāraḥ prāṇā nava prabho*  
*tejo-'b-annāni koṣṭhāni*  
*kulam indriya-saṅgrahaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The five sense objects are the gardens. The gates are nine openings of the body. The storerooms are fire, water and earth. The communities are the senses and mind combined.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses further describe the body.

### Text 59

*tasmiṁs tvaṁ rāmayā spṛṣṭo*  
*ramamāṇo 'śruta-smṛtiḥ*  
*tat-saṅgād īdṛśīṁ prāpto*  
*daśāṁ pāpīyasīṁ prabho*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! Having entered that place with the woman, enjoying, without hearing about knowledge of the self, you have attained this sinful condition by association with her.**

**Комментарий:**

O person capable of all sādhanas (*vibho*)! (instead of *prabho*).

*māyā hy eṣā mayā sṛṣṭā*  
*yat pumāṁsaṁ striyaṁ satīm*  
*manyase nobhayaṁ yad vai*  
*haṁsau paśyāvayor gatim*

**This** ***māyā*** **has been created by me. By this** ***māyā,*** **you think you are a pure woman, a man or a eunuch. Therefore, you should see our natures as two pure entities. (SB** [**4.28.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/28.61)**)**

Citsukha has *haṁsam* instead of *haṁsau*. Another version has *paśyātha nau gatim.* You should see me.

### Text 62

*ahaṁ bhavān na cānyas tvaṁ*  
*tvam evāhaṁ vicakṣva bhoḥ*  
*na nau paśyanti kavayaś*  
*chidraṁ jātu manāg api*

**Перевод:**

**I and you are different, but you are not something different from me. Ah! See without anger! The wise never see us in a faulty manner at all.**

**Комментарий:**

Having established the purity suitable of friendship, the Lord establishes oneness of the two.

### Text 63

*yathā puruṣa ātmānam*  
*ekam ādarśa-cakṣuṣoḥ*  
*dvidhābhūtam avekṣeta*  
*tathaivāntaram āvayoḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a man, though one entity, will the image of himself in a mirror and in the eye are different, you should see that I and you are also different.**

**Комментарий:**

“Though we are different from each other, why do you control māyā, whereas I am subject to it? You have already shown this difference:

*māyā hy eṣā mayā sṛṣṭā yat pumāṁsaṁ striyaṁ satīm*  
*manyase nobhayaṁ yad vai haṁsau paśyāvayor gatim*

**This** ***māyā*** **has been created by me. By this** ***māyā,*** **you think you are a pure woman, a man or a eunuch. Therefore, you should see our natures as two pure entities. (SB** [**4.28.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/28.61)**)**

"And why are we friends?” That is explained in this verse.

The meaning of the verse is this. The self appears in a mirror or reflective object as another form of one’s body. Or in the case of one’s eye, the self identifies with that form of light emanating from that sense organ. In this way one form is seen as two. Similarly, you should see that we two are different, since the pure jīva, my aṁśa or ray, becomes almost like a reflection in the upādhis of matter. Explaining the eye as the medium for an inferior reflection and rejecting one’s own eye and speaking of someone else’s eye is not acceptable. One’s own eye is clear from the verse. Using the example of oil, is not acceptable. Difference with inherent non-difference is indicated to confirm the relationship as friends. This was stated at the beginning with “Do you know that I am your friends? We two swans are friends.”

### Text 64

*evaṁ sa mānaso haṁso*  
*haṁsena pratibodhitaḥ*  
*sva-sthas tad-vyabhicāreṇa*  
*naṣṭām āpa punaḥ smṛtim*

**Перевод:**

**Thus one swan, after being instructed by the other swan who was situated in without material coverings, regained his memory which was lost by aversion to the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

The sage summarizes the story. In a state devoid of covering of material energy (*svasthaḥ*), he gained his previous memory which was destroyed by aversion to the one swam who was the Lord (*tad-vyabhicāreṇa*). Previously he said, “Do you remember my friendship? Do you remember that I am the unknown friend?” He regained that arrangement of friendship. The word *punaḥ* (again) and *smṛti* (remembrance), indicating the destruction of forgetfulness, refer to a continuous process. It is actually without beginning since the nature of the jīva is to be covered without beginning. If it had a beginning then it would acquire effects not caused by actions previously performed (it would get a body without deserving it, since it had no karma in the beginning).

### Text 65

*barhiṣmann etad adhyātmaṁ*  
*pārokṣyeṇa pradarśitam*  
*yat parokṣa-priyo devo*  
*bhagavān viśva-bhāvanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King Prācīnabarhi! I have given spiritual instructions indirectly through an allegory, since the Lord, acting for the welfare of the universe, is fond of indirect expression.**

**Комментарий:**

In relation to this, he makes clear the affection of the Lord.

## Chapter Twenty-nine

### Text 1

*prācīnabarhir uvāca*  
*bhagavaṁs te vaco 'smābhir*  
*na samyag avagamyate*  
*kavayas tad vijānanti*  
*na vayaṁ karma-mohitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Prācīnabarhi said: O great devotee! I cannot completely understand your words. The wise understand, but I cannot, since I am bewildered by** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

He understood the story but not its meaning in terms of the ātmā.

### Text 3

*yo 'vijñātāhṛtas tasya*  
*puruṣasya sakheśvaraḥ*  
*yan na vijñāyate pumbhir*  
*nāmabhir vā kriyā-guṇaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who was described as unknown, the friend of the j*****īva*****, is the Supreme Lord, since man does not recognize him by his names, his actions or his qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *āhvaya* instead of *āhṛta.* Persons who do not know scriptures do not know about the Lord, since the names, forms etc. are known from scripture alone.

### Text 5

*buddhiṁ tu pramadāṁ vidyān*  
*mamāham iti yat-kṛtam*  
*yām adhiṣṭhāya dehe 'smin*  
*pumān bhuṅkte 'kṣabhir guṇān*

**Перевод:**

**Know that the woman is intelligence (actually ignorance), which produces the concepts of I and mine, taking shelter of which, the** ***jīva*** **enjoys the** ***guṇas*** **with his senses in the body.**

**Комментарий:**

In the waking state intelligence thinks in terms of me and mine. Identity in sleep state follows after that.

### Text 8

*akṣiṇī nāsike karṇau*  
*mukhaṁ śiśna-gudāv iti*  
*dve dve dvārau bahir yāti*  
*yas tad-indriya-saṁyutaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The nine gates are the two eyes, two nostrils, two eyes, mouth, penis and anus. The** ***jīva*** **goes out along with the senses through the doors.**

**Комментарий:**

The mouth also has two senses—speech and taste, considered as one.

### Text 9

*akṣiṇī nāsike āsyam*  
*iti pañca puraḥ kṛtāḥ*  
*dakṣiṇā dakṣiṇaḥ karṇa*  
*uttarā cottaraḥ smṛtaḥ*  
*paścime ity adho dvārau*  
*gudaṁ śiśnam ihocyate*

**Перевод:**

**The two eyes, two nostrils and mouth are the eastern gates and the two ears are the southern and northern gates. The penis and anus are the western gates.**

**Комментарий:**

This is a six line verse.

### Text 10

*khadyotāvirmukhī cātra*  
*netre ekatra nirmite*  
*rūpaṁ vibhrājitaṁ tābhyāṁ*  
*vicaṣṭe cakṣuṣeśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The two gates named Khadyotā and Āvirmukhī which have been mentioned are the two eyes constructed side by side. The city called Vibhrājita is the sense object called form. The j*****īva*** **sees by the eye sense organ.**

**Комментарий:**

SB [4.25.47](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/25.47) mentions the name of the eye as Dyuman.

### Text 14

*āsurī meḍhram arvāg-dvār*  
*vyavāyo grāmiṇāṁ ratiḥ*  
*upastho durmadaḥ prokto*  
*nirṛtir guda ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**Āsurī, the lower door, is the penis. Sex life is the place called Grāmaka. Durmada is the sense organ called the penis. The gate called Nirṛti is the anus.**

**Комментарий:**

Citsukha has *durmada.*

### Text 17

*yathā yathā vikriyate*  
*guṇākto vikaroti vā*  
*tathā tathopadraṣṭātmā*  
*tad-vṛttīr anukāryate*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever changes the intelligence undergoes during sleep or however it transforms the senses during waking state, the** ***jīva,*** **contaminated by the qualities of the intelligence, imitates the intelligence’s action**

**Комментарий:**

This is the correct version of the verse.

### Text 18-20

*deho rathas tv indriyāśvaḥ*  
*saṁvatsara-rayo 'gatiḥ*  
*dvi-karma-cakras tri-guṇa-*  
*dhvajaḥ pañcāsu-bandhuraḥ*  
*mano-raśmir buddhi-sūto*  
*hṛn-nīḍo dvandva-kūbaraḥ*  
*pañcendriyārtha-prakṣepaḥ*  
*sapta-dhātu-varūthakaḥ*  
*ākūtir vikramo bāhyo*  
*mṛga-tṛṣṇāṁ pradhāvati*  
*ekādaśendriya-camūḥ*  
*pañca-sūnā-vinoda-kṛt*

**Перевод:**

**The chariot is the body. The horses are the senses. Its speed is the force of time. The two wheels are sin and piety. The three flags are the** ***guṇas.*** **The five connecting rods are the five life airs. The bridle is the mind. The driver is the intelligence. The seat is the heart. The two poles attached to the yoke are happiness and distress. The five weapons are the five sense objects. The seven coverings are the seven** ***dhātus.*** **The five exploits are the actions of the action senses. The eleven commanders are the eleven senses. Going hunting on the chariot means seeking material pleasure through the five senses.**

**Комментарий:**

*Ākūtiḥ* refers to the five action senses.

### Text 21

*saṁvatsaraś caṇḍavegaḥ*  
*kālo yenopalakṣitaḥ*  
*tasyāhānīha gandharvā*  
*gandharvyo rātrayaḥ smṛtāḥ*  
*haranty āyuḥ parikrāntyā*  
*ṣaṣṭy-uttara-śata-trayam*

**Перевод:**

**Caṇḍavega is the year by which time is perceived. The Gandharvas are the days and the female Gandharvas are the nights. By the rotation of 360 days and nights the life span decreases.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes *parisṛtyā* is seen instead of *parikrāntyā.*

### Text 23-25

*ādhayo vyādhayas tasya*  
*sainikā yavanāś carāḥ*  
*bhūtopasargāśu-rayaḥ*  
*prajvāro dvi-vidho jvaraḥ*  
*evaṁ bahu-vidhair duḥkhair*  
*daiva-bhūtātma-sambhavaiḥ*  
*kliśyamānaḥ śataṁ varṣaṁ*  
*dehe dehī tamo-vṛtaḥ*  
*prāṇendriya-mano-dharmān*  
*ātmany adhyasya nirguṇaḥ*  
*śete kāma-lavān dhyāyan*  
*mamāham iti karma-kṛt*

**Перевод:**

**The soldiers of the King of the Yavanas are mental and physical diseases. He experiences difficulties in the body due to the elements. Prajvāra is fever of two types. Thus the** ***jīva*****, covered by ignorance, dwells in the body for a hundred years, suffering from various pains due to body, mind and higher causes. Imposing the qualities of the life air, senses and mind on his soul, though he is actually without material qualities, he performs actions, meditating on me and mine, and lies down on particles of enjoyment.**

**Комментарий:**

The verses go together.

### Text 26-27

*yadātmānam avijñāya*  
*bhagavantaṁ paraṁ gurum*  
*puruṣas tu viṣajjeta*  
*guṇeṣu prakṛteḥ sva-dṛk*  
*guṇābhimānī sa tadā*  
*karmāṇi kurute 'vaśaḥ*  
*śuklaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ lohitaṁ vā*  
*yathā-karmābhijāyate*

**Перевод:**

**When the** ***jīva,*** **self seer, forgetting the Supreme Lord, the supreme** ***guru,*** **identifies with the** ***guṇas*** **and becomes attached to the** ***guṇas*****, he helplessly performs actions in** ***sattva, rajas*** **or** ***tamas*****, and takes birth according to his actions.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 30-31

*kṣut-parīto yathā dīnaḥ*  
*sārameyo gṛhaṁ gṛham*  
*caran vindati yad-diṣṭaṁ*  
*daṇḍam odanam eva vā*  
*tathā kāmāśayo jīva*  
*uccāvaca-pathā bhraman*  
*upary adho vā madhye vā*  
*yāti diṣṭaṁ priyāpriyam*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a wretched dog, afflicted with hunger, wandering from house to house, by fate sometimes gets the stick and sometimes gets food, the j*****īva*****, full of desires, wandering on high or low roads, by fate attains enjoyable or disagreeable bodies in the higher, middle or lower planets.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 39-40

*yatra bhāgavatā rājan*  
*sādhavo viśadāśayāḥ*  
*bhagavad-guṇānukathana-*  
*śravaṇa-vyagra-cetasaḥ*  
*tasmin mahan-mukharitā madhubhic-caritra-*  
*pīyūṣa-śeṣa-saritaḥ paritaḥ sravanti*  
*tā ye pibanty avitṛṣo nṛpa gāḍha-karṇais*  
*tān na spṛśanty aśana-tṛḍ-bhaya-śoka-mohāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Where there are devotees with pure hearts and proper conduct, who are eager to hear discussions of the qualities of the Lord, profuse streams of nectar of the pastimes of the Lord emanating from the mouths of the saintly devotees, the** ***mahāprasāda*** **of the devotees, flow everywhere. Those who drink that nectar with firm ears, with constant thirst, O King, will not be touched by hunger, thirst, fear, lamentation and illusion.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotees are eager to hear and discuss the qualities of the Lord. The discussion does not take effort and does not depend on other sādhanas. Nor is it interrupted. Thus it flows like rivers of nectar.

### Text 41

*etair upadruto nityaṁ*  
*jīva-lokaḥ svabhāvajaiḥ*  
*na karoti harer nūnaṁ*  
*kathāmṛta-nidhau ratim*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are constantly disturbed by habitual hunger, thirst, fear, lamentation and illusion can never develop attraction for the ocean of nectar of topics concerning the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

By the power of association with great devotees, the topics produce rati which destroys all obstacles.

### Text 42-44

*prajāpati-patiḥ sākṣād*  
*bhagavān giriśo manuḥ*  
*dakṣādayaḥ prajādhyakṣā*  
*naiṣṭhikāḥ sanakādayaḥ*  
*marīcir atry-aṅgirasau*  
*pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ*  
*bhṛgur vasiṣṭha ity ete*  
*mad-antā brahma-vādinaḥ*  
*adyāpi vācas-patayas*  
*tapo-vidyā-samādhibhiḥ*  
*paśyanto 'pi na paśyanti*  
*paśyantaṁ parameśvaram*

**Перевод:**

**Omniscient Brahmā, the father of all progenitors, Śiva, Manu, Dakṣa and the other rulers of humankind, the four Kumāras, Marīci, Atri, Aṅgirā, Pulastya, Pulaha, Kratu, Bhṛgu, Vasiṣṭha, and I as well, though we know the Vedas and skillful at speaking and, though even today we reflect upon the Supreme Lord by austerity, knowledge and concentration, cannot see the Lord who sees everything.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowledge of the Lord cannot arise in persons like us by other sādhanas. Only hearing the topics will do this. This is expressed in four verses by negation. Brahmā, Śiva and Manu performed austerities, and meditated with concentration, worshiping the Lord. Though they see the Lord, according to their knowledge, they do not see him.

### Text 45

*śabda-brahmaṇi duṣpāre*  
*caranta uru-vistare*  
*mantra-liṅgair vyavacchinnaṁ*  
*bhajanto na viduḥ param*

**Перевод:**

**Those who study the Vedas, vast in meaning and words, and who worship the** ***devatās*** **distinguished by typical markings and** ***mantras*****, do not know the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who are fixed in karmas do not know the Lord.

### Text 46

*yadā yasyānugṛhṇāti*  
*bhagavān ātma-bhāvitaḥ*  
*sa jahāti matiṁ loke*  
*vede ca pariniṣṭhitām*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord shows mercy to the devotee, the devotee gives up thinking of** ***karma-kāṇḍa*** **and material affairs.**

**Комментарий:**

The mercy of the Lord, which depends on particular circumstances, according to the faith in great devotees, gives bhakti which disregards everything else. When the Lord gives mercy, the person, becoming pure in heart (*ātma-bhāvitaḥ*) by hearing topics of the Lord from great devotees, he gives up being fixed in material affairs (*loke*) and karma-kāṇḍa (*vede*).

### Text 47

*tasmāt karmasu barhiṣmann*  
*ajñānād artha-kāśiṣu*  
*mārtha-dṛṣṭiṁ kṛthāḥ śrotra-*  
*sparśiṣv aspṛṣṭa-vastuṣu*

**Перевод:**

**O King Prācīnabarhiṣat! Therefore, out of ignorance, never make your ultimate goal of life prescribed** ***karmas,*** **which appear to be legitimate goals, which are pleasing to hear about, but which do not produce substantial results.**

**Комментарий:**

Karmas appear to be of value (*artha-kāśiṣu*).

### Text 48

*svaṁ lokaṁ na vidus te vai*  
*yatra devo janārdanaḥ*  
*āhur dhūmra-dhiyo vedaṁ*  
*sakarmakam atad-vidaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Foolish people do not know the Lord’s planet where the Lord resides. Not knowing the Vedas, they speak of the Vedas in terms of prescribed** ***karmas*** **alone.**

**Комментарий:**

The meaning of śruti is discussed in this verse. *ka itthā veda yatra saḥ*: who knows where the Lord exists? (Katha Upaniṣad)

The foolish people who say that the Veda recommend just karma do not know Vaikuṇṭha (*svam lokam*) of Janārdana, but know only Svarga. They do not know that in that place the highest action is bhakti.

### Text 49

*āstīrya darbhaiḥ prāg-agraiḥ*  
*kārtsnyena kṣiti-maṇḍalam*  
*stabdho bṛhad-vadhān mānī*  
*karma nāvaiṣi yat param*  
*tat karma hari-toṣaṁ yat*  
*sā vidyā tan-matir yayā*

**Перевод:**

**Covering the whole earth with** ***kuśa*** **grass, with tips facing east, thinking yourself a great sacrificer because of sacrificing animals, and misbehaving, you do not know the supreme activity. That activity which is pleasing the Lord is the real activity. That activity by which one concentrates on the Lord is knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

The highest action is shown. That action which pleases the Lord is real action since it must be done. That knowledge arising from scripture is that by which one realizes the Lord.

### Text 51

*sa vai priyatamaś cātmā*  
*yato na bhayam aṇv api*  
*iti veda sa vai vidvān*  
*yo vidvān sa gurur hariḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is most beloved, though the** ***jīva*** **is also dear, because from worshipping the Lord there is not the least fear or suffering. He who knows the two** ***ātmās*** **is learned. Such a learned person should be considered your** ***guru*** **and should be worshipped like the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

He teaches that one should take shelter of a qualified guru, indicating what was told in the story of Purañjana. It is well known (*vai*) that the Lord (*saḥ*) is most dear to all people, qualified for the highest affection since he is ātmā. Another version has *svātmā*: he is the ātmā of the jīvas (*sva*) who are like rays of the sun, because of whom there is no fear. He who knows this is learned. He is guru. Such a guru is also the Lord.

### Text 52

*nārada uvāca*  
*praśna evaṁ hi sañchinno*  
*bhavataḥ puruṣarṣabha*  
*atra me vadato guhyaṁ*  
*niśāmaya suniścitam*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: O best of men! Thus your question has been answered. Please hear from me a secret I have ascertained.**

**Комментарий:**

There are two sentences in this verse.

### Text 54

*sumanaḥ-sama-dharmaṇāṁ strīṇāṁ śaraṇa āśrame puṣpa-madhu-gandhavat kṣudratamaṁ kāmya-karma-vipākajaṁ kāma-sukha-lavaṁ jaihvyaupasthyādi vicinvantaṁ mithunī-bhūya tad-abhiniveśita-manasaṁ ṣaḍaṅghri-gaṇa-sāma-gītavad atimanohara-vanitādi-janālāpeṣv atitarām atipralobhita-karṇam agre vṛka-yūthavad ātmana āyur harato 'ho-rātrān tān kāla-lava-viśeṣān avigaṇayya gṛheṣu viharantaṁ pṛṣṭhata eva parokṣam anupravṛtto lubdhakaḥ kṛtānto 'ntaḥ śareṇa yam iha parāvidhyati tam imam ātmānam aho rājan bhinna-hṛdayaṁ draṣṭum arhasīti.*

**Перевод:**

**While you contemplate the drop of sense enjoyment from the tongue and genital arising as a result of** ***kāmya-karmas,*** **which is actually insignificant, like the sweet fragrance of flowers which fades away, in the** ***āśrama*** **or garden of women who are beautiful like flowers, with your mind absorbed in sex life like the deer with his mate, with your ears extremely greedy for attractive gossip with women, which similar to the songs of the bees, while you play in houses without considering each second of time or the days and the nights, which steal way your life like a pack of wolves hiding in front—death, like a unseen hunter lurking behind, pursues you with his arrow that pierces the heart. O King! You should understand that the deer, pierced in the heart, is you.**

**Комментарий:**

An additional statement at the end is sometimes seen: *yathā mṛagayu-hatam mṛgam.*

### Text 57

*saṁśayo 'tra tu me vipra*  
*sañchinnas tat-kṛto mahān*  
*ṛṣayo 'pi hi muhyanti*  
*yatra nendriya-vṛttayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! You have destroyed my great doubt created by these teachers by your teachings. Even sages who have controlled the senses are bewildered.**

**Комментарий:**

The doubt concerning bhakti has been destroyed, but one doubt concerning karma remains. It is not proper to destroy the doubt now but I desire that, to allay any remembrance of it.

### Text 58

*karmāṇy ārabhate yena*  
*pumān iha vihāya tam*  
*amutrānyena dehena*  
*juṣṭāni sa yad aśnute*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the body by which he performs actions, a person then enjoys the results in a different body on another planet after death.**

### Text 59

*iti veda-vidāṁ vādaḥ*  
*śrūyate tatra tatra ha*  
*karma yat kriyate proktaṁ*  
*parokṣaṁ na prakāśate*

**Перевод:**

**This statement is heard from the knowers of the Vedas. Also, the action prescribed in the Vedas that is performed disappears with the end of the act, and its future result also is absent.**

**Комментарий:**

Giving up the body by which he performs actions, he then enjoys by another body after that life actions accomplished by those previous actions. That is the conclusion of the knowers of the Vedas. An action disappears as soon as it occurs.
