How does enjoyment arise in another body, since the act cannot spread itself elsewhere as an impression, like the recollection of bathing or eating for instance, or as a subtle form, like the tanmātra of fire?

### Text 64

*nānubhūtaṁ kva cānena*  
*dehenādṛṣṭam aśrutam*  
*kadācid upalabhyeta*  
*yad rūpaṁ yādṛg ātmani*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes one experiences objects in the mind in the present body which have not been experienced, seen or heard in this life, with particular forms and varieties.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.

### Text 74

*evaṁ pañca-vidhaṁ liṅgaṁ*  
*tri-vṛt ṣoḍaśa vistṛtam*  
*eṣa cetanayā yukto*  
*jīva ity abhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**The form which consists of the five** ***prāṇas*** **and the three** ***guṇas,*** **which is furnished with the sixteen transformations (ten senses, five sense objects, and mind), and which is endowed with consciousness is called the subtle body.**

**Комментарий:**

The subtle body is called jīva because it is endowed with the consciousness of the jīva.

### Text 76-77

*yathā tṛṇa-jalūkeyaṁ*  
*nāpayāty apayāti ca*  
*na tyajen mriyamāṇo 'pi*  
*prāg-dehābhimatiṁ janaḥ*  
*yāvad anyaṁ na vindeta*  
*vyavadhānena karmaṇām*  
*mana eva manuṣyendra*  
*bhūtānāṁ bhava-bhāvanam*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a caterpillar does not give up one leaf while moving to the next leaf, the** ***jīva*** **does not give up his previous identity even while dying, until he accepts another body by firmly holding his** ***karmas.*** **O King of men! The mind of living beings is the cause of repeated birth.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. After giving up one body and before entering another body, the person will be without a body. This is explained. The caterpillar without giving up the previous leaf moves to the next leaf. Similarly, the jīva does not give up his previous identity when he is dying. The mind is the cause of rebirth.

### Text 78

*yadākṣaiś caritān dhyāyan*  
*karmāṇy ācinute 'sakṛt*  
*sati karmaṇy avidyāyāṁ*  
*bandhaḥ karmaṇy anātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When one performs actions by the senses while thinking of objects of enjoyment, bondage to action of the body occurs, since each of those actions have the seed of ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

Concerning ignorance, one should refer to the following:

*yayā sammohito jīva ātmānaṁ tri-guṇātmakam \|*  
*paro ’pi manute ’narthaṁ tat-kṛtaṁ cābhipadyate \|\|*

**Bewildered by that** ***māyā,*** **the** ***jīva*****, though separate from the three** ***guṇas,*** **considers himself made of the three** ***guṇas*** **and takes on material existence created by the** ***guṇas.*** **(SB** [**1.7.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/7.5)**)**

### Text 79

*atas tad apavādārthaṁ*  
*bhaja sarvātmanā harim*  
*paśyaṁs tad-ātmakaṁ viśvaṁ*  
*sthity-utpatty-apyayā yataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, to counteract ignorance, seeing the Supreme Lord as the whole universe, worship the Lord who creates maintains and destroys the universe with all your heart.**

**Комментарий:**

To counteract ignorance the following is necessary:

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.*** **(SB** [**11.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37)**)**

### Text 82

*tatraikāgra-manā dhīro*  
*govinda-caraṇāmbujam*  
*vimukta-saṅgo 'nubhajan*  
*bhaktyā tat-sāmyatām agāt*

**Перевод:**

**There, by fully concentrating his mind while being free of material contamination and continually worshipping the lotus feet of Govinda, he attained** ***sārūpya*** **by his devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

*Tat sāmyatām* means attaining a similar form.

### Text 84

*etan mukunda-yaśasā bhuvanaṁ punānaṁ*  
*devarṣi-varya-mukha-niḥsṛtam ātma-śaucam*  
*yaḥ kīrtyamānam adhigacchati pārameṣṭhyaṁ*  
*nāsmin bhave bhramati mukta-samasta-bandhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who studies this story filled with the glories of Mukunda which is purifying for the whole world and purifying for the soul, which flowed from the mouth of Nārada and is continually sung by Brahmā becomes free from all bondage and does not wander in the material world.**

**Комментарий:**

The story has the qualities described in verse 29.

### Text 85

*adhyātma-pārokṣyam idaṁ*  
*mayādhigatam adbhutam*  
*evaṁ striyāśramaḥ puṁsaś*  
*chinno 'mutra ca saṁśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I have studied this astounding spiritual allegory. By doing this, I am free from** ***ahaṅkāra*** **and doubts concerning how one experiences** ***karma*** **in the next life.**

**Комментарий:**

The form *striyāśraya* instead of *stryāśraya* is for meter.

## Chapter Thirty

### Text 5

*suparṇa-skandham ārūḍho*  
*meru-śṛṅgam ivāmbudaḥ*  
*pīta-vāsā maṇi-grīvaḥ*  
*kurvan vitimirā diśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Riding on the shoulders of Garuḍa, he appeared like a cloud on the peak of Mount Meru. Wearing yellow cloth, with a jewel around his neck, he illuminated the directions.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe the Lord.

### Text 6

*kāśiṣṇunā kanaka-varṇa-vibhūṣaṇena*  
*bhrājat-kapola-vadano vilasat-kirīṭaḥ*  
*aṣṭāyudhair anucarair munibhiḥ surendrair*  
*āsevito garuḍa-kinnara-gīta-kīrtiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**His cheeks shone because of his bright gold earrings with colorful gems. On his head was a brilliant crown. He was served by eight weapons, servants, sages, and** ***devatās.*** **Garuḍa and Kinnaras sang his glories.**

**Комментарий:**

The golden ornaments such as earrings shone (*kāśiṣnunā*) because of their astonishing nature, not because they were gold. He had meditated on the four armed form of the Lord of Vaikuṇṭha as taught by Śiva. But an eight armed form appeared. This was not to fulfill his desires, but to appease him.

### Text 8

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*varaṁ vṛṇīdhvaṁ bhadraṁ vo*  
*yūyaṁ me nṛpa-nandanāḥ*  
*sauhārdenāpṛthag-dharmās*  
*tuṣṭo 'haṁ sauhṛdena vaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: All fortune to you! Please choose a benediction, O sons of the King! By your mutual friendship you have the same qualities. I am pleased with your friendship.**

**Комментарий:**

Because of your natural affection, I am satisfied with you, my worshippers, because I have attained that affection—more than being satisfied by the worship itself.

### Text 9

*yo 'nusmarati sandhyāyāṁ*  
*yuṣmān anudinaṁ naraḥ*  
*tasya bhrātṛṣv ātma-sāmyaṁ*  
*tathā bhūteṣu sauhṛdam*

**Перевод:**

**One who remembers you brothers daily in the evening will have friendship to all beings and close friendship with his brothers.**

**Комментарий:**

There will be mutual affection among devotees with whom one lives.

### Text 20

*navyavad dhṛdaye yaj jño*  
*brahmaitad brahma-vādibhiḥ*  
*na muhyanti na śocanti*  
*na hṛṣyanti yato gatāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who have attained my form which is known as Brahman by the followers of Brahman are not bewildered. They do not lament and do not rejoice, since I, full of knowledge, have entered their fresh lotus hearts.**

**Комментарий:**

Because a person hears topics of the Lord, I, the Lord, enter (*aye*) the heart of the hearer in an ever-fresh manner (*navyavat*), through speakers about Brahman. That is Śrīdhara Svāmī’s explanation. Or I shine in the heart as if ever-fresh. This is called Brahman by the followers of Brahman.

### Text 21

*maitreya uvāca*  
*evaṁ bruvāṇaṁ puruṣārtha-bhājanaṁ*  
*janārdanaṁ prāñjalayaḥ pracetasaḥ*  
*tad-darśana-dhvasta-tamo-rajo-malā*  
*girāgṛṇan gadgadayā suhṛttamam*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: With folded hands, the Pracetās, devoid of all** ***tamas*** **and** ***rajas*** **by his presence, then offered prayers with choked voices to the Supreme Lord, the greatest friend, the vessel holding life’s goals, who had just spoken.**

**Комментарий:**

They were cleansed of rajas and tamas by seeing the Lord.

### Text 23

*śuddhāya śāntāya namaḥ sva-niṣṭhayā*  
*manasy apārthaṁ vilasad-dvayāya*  
*namo jagat-sthāna-layodayeṣu*  
*gṛhīta-māyā-guṇa-vigrahāya*

**Перевод:**

**We offer respects to you, who are pure, peaceful, fixed in your** ***svarūpa,*** **who make the dualities of the world useless to the mind, and who accept material forms such as Brahmā and Indra for creating, maintaining and destroying the world.**

**Комментарий:**

We offer respects to you who make the world of cause and effect, or the processes of karma and jñāna, useless to the mind of persons who are fixed in you. We offer respects to you who have forms which preside over the guṇas for creation, maintenance and destruction.

### Text 29

*yenopaśāntir bhūtānāṁ*  
*kṣullakānām apīhatām*  
*antarhito 'ntar-hṛdaye*  
*kasmān no veda nāśiṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**By your remembering your servants, the servants feel happiness. Since you are situated in the hearts of the most insignificant and greedy people, how can you, purifier of the mind, not know their desires?**

**Комментарий:**

They reveal their low condition.

### Text 30

*asāv eva varo 'smākam*  
*īpsito jagataḥ pate*  
*prasanno bhagavān yeṣām*  
*apavarga-gurur gatiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O master of the universe! Our desired boon is your mercy. The Lord should be pleased with those who have attained that mercy which surpasses liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Though we are fallen, we will attain your pleasure. We desire the boon of your mercy. That goal is greater than merging in Brahman (*apavarga-guruḥ*). Or the goal is obtaining an instructor of bhakti yoga (*apavarga-guruḥ*).

### Text 31

*varaṁ vṛṇīmahe 'thāpi*  
*nātha tvat parataḥ parāt*  
*na hy antas tvad-vibhūtīnāṁ*  
*so 'nanta iti gīyase*

**Перевод:**

**We ask for another boon from you who are the cause of all causes. Since there is no end to your splendor, you are called Ananta, the unlimited.**

**Комментарий:**

We ask a boon from you who are superior (*parāt*) to the best form called Brahman (*parataḥ*).

### Text 33

*yāvat te māyayā spṛṣṭā*  
*bhramāma iha karmabhiḥ*  
*tāvad bhavat-prasaṅgānāṁ*  
*saṅgaḥ syān no bhave bhave*

**Перевод:**

**As long as we wander about in this world because of our actions, contaminated by** ***māyā*****, we want the association of devotees, life after life.**

**Комментарий:**

By approaching your lotus feet, we attain freedom from saṁsāra and its touch of māyā. Thus we request devotee association at this time.

### Text 34

*tulayāma lavenāpi*  
*na svargaṁ nāpunar-bhavam*  
*bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasya*  
*martyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**We do not compare attainment of Svarga or liberation to even a moment’s association with a devotee, what to speak of comparing it to the enjoyments of this life.**

**Комментарий:**

One should not consider this a secondary element, because it cannot be compared to Svarga---enjoyment of worldly happiness--or liberation.

### Text 35

*yatreḍyante kathā mṛṣṭās*  
*tṛṣṇāyāḥ praśamo yataḥ*  
*nirvairaṁ yatra bhūteṣu*  
*nodvego yatra kaścana*

**Перевод:**

**In that association, pure topics of the Lord are discussed, from which all hankerings cease. In that association, there is no hostility to any living being. In that association there is no agitation.**

**Комментарий:**

The highest sādhana and sādhya of all had been shown. That is shown in three verses. All hankerings cease since the topics produce the highest attainment and thus one is without enemies.

### Text 36

*yatra nārāyaṇaḥ sākṣād*  
*bhagavān nyāsināṁ gatiḥ*  
*saṁstūyate sat-kathāsu*  
*mukta-saṅgaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In that association, Nārāyaṇa, the Supreme Lord, the goal of the** ***sannyāsīs,*** **is repeatedly praised by persons without material contamination through pure discussions.**

**Комментарий:**

The highest goal is shown.

### Text 37

*teṣāṁ vicaratāṁ padbhyāṁ*  
*tīrthānāṁ pāvanecchayā*  
*bhītasya kiṁ na roceta*  
*tāvakānāṁ samāgamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the devotees, with a desire to be purified, wander to the holy places, should not a person suffering fear of this world relish a meeting with those devotees?**

**Комментарий:**

Easy attainment of devotees, even though they are rare, is described. They wander about with attraction to purifying themselves by tīrthas or they desire to go there to purify their desires by touching the tīrthas with their feet, or they go there with a desire to attain the Lord who is the ultimate purifier of tīrthas. Or they go with a desire to purify the tīrthas. Or they desire to purify the people who go to the tīrthas. If a person who fears saṁsāra should relish meeting devotees, how much more a person who relishes the Lord will relish meeting devotees?

### Text 38

*vayaṁ tu sākṣād bhagavan bhavasya*  
*priyasya sakhyuḥ kṣaṇa-saṅgamena*  
*suduścikitsyasya bhavasya mṛtyor*  
*bhiṣaktamaṁ tvādya gatiṁ gatāḥ sma*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! By association with your dear friend Śiva, we have today attained you, the best remedy for birth and death, which are difficult to cure.**

**Комментарий:**

We do not pray out of fear, but with an understanding of the highest human goal, since by attaining a moment’s association with great devotees, one attains you, who destroys all fear. The word *tu* indicates difference from others--he is your dear friend. In spite of statements of non-difference between guru and the Lord and Śiva and the Lord, here is shown that Śiva is the Lord’s friend. This is the view of the pure devotees.

### Text 39-40

*yan naḥ svadhītaṁ guravaḥ prasāditā*  
*viprāś ca vṛddhāś ca sad-ānuvṛttyā*  
*āryā natāḥ suhṛdo bhrātaraś ca*  
*sarvāṇi bhūtāny anasūyayaiva*  
*yan naḥ sutaptaṁ tapa etad īśa*  
*nirandhasāṁ kālam adabhram apsu*  
*sarvaṁ tad etat puruṣasya bhūmno*  
*vṛṇīmahe te paritoṣaṇāya*

**Перевод:**

**We have studied and satisfied the teachers,** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and seniors in knowledge by our proper conduct. We have respected the senior devotees, friends, and our brothers. We have been kind to all beings, without envy. We have performed severe penance—not taking food for a long time while remaining in the water. We select all this for pleasing you, the great Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. We do all this to please you (*paritoṣanāya*). They can please him by being completely dedicated to satisfying the object of their love.

### Text 41

*manuḥ svayambhūr bhagavān bhavaś ca*  
*ye 'nye tapo-jñāna-viśuddha-sattvāḥ*  
*adṛṣṭa-pārā api yan-mahimnaḥ*  
*stuvanty atho tvātma-samaṁ gṛṇīmaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Manu, Brahmā, Śiva, and others who became purified by austerity and knowledge, though they are not able to see your glories completely, offer prayers to you. In the same way, we offer prayers to the best of our ability.**

**Комментарий:**

Our request is according to our intelligence, not the Lord’s. By your mercy, the request should be fulfilled in a suitable way.

### Text 43

*maitreya uvāca*  
*iti pracetobhir abhiṣṭuto hariḥ*  
*prītas tathety āha śaraṇya-vatsalaḥ*  
*anicchatāṁ yānam atṛpta-cakṣuṣāṁ*  
*yayau sva-dhāmānapavarga-vīryaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: The Supreme Lord, thus praised by the Pracetās, was pleased and said “So be it.” Though their eyes could not be satisfied and they did not desire his departure, the Lord, affectionate to the surrendered, who has vigorous strength, then departed.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord agreed and gave blessings. Though they did not desire it, the Lord departed since he was dependent on his unlimited devotees (*sva-dhāma*).

### Text 44

*atha niryāya salilāt*  
*pracetasa udanvataḥ*  
*vīkṣyākupyan drumaiś channāṁ*  
*gāṁ gāṁ roddhum ivocchritaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Rising from the waters of the ocean, the Pracetās, seeing that the earth was covered with tall trees which appeared to obstruct heaven, became angry.**

**Комментарий:**

They became angry because the trees were an obstacle for ruling according to the Lord’s order, and it was proper that servants follow that order.

### Text 47

*tatrāvaśiṣṭā ye vṛkṣā*  
*bhītā duhitaraṁ tadā*  
*ujjahrus te pracetobhya*  
*upadiṣṭāḥ svayambhuvā*

**Перевод:**

**The frightened trees which still remained, on advice of Brahmā, offered their daughter to the Pracetās.**

**Комментарий:**

The trees means the deities presiding over the trees.

### Text 49

*cākṣuṣe tv antare prāpte*  
*prāk-sarge kāla-vidrute*  
*yaḥ sasarja prajā iṣṭāḥ*  
*sa dakṣo daiva-coditaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who, inspired by the Lord, created the desired progeny at the time of Cākṣuṣa Manvantara when the previous creation had been destroyed by time, is known as Dakṣa.**

**Комментарий:**

At that time, the devatās had exhausted their karmas for enjoyment and the world was flooded with water after the fifth Manvantara.

## Chapter Thirty-one

### Text 1

*maitreya uvāca*  
*tata utpanna-vijñānā*  
*āśv adhokṣaja-bhāṣitam*  
*smaranta ātmaje bhāryāṁ*  
*visṛjya prāvrajan gṛhāt*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Developing knowledge and remembering the words of the Lord, the Pracetās left their wife with their son and left the house.**

**Комментарий:**

This occurred after Dakṣa became king.

### Text 2

*dīkṣitā brahma-satreṇa*  
*sarva-bhūtātma-medhasā*  
*pratīcyāṁ diśi velāyāṁ*  
*siddho 'bhūd yatra jājaliḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With a vow to contemplate the meaning of the Vedas and thereby understand that all beings are equal to oneself, they attained perfection in the east, on the shore of the ocean where Jājali resided.**

**Комментарий:**

*Brahma-satre*ṇa means gathering together to discuss truth. *Velāyām* means “on the shore of the ocean.” Since Priyavrata created the oceans and as born previously, his rulership was also previous. His creation of the oceans is explained in the Fifth Canto.

### Text 5

*pracetasa ūcuḥ*  
*svāgataṁ te surarṣe 'dya*  
*diṣṭyā no darśanaṁ gataḥ*  
*tava caṅkramaṇaṁ brahmann*  
*abhayāya yathā raveḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Pracetās said: Welcome to you! O sage of the** ***devatās*****! By good fortune we can see you. O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! You have come. Your movement, like that of the sun, creates fearlessness.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as the fear of thieves disappears on seeing the sun, we have no fear of saṁsāra on seeing you.

### Text 6

*yad ādiṣṭaṁ bhagavatā*  
*śivenādhokṣajena ca*  
*tad gṛheṣu prasaktānāṁ*  
*prāyaśaḥ kṣapitaṁ prabho*

**Перевод:**

**O master! What Śiva and Viṣṇu taught us has almost been destroyed since we were attached to household life.**

**Комментарий:**

On Śiva’s instructions, they immediately began searching for Brahman.

### Text 7

*tan naḥ pradyotayādhyātma-*  
*jñānaṁ tattvārtha-darśanam*  
*yenāñjasā tariṣyāmo*  
*dustaraṁ bhava-sāgaram*

**Перевод:**

**Please enlighten us about knowledge of the** ***jīva,*** **which shows the highest truth, by which we can easily cross the difficult ocean of material existence.**

**Комментарий:**

They inquire in general.

### Text 8

*maitreya uvāca*  
*iti pracetasāṁ pṛṣṭo*  
*bhagavān nārado muniḥ*  
*bhagavaty uttama-śloka*  
*āviṣṭātmābravīn nṛpān*

**Перевод:**

**Maitreya said: Requested by the Pracetās, the great sage Nārada, his mind absorbed in the Lord, then spoke to those kings.**

**Комментарий:**

Nārada was absorbed in the Lord, either by nature or remembering the mercy of the Lord to the Pracetās. Otherwise how could he give teachings?

### Text 9

*nārada uvāca*  
*taj janma tāni karmāṇi*  
*tad āyus tan mano vacaḥ*  
*nṛṇāṁ yena hi viśvātmā*  
*sevyate harir īśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: The birth, activities, lifespan, mind and words of humans takes on real value by service to the Supreme Lord, the soul of the universe.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to make them give up attachment to Brahman he praises attachment to the Lord.

### Text 10

*kiṁ janmabhis tribhir veha*  
*śaukra-sāvitra-yājñikaiḥ*  
*karmabhir vā trayī-proktaiḥ*  
*puṁso 'pi vibudhāyuṣā*

**Перевод:**

**Without that service, what is the use of taking three births by semen,** ***upanayanam*** **and initiation? What is the use of ritual acts mentioned in the Vedas? What is the use of the lifespan of a** ***devatā*** **for a human?**

**Комментарий:**

This is expressed in three verses by stating the opposite.

### Text 11

*śrutena tapasā vā kiṁ*  
*vacobhiś citta-vṛttibhiḥ*  
*buddhyā vā kiṁ nipuṇayā*  
*balenendriya-rādhasā*

**Перевод:**

**Without service to the Lord, what is the use of hearing scriptures like Vedānta, austerity, expert discussions of scripture, the ability to understand scriptures, intelligence, expertise, physical strength or sharpness of the senses?**

**Комментарий:**

Rejecting meditation of the pure jīva and Brahman, he established meditation on the Lord.

### Text 13

*śreyasām api sarveṣām*  
*ātmā hy avadhir arthataḥ*  
*sarveṣām api bhūtānāṁ*  
*harir ātmātmadaḥ priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Practically speaking, the** ***jīvātmā*** **is the goal of all auspicious acts. But the Supreme Lord is the ā*****tmā*** **of all** ***jīvātmās*****. When he is pleased, he gives himself.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is root of everything since the pure jīva is a portion of his taṭastha-śakti and Brahman is his impersonal manifestation only. The special nature of the devotees is described. He gives himself to them. He is the shelter of the highest prema.

### Text 14

*yathā taror mūla-niṣecanena*  
*tṛpyanti tat-skandha-bhujopaśākhāḥ*  
*prāṇopahārāc ca yathendriyāṇāṁ*  
*tathaiva sarvārhaṇam acyutejyā*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the branches and twigs are satisfied by watering the root, and just as the senses are satisfied by nourishing the life air, by worshipping the Supreme Lord all are worship is accomplished.**

**Комментарий:**

Having stated that karma and jñāna kāṇḍas ultimately lead to the Lord, he speaks of upāsana-kāṇḍa as also leading to the Lord.

### Text 15

*yathaiva sūryāt prabhavanti vāraḥ*  
*punaś ca tasmin praviśanti kāle*  
*bhūtāni bhūmau sthira-jaṅgamāni*  
*tathā harāv eva guṇa-pravāhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as water arises from the sun and enters the sun, and just as animals and plants arise from the earth and enter the earth, the world made of** ***guṇas*** **arises from the Lord and enters him.**

**Комментарий:**

Verse 16 is accepted by Citsukha and Sambandhokti but not by Śrīdhara Svāmī.

### Text 17

*yathā nabhasy abhra-tamaḥ-prakāśā*  
*bhavanti bhūpā na bhavanty anukramāt*  
*evaṁ pare brahmaṇi śaktayas tv amū*  
*rajas tamaḥ sattvam iti pravāhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Just as clouds, darkness, and light appear and disappear in the sky in succession, the energies of** ***rajas, tamas*** **and** ***sattva*** **appear and disappear in the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

*Pare brahmaṇi* means “in Bhagavān.”

### Text 18

*tenaikam ātmānam aśeṣa-dehināṁ*  
*kālaṁ pradhānaṁ puruṣaṁ pareśam*  
*sva-tejasā dhvasta-guṇa-pravāham*  
*ātmaika-bhāvena bhajadhvam addhā*

**Перевод:**

**Because the Lord is the cause of all things, you should worship directly, with one** ***bhāva*** **in the mind, the Supreme Lord, who is the soul of unlimited** ***jīvas,*** **who is the indirect cause called time, the material cause called** ***prakṛti,*** **who is the** ***jīva*****, and who is completely free of the influence of the material** ***guṇas*** **by his power.**

**Комментарий:**

You should worship the Lord as the root of everything (*ātmaika-bhāvena*) or with steadiness. Or you should worship the Lord with one bhava such as dāsya, and not mix it with other bhāvas.

### Text 19

*dayayā sarva-bhūteṣu*  
*santuṣṭyā yena kena vā*  
*sarvendriyopaśāntyā ca*  
*tuṣyaty āśu janārdanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is quickly pleased by a person showing mercy to all beings, by being satisfied by whatever comes of its own accord, and by controlling all the senses.**

**Комментарий:**

Favorable supporting qualities are shown.

### Text 20

*apahata-sakalaiṣaṇāmalātmany*  
*aviratam edhita-bhāvanopahūtaḥ*  
*nija-jana-vaśa-gatvam ātmano 'yan*  
*na sarati chidravad akṣaraḥ satāṁ hi*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly beckoned by the increasing devotion present in his devotees’ pure minds which have destroyed all material desires, the Lord, knowing that he is controlled by his devotees, does not leave them, just as space does not leave one’s side.**

**Комментарий:**

Always joining (*yan*) himself with being controlled by his devotees, he does not leave them.

### Text 22

*śriyam anucaratīṁ tad-arthinaś ca*  
*dvipada-patīn vibudhāṁś ca yat sva-pūrṇaḥ*  
*na bhajati nija-bhṛtya-varga-tantraḥ*  
*katham amum udvisṛjet pumān kṛta-jñaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, being complete in his** ***svarūpa*****, does not care for the sum total of all wealth, which is worshipped by those desiring all wealth, and does not care for those desiring even a little wealth, or for human kings or** ***devatās.*** **He is dependent on his devotees. How could the devotee reject this Lord even a little?**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord does not care for material wealth which follows him (*śriyam*) or for persons who pursue wealth for themselves. Those who pursue the Lord’s Lakṣmī have no desire for themselves. He does not care for devatās, rulers of men, since being complete in himself (*sva-pūrṇaḥ*), he is only attracted to his devotees. He is controlled by devotees, being controlled by his own mercy. How could one reject him even a little?

### Text 28

*imāṁ tu kauṣāraviṇopavarṇitāṁ*  
*kṣattā niśamyājita-vāda-sat-kathām*  
*pravṛddha-bhāvo 'śru-kalākulo muner*  
*dadhāra mūrdhnā caraṇaṁ hṛdā hareḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this from Maitreya, Vidura, his emotions increasing from hearing about the Lord, with tears in his eyes, touched the feet of Maitreya with his head, and mentally touched the feet of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Hearing the topic of the Lord spoke by his devotee (i*mam*), his emotions increased.

### Text 30

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*ity ānamya tam āmantrya*  
*viduro gajasāhvayam*  
*svānāṁ didṛkṣuḥ prayayau*  
*jñātīnāṁ nirvṛtāśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva said: Offering respects to Maitreya, and taking his permission, Vidura, with joyful heart, desiring to see his relatives, departed for Hastināpura.**

**Комментарий:**

This is linked to the descriptions in the First Canto. Vidura was joyful because, through Maitreya, he began searching for acceptance by the Lord.

### Text 31

*etad yaḥ śṛṇuyād rājan*  
*rājñāṁ hary-arpitātmanām*  
*āyur dhanaṁ yaśaḥ svasti*  
*gatim aiśvaryam āpnuyāt*

**Перевод:**

**O King! He who hears this story of the kings who gave themselves to the Lord will attain long life, wealth, fame, good fortune, and the Lord’s abode.**

**Комментарий:**

In great bliss he blesses the hearers who still have material desires.
